        pcall(function()
            if g then g:Destroy() end

    end)
end)

local function deepCopyPos(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return { X=0.5, XOffset=0, Y=0.5, YOffset=0 } end
    return {
        X = tonumber(t.X) or 0.5,
        XOffset = tonumber(t.XOffset) or 0,
        Y = tonumber(t.Y) or 0.5,
        YOffset = tonumber(t.YOffset) or 0,
    }
end

local function posToUDim2(p)
    p = deepCopyPos(p)
    return UDim2.new(p.X, p.XOffset, p.Y, p.YOffset)
end

local function udim2ToPos(u)
    return {
        X = u.X.Scale,
        XOffset = u.X.Offset,
        Y = u.Y.Scale,
        YOffset = u.Y.Offset,
    }
end

local function rawSaveConfig()
    pcall(function()
        if type(writefile) == "function" then
            -- On encode la table brute si proxy, sinon Config direct
            local toSave = rawget(Config, "__RAW") or Config
            writefile(CONFIG_PATH, HttpService:JSONEncode(toSave))
            print("[ZENO] SAVE OK -> "..CONFIG_PATH)
        end
    end)
end

local _saveDebounce, _saveQueued = false, false
local function saveConfig()
    if _saveDebounce then _saveQueued=true return end
    _saveDebounce=true
    task.spawn(function()
        rawSaveConfig()
        task.wait(0.12)
        _saveDebounce=false
        if _saveQueued then _saveQueued=false saveConfig() end
    end)
end
_G.ZenoSaveConfig = saveConfig
_G.SaveConfig = saveConfig

-- Suppression ancien config (une seule fois, en différé pour pas bloquer)
task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(OLD_PATH) then
            delfile(OLD_PATH)
            print("[ZENO] Ancien config supprime: "..OLD_PATH)
        end
        if isfolder and isfolder(OLD_FOLDER) then
            if listfiles then
                for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(OLD_FOLDER)) do
                    pcall(function() delfile(f) end)
                end
            end
            if delfolder then delfolder(OLD_FOLDER) end
            print("[ZENO] Ancien dossier supprime: "..OLD_FOLDER)
        end
    end)
end)


local function isPosTable(t)
    return type(t) == "table" and (t.X ~= nil or t.XOffset ~= nil or t.Y ~= nil or t.YOffset ~= nil)
        and t.ragdoll == nil and t.balloon == nil and t.jail == nil
end

local function isPosTable(t)
    return type(t) == "table" and (t.X ~= nil or t.XOffset ~= nil or t.Y ~= nil or t.YOffset ~= nil)
        and t.ragdoll == nil and t.balloon == nil and t.jail == nil
end

local function loadConfig()
    pcall(function()
        if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
        -- essaie nouveau fichier d'abord, puis ancien pour migration
        local pathToLoad = nil
        if isfile(CONFIG_PATH) then pathToLoad = CONFIG_PATH
        elseif isfile(OLD_PATH) then pathToLoad = OLD_PATH end
        if not pathToLoad then 
            print("[ZENO] Pas de config, creation nouveau "..CONFIG_PATH)
            rawSaveConfig()
            return 
        end
        local d = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(pathToLoad))
        if type(d) ~= "table" then return end
        for k, v in pairs(d) do
            if Config[k] ~= nil then
                if type(Config[k]) == "table" and type(v) == "table" then
                    if isPosTable(Config[k]) or isPosTable(v) then
                        Config[k] = deepCopyPos(v)
                    else
                        for kk, vv in pairs(v) do
                            Config[k][kk] = vv
                        end
                    end
                else
                    Config[k] = v
                end
            else
                Config[k] = v
            end
        end
        print("[ZENO] Config charge: "..pathToLoad)
        -- si on a chargé l'ancien, on le re-save dans le nouveau fichier seul
        if pathToLoad == OLD_PATH then
            rawSaveConfig()
        end
    end)
end
loadConfig()

-- ===== AUTO-SAVE PROXY : CHAQUE Config.xxx = ... SAVE DIRECT EN FICHIER =====
do
    local raw = Config
    local proxy = {}
    rawset(proxy, "__RAW", raw)
    Config = setmetatable(proxy, {
        __index = function(_,k) 
            if k=="__RAW" then return raw end
            return raw[k] 
        end,
        __newindex = function(_,k,v) 
            raw[k]=v 
            saveConfig() 
        end,
    })
end

local function hookPanelAutoSave(panel, configKey)
    if not panel or not panel.GetPropertyChangedSignal then return end
    panel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
        local raw = rawget(Config, "__RAW")
        if raw then
            raw[configKey] = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
            saveConfig()
        end
    end)
end
_G.ZenoHookPanel = hookPanelAutoSave


-- ensure nested defaults after load
do
    local defCmds = { balloon=true, inverse=true, jail=true, jumpscare=true, morph=true, nightvision=true, ragdoll=true, rocket=true, tiny=true }
    if type(Config.clickToAPCommands) ~= "table" or Config.clickToAPCommands.X ~= nil then
        Config.clickToAPCommands = defCmds
    end
    if type(Config.adminPanelButtons) ~= "table" or Config.adminPanelButtons.X ~= nil then
        Config.adminPanelButtons = { ragdoll=true, jail=true, rocket=true, balloon=true }
    end
end

    if Config.clickSound == false then return end
    pcall(function()
        local s = Instance.new("Sound")
        s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://6895079853"
        s.Volume = 1.15
        s.PlaybackSpeed = 1.0
        s.Parent = SoundService
        s:Play()
        s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
        task.delay(2, function() pcall(function() s:Destroy() end) end)
    end)
end
_G.ZenoClickSound = playClickSound

-- ================================================================
-- ESP PLAYER + ESP BRAINROT (Highlight + Billboard)
-- ================================================================
do
    local espPlayerOn = false
    local espPlayerConns = {}
    local espPlayerHolders = {}

    local function espParent()
        if playerGui then return playerGui end
        if gethui then
            local h = gethui()
            if h then return h end
        end
        return game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end

    local function clearPlayerEsp(plr)
        local h = espPlayerHolders[plr]
        if h then pcall(function() h:Destroy() end) end
        espPlayerHolders[plr] = nil
    end

    local function makePlayerEsp(plr)
        if not espPlayerOn or plr == player then return end
        local char = plr.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        clearPlayerEsp(plr)
        local parent = espParent()
        if not parent then return end

        local holder = Instance.new("Folder")
        holder.Name = "ZenoEspPlayer_" .. plr.Name
        holder.Parent = parent

        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Adornee = char
        hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 90)
        hl.FillTransparency = 0.55
        hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 200)
        hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Parent = holder

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Adornee = hrp
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 36)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
        bb.Parent = holder

        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = plr.DisplayName ~= plr.Name and (plr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. plr.Name .. ")") or plr.Name
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLbl.TextSize = 13
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
        nameLbl.Parent = bb

        espPlayerHolders[plr] = holder

        local ca = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            if not espPlayerOn then return end
            clearPlayerEsp(plr)
            newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if espPlayerOn then makePlayerEsp(plr) end
        end)
        espPlayerConns[#espPlayerConns + 1] = ca
    end

    local function setEspPlayer(on)
        on = on and true or false
        _G.ZenoEspPlayer = on
        if on == espPlayerOn then return end
        if not on then
            espPlayerOn = false
            for _, c in ipairs(espPlayerConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            espPlayerConns = {}
            for plr in pairs(espPlayerHolders) do clearPlayerEsp(plr) end
            return
        end
        espPlayerOn = true
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= player then makePlayerEsp(plr) end
        end
        espPlayerConns[#espPlayerConns + 1] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            if not espPlayerOn then return end
            task.wait(0.8)
            makePlayerEsp(plr)
        end)
        espPlayerConns[#espPlayerConns + 1] = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
            clearPlayerEsp(plr)
        end)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetEspPlayer = setEspPlayer

    -- Brainrot ESP (methode KOBY : Humanoid models sur podiums + anchors)
    local brOn = false
    local brConn = nil
    local brEntries = {}
    local brFolder = nil
    local brAnchors = nil

    local function ensureBrFolder()
        if brFolder and brFolder.Parent then return brFolder end
        local parent = espParent()
        brFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
        brFolder.Name = "ZenoBrEsp"
        brFolder.Parent = parent
        return brFolder
    end

    local function ensureAnchors()
        if brAnchors and brAnchors.Parent then return brAnchors end
        brAnchors = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ZenoBrEspAnchors")
        if not brAnchors then
            brAnchors = Instance.new("Folder")
            brAnchors.Name = "ZenoBrEspAnchors"
            brAnchors.Parent = Workspace
        end
        return brAnchors
    end

    local function clearBr(uid)
        local e = brEntries[uid]
        if e then
            pcall(function() if e.holder then e.holder:Destroy() end end)
            pcall(function() if e.anchor then e.anchor:Destroy() end end)
        end
        brEntries[uid] = nil
    end

    local function clearAllBr()
        for uid in pairs(brEntries) do clearBr(uid) end
        if brFolder then pcall(function() brFolder:Destroy() end) brFolder = nil end
    end

    -- Trouve le vrai model du pet (Humanoid, pas un joueur)
    local function findPetModelOnPodium(podium)
        if not podium then return nil end
        -- Priorite: model avec Humanoid (pas un joueur)
        for _, desc in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("Model") and desc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(desc) then
                    return desc
                end
            end
        end
        -- Fallback: model avec MeshPart (hors Claim/Base/Decorations)
        for _, desc in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("Model") then
                local n = desc.Name
                if n ~= "Claim" and n ~= "Base" and n ~= "Decorations" and n ~= "PlotSign" then
                    local hasMesh = false
                    for _, c in ipairs(desc:GetDescendants()) do
                        if c:IsA("MeshPart") or c:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                            hasMesh = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if hasMesh and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(desc) then
                        return desc
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getPart(model)
        if not model then return nil end
        return model.PrimaryPart
            or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end

    local function applyBr(uid, model, part, nameText, genText, isTop)
        if not part then return end
        local folder = ensureBrFolder()
        local e = brEntries[uid]

        if e and e.model and e.model ~= model and model then
            clearBr(uid)
            e = nil
        end

        if not e then
            local holder = Instance.new("Folder")
            holder.Name = "Br_" .. tostring(uid):gsub("[^%w_]", "_")
            holder.Parent = folder

            local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
            hl.Name = "HL"
            hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            hl.FillTransparency = 0.4
            hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
            hl.Parent = holder

            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "BB"
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 48)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.2, 0)
            bb.Parent = holder

            local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            nameLbl.Name = "Name"
            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            nameLbl.TextSize = 13
            nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 245, 255)
            nameLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
            nameLbl.Parent = bb

            local genLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            genLbl.Name = "Gen"
            genLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            genLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
            genLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            genLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            genLbl.TextSize = 12
            genLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
            genLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
            genLbl.Parent = bb

            e = { holder = holder, hl = hl, bb = bb, nameLbl = nameLbl, genLbl = genLbl, model = model }
            brEntries[uid] = e
        end

        e.model = model or e.model
        if model then
            e.hl.Adornee = model
        else
            e.hl.Adornee = part
        end
        e.bb.Adornee = part
        e.nameLbl.Text = nameText or "?"
        e.genLbl.Text = genText or ""

        if isTop then
            e.hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
            e.hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 245, 120)
            e.hl.FillTransparency = 0.28
            e.nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 80)
        else
            e.hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 210, 255)
            e.hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(160, 235, 255)
            e.hl.FillTransparency = 0.42
            e.nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 245, 255)
        end
    end

    local function fmtGen(mps)
        mps = tonumber(mps) or 0
        if mps >= 1e12 then return string.format("$%.1fT/s", mps / 1e12) end
        if mps >= 1e9 then return string.format("$%.1fB/s", mps / 1e9) end
        if mps >= 1e6 then return string.format("$%.1fM/s", mps / 1e6) end
        if mps >= 1e3 then return string.format("$%.1fK/s", mps / 1e3) end
        return "$" .. tostring(math.floor(mps + 0.5)) .. "/s"
    end

    local function refreshBrEsp()
        if not brOn then return end
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end

        local seen = {}
        local topUid, topMps = nil, -1
        local found = {}

        -- 1) Scan workspace podiums (le plus fiable pour l'affichage)
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not podiums then continue end
            for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                local model = findPetModelOnPodium(podium)
                if model then
                    local part = getPart(model)
                    if part then
                        local uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(podium.Name)
                        local mps = 0
                        -- essayer de matcher le cache pets si dispo
                        pcall(function()
                            local cache = rawget(_G, "ZenoAllPetsCache")
                            if type(cache) == "table" then
                                for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                                    if pet.uid == uid or (pet.plot == plot.Name and tostring(pet.slot) == tostring(podium.Name)) then
                                        mps = tonumber(pet.mps) or 0
                                        found[uid] = {
                                            uid = uid,
                                            model = model,
                                            part = part,
                                            name = pet.name or model.Name,
                                            mps = mps,
                                        }
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        if not found[uid] then
                            found[uid] = {
                                uid = uid,
                                model = model,
                                part = part,
                                name = model.Name,
                                mps = 0,
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- 2) Completer avec le cache scan (pets stream pas encore visibles -> anchor)
        pcall(function()
            local cache = rawget(_G, "ZenoAllPetsCache")
            if type(cache) == "table" then
                for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                    if pet.mine then continue end
                    local uid = pet.uid or (tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot))
                    if found[uid] then
                        found[uid].name = pet.name or found[uid].name
                        found[uid].mps = tonumber(pet.mps) or found[uid].mps or 0
                    elseif typeof(pet.position) == "Vector3" then
                        local anchors = ensureAnchors()
                        local anchor = anchors:FindFirstChild(uid)
                        if not anchor then
                            anchor = Instance.new("Part")
                            anchor.Name = uid
                            anchor.Anchored = true
                            anchor.CanCollide = false
                            anchor.CanQuery = false
                            anchor.CanTouch = false
                            anchor.CastShadow = false
                            anchor.Transparency = 1
                            anchor.Size = Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3)
                            anchor.Parent = anchors
                        end
                        anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(pet.position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
                        found[uid] = {
                            uid = uid,
                            model = nil,
                            part = anchor,
                            name = pet.name or "?",
                            mps = tonumber(pet.mps) or 0,
                            anchor = anchor,
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        for uid, data in pairs(found) do
            local m = tonumber(data.mps) or 0
            if m > topMps then topMps = m; topUid = uid end
        end

        for uid, data in pairs(found) do
            seen[uid] = true
            local isTop = (uid == topUid and topMps > 0)
            applyBr(uid, data.model, data.part, data.name, fmtGen(data.mps), isTop)
            if data.anchor then
                local e = brEntries[uid]
                if e then e.anchor = data.anchor end
            end
        end

        for uid in pairs(brEntries) do
            if not seen[uid] then clearBr(uid) end
        end
    end

    local function setEspBrainrot(on)
        on = on and true or false
        _G.ZenoEspBrainrot = on
        Config.espBrainrot = on
        if on == brOn then
            if on then task.defer(refreshBrEsp) end
            return
        end
        brOn = on
        if not on then
            if brConn then pcall(function() brConn:Disconnect() end) brConn = nil end
            clearAllBr()
            return
        end
        local last = 0
        brConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - last < 0.85 then return end
            last = now
            refreshBrEsp()
        end)
        task.defer(refreshBrEsp)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetEspBrainrot = setEspBrainrot

    -- restore from config after short delay
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(1.5)
        if Config.espPlayer == true then setEspPlayer(true) end
        if Config.espBrainrot == true then setEspBrainrot(true) end
    end)
end


-- ================================================================
-- AUTO BUY + AUTO TURRET + ESP STEALERS + UNLOCK DOOR/BASE
-- ================================================================
do
    -- ---------- ESP STEALERS ----------
    local stealersOn = false
    local stealerEntries = {}
    local stealerConn = nil
    local stealerFolder = nil

    local function stealerParent()
        if playerGui then return playerGui end
        if gethui then local h = gethui(); if h then return h end end
        return game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end

    local function clearStealer(uid)
        local e = stealerEntries[uid]
        if e then
            pcall(function() if e.hl then e.hl:Destroy() end end)
            pcall(function() if e.bb then e.bb:Destroy() end end)
        end
        stealerEntries[uid] = nil
    end

    local function clearAllStealers()
        for uid in pairs(stealerEntries) do clearStealer(uid) end
        if stealerFolder then pcall(function() stealerFolder:Destroy() end) stealerFolder = nil end
    end

    local function applyStealer(plr)
        if not stealersOn or not plr or plr == player then return end
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char then clearStealer(plr.UserId); return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local stealing = plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
            or (char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
        if not stealing or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not hrp then
            clearStealer(plr.UserId)
            return
        end

        if not stealerFolder or not stealerFolder.Parent then
            stealerFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
            stealerFolder.Name = "ZenoEspStealers"
            stealerFolder.Parent = stealerParent()
        end

        local e = stealerEntries[plr.UserId]
        if not e then
            local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
            hl.Adornee = char
            hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
            hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 80)
            hl.FillTransparency = 0.45
            hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
            hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            hl.Parent = stealerFolder

            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "StealerBB"
            bb.Adornee = hrp
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 36)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.MaxDistance = 500
            bb.Parent = stealerFolder

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            lbl.TextSize = 13
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 100)
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
            lbl.Text = "STEALING • " .. (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name)
            lbl.Parent = bb

            e = { hl = hl, bb = bb, lbl = lbl }
            stealerEntries[plr.UserId] = e
        else
            e.hl.Adornee = char
            e.bb.Adornee = hrp
            e.lbl.Text = "STEALING • " .. (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name)
        end
    end

    local function refreshStealers()
        if not stealersOn then return end
        local seen = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= player then
                seen[plr.UserId] = true
                applyStealer(plr)
            end
        end
        for uid in pairs(stealerEntries) do
            if not seen[uid] then clearStealer(uid) end
        end
    end

    local function setEspStealers(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.espStealers = on
        _G.ZenoEspStealers = on
        stealersOn = on
        if not on then
            if stealerConn then pcall(function() stealerConn:Disconnect() end) stealerConn = nil end
            clearAllStealers()
            return
        end
        if not stealerConn then
            local last = 0
            stealerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 0.9 then return end
                last = now
                refreshStealers()
            end)
        end
        task.defer(refreshStealers)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetEspStealers = setEspStealers

    -- ---------- AUTO BUY (lock + follow + rebuy) ----------
    local buyOn = false
    local buyConn = nil
    local buyFollowConn = nil
    local buyRing = nil
    local buyCache = {}
    local buyCacheT = 0
    local buyLastFireT = 0
    local buyLastRingT = 0
    local buyScanning = false
    local lockedPrompt = nil
    local lockedPart = nil
    local bodyPos = nil

    local function buyRange()
        return math.clamp(tonumber(Config.autoBuyRange) or 17, 5, 60)
    end

    local function destroyBuyRing()
        if buyRing then pcall(function() buyRing:Destroy() end) buyRing = nil end
        local e = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ZenoAutoBuyRing")
        if e then pcall(function() e:Destroy() end) end
    end

    local function ensureBuyRing()
        if buyRing and buyRing.Parent then return buyRing end
        destroyBuyRing()
        local r = Instance.new("Part")
        r.Name = "ZenoAutoBuyRing"
        r.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        r.Anchored = true
        r.CanCollide = false
        r.CanTouch = false
        r.CanQuery = false
        r.CastShadow = false
        r.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        r.Transparency = 0.6
        r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
        local range = buyRange()
        r.Size = Vector3.new(0.3, range * 2, range * 2)
        r.Parent = Workspace
        buyRing = r
        return r
    end

    local function destroyBodyPos()
        if bodyPos then pcall(function() bodyPos:Destroy() end) bodyPos = nil end
        _G.ZenoAutoBuyFollowing = false
    end

    local function ensureBodyPos(hrp)
        if bodyPos and bodyPos.Parent == hrp then return bodyPos end
        destroyBodyPos()
        local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bp.Name = "ZenoAutoBuyBP"
        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
        bp.P = 12000
        bp.D = 1500
        bp.Position = hrp.Position
        bp.Parent = hrp
        bodyPos = bp
        _G.ZenoAutoBuyFollowing = true
        return bp
    end

    local function isBuyPrompt(pr)
        if not pr or not pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return false end
        local a = (pr.ActionText or "")
        local al = a:lower()
        local ol = (pr.ObjectText or ""):lower()
        if al:find("sell", 1, true) or ol:find("sell", 1, true) then return false end
        if al:find("steal", 1, true) or ol:find("steal", 1, true) then return false end
        if a == "Purchase" or al:find("purchase", 1, true) or al:find("comprar", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        if al:find("buy", 1, true) then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function isPromptOnOwnPlot(pr)
        local cur = pr
        for _ = 1, 14 do
            if not cur then break end
            if cur.Parent and cur.Parent.Name == "Plots" then
                local ok, own = pcall(function()
                    local f = rawget(_G, "ZenoIsOwnPlot")
                    if type(f) == "function" then return f(cur.Name) end
                    return false
                end)
                return ok and own == true
            end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function resolvePromptPart(pr)
        local p = pr and pr.Parent
        if not p then return nil end
        if p:IsA("Attachment") then p = p.Parent end
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
        if p and p:IsA("Model") then
            return p.PrimaryPart or p:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        end
        local cur = p
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not cur then break end
            if cur:IsA("BasePart") then return cur end
            if cur:IsA("Model") then
                local bp = cur.PrimaryPart or cur:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if bp then return bp end
            end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function refreshBuyList()
        if buyScanning then return buyCache end
        buyScanning = true
        local list = {}
        local seen = {}
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and not seen[obj] and isBuyPrompt(obj) then
                    if not isPromptOnOwnPlot(obj) then
                        local part = resolvePromptPart(obj)
                        if part then
                            seen[obj] = true
                            list[#list + 1] = { prompt = obj, part = part }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        buyCache = list
        buyCacheT = os.clock()
        buyScanning = false
        return list
    end

    local function fireBuyPrompt(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
        pcall(function()
            prompt.Enabled = true
            local oldMax = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
            local oldHold = prompt.HoldDuration
            local oldLos = prompt.RequiresLineOfSight
            pcall(function()
                prompt.MaxActivationDistance = math.max(oldMax or 0, 50)
                prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
            end)
            if type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                fireproximityprompt(prompt)
                task.defer(function() pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(prompt) end) end)
            else
                pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldBegin() end)
                task.delay(0.05, function() pcall(function() prompt:InputHoldEnd() end) end)
            end
            task.delay(0.2, function()
                pcall(function()
                    if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                        prompt.MaxActivationDistance = oldMax
                        prompt.HoldDuration = oldHold
                        prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = oldLos
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function clearLock()
        lockedPrompt = nil
        lockedPart = nil
        destroyBodyPos()
    end

    local function lockAlive()
        return lockedPrompt and lockedPrompt.Parent and lockedPart and lockedPart.Parent
    end

    local function tickAutoBuy()
        if not buyOn then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then
            clearLock()
            return
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        local range = buyRange()

        if now - buyLastRingT >= 0.2 then
            buyLastRingT = now
            if not buyRing or not buyRing.Parent then ensureBuyRing() end
            if buyRing then
                buyRing.Size = Vector3.new(0.3, range * 2, range * 2)
                buyRing.CFrame = (hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))) + Vector3.new(0, -2.2, 0)
            end
        end

        if now - buyCacheT > 0.7 then
            task.defer(refreshBuyList)
        end

        -- Si lock mort (achete par toi ou autre) -> chercher un nouveau
        if not lockAlive() then
            clearLock()
            local hp = hrp.Position
            local best, bestD, bestPart = nil, range + 8, nil
            for _, entry in ipairs(buyCache) do
                local pr, part = entry.prompt, entry.part
                if pr and pr.Parent and part and part.Parent then
                    local d = (hp - part.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestD then
                        bestD, best, bestPart = d, pr, part
                    end
                end
            end
            if best then
                lockedPrompt = best
                lockedPart = bestPart
            end
        end

        -- Follow le brainrot locke
        if lockAlive() then
            local above = lockedPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
            local bp = ensureBodyPos(hrp)
            bp.Position = above
            -- spam purchase tant que le prompt existe (rebuy si concurrent)
            if now - buyLastFireT >= 0.12 then
                buyLastFireT = now
                fireBuyPrompt(lockedPrompt)
            end
        else
            destroyBodyPos()
        end
    end

    local function setAutoBuy(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.autoBuy = on
        buyOn = on
        saveConfig()
        if not on then
            if buyConn then pcall(function() buyConn:Disconnect() end) buyConn = nil end
            clearLock()
            destroyBuyRing()
            return
        end
        ensureBuyRing()
        task.defer(refreshBuyList)
        if not buyConn then
            local last = 0
            buyConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 0.08 then return end
                last = now
                tickAutoBuy()
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetAutoBuy = setAutoBuy
    _G.ZenoSetAutoBuyRange = function(v)
        Config.autoBuyRange = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 17, 5, 60)
        saveConfig()
        if buyOn and buyRing and buyRing.Parent then
            local range = buyRange()
            buyRing.Size = Vector3.new(0.3, range * 2, range * 2)
        end
    end

    -- ---------- AUTO TURRET / SENTRY ----------
    local turretOn = false
    local turretConns = {}
    local activeSentries = {}

    local function stopTurretAll()
        for _, c in pairs(turretConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        turretConns = {}
        for s, c in pairs(activeSentries) do
            pcall(function() if c then c:Disconnect() end end)
        end
        activeSentries = {}
    end

    local function handleSentry(child)
        if not turretOn or not child or not child.Parent then return end
        if not child.Name or not tostring(child.Name):find("Sentry") then return end
        if activeSentries[child] then return end

        local ownerId = tostring(child.Name):match("Sentry_(%d+)")
        if ownerId and tonumber(ownerId) == player.UserId then return end

        local sentryPart = nil
        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
            sentryPart = child
        elseif child:IsA("Model") then
            sentryPart = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        end
        if not sentryPart then return end

        pcall(function()
            sentryPart.CanCollide = false
        end)

        local lastAtk = 0
        local hb
        hb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not turretOn or not child.Parent then
                pcall(function() hb:Disconnect() end)
                activeSentries[child] = nil
                return
            end
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastAtk < 0.05 then return end
            lastAtk = now

            local char = player.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not hrp then return end

            -- garder la sentry proche devant soi
            pcall(function()
                local targetCF = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2)
                if child:IsA("Model") and child.PrimaryPart then
                    child:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(targetCF)
                elseif sentryPart and sentryPart.Parent then
                    sentryPart.CFrame = targetCF
                end
            end)

            local bat = player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Bat") or char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
            if bat then
                if bat.Parent == player.Backpack then
                    pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
                end
                if bat.Parent == char then
                    pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
                end
            end
        end)
        activeSentries[child] = hb
    end

    local function scanSentries()
        if not turretOn then return end
        local folders = {}
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if plots then folders[#folders + 1] = plots end
        local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
        if debris then folders[#folders + 1] = debris end
        folders[#folders + 1] = Workspace
        for _, folder in ipairs(folders) do
            pcall(function()
                for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d.Name and tostring(d.Name):find("Sentry") then
                        handleSentry(d)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function setAutoTurret(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.autoTurret = on
        turretOn = on
        if not on then
            stopTurretAll()
            return
        end
        task.spawn(scanSentries)
        if not turretConns.desc then
            turretConns.desc = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if not turretOn then return end
                if ch.Name and tostring(ch.Name):find("Sentry") then
                    task.defer(function() handleSentry(ch) end)
                end
            end)
        end
        if not turretConns.poll then
            local last = 0
            turretConns.poll = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not turretOn then return end
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 2.5 then return end
                last = now
                scanSentries()
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetAutoTurret = setAutoTurret
    _G.ZenoScanSentries = scanSentries

    -- ---------- UNLOCK DOOR / BASE ----------
    local unlockOn = false
    local unlockConn = nil

    local function tryUnlockNearby()
        if not unlockOn then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end

        local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local pos = nil
            if sign then
                if sign:IsA("BasePart") then pos = sign.Position
                elseif sign.PrimaryPart then pos = sign.PrimaryPart.Position
                else
                    local bp = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if bp then pos = bp.Position end
                end
            end
            if not pos then
                pcall(function()
                    local cf = plot:GetPivot()
                    pos = cf.Position
                end)
            end
            if pos then
                local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
                if d < closestDist then closestDist = d; closest = plot end
            end
        end
        if not closest or closestDist > 160 then return end

        for _, desc in ipairs(closest:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and desc.Enabled then
                local promptText = ((desc.ObjectText or "") .. " " .. (desc.ActionText or "")):lower()
                if promptText:find("door") or promptText:find("unlock") or promptText:find("open")
                    or promptText:find("porte") or promptText:find("ouvrir") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                            fireproximityprompt(desc)
                        else
                            desc:InputHoldBegin()
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            desc:InputHoldEnd()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function setUnlockDoor(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.unlockDoor = on
        unlockOn = on
        if not on then
            if unlockConn then pcall(function() unlockConn:Disconnect() end) unlockConn = nil end
            return
        end
        if not unlockConn then
            local last = 0
            unlockConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 0.45 then return end
                last = now
                tryUnlockNearby()
            end)
        end
        task.defer(tryUnlockNearby)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetUnlockDoor = setUnlockDoor
    _G.ZenoUnlockNearby = tryUnlockNearby

    -- restore
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if Config.espStealers == true then setEspStealers(true) end
        if Config.autoBuy == true then setAutoBuy(true) end
        if Config.autoTurret == true then setAutoTurret(true) end
        if Config.unlockDoor == true then setUnlockDoor(true) end
    end)
end


local Theme = {
    -- GLASS NOIR (transparent black, no color themes)
    Panel      = Color3.fromRGB(7,9,13),      PanelAlt   = Color3.fromRGB(13,16,22),
    PanelField = Color3.fromRGB(17,21,28),   PanelField2= Color3.fromRGB(28,34,44),
    Border     = Color3.fromRGB(58,68,86),   Outline    = Color3.fromRGB(112,128,152),
    Text       = Color3.fromRGB(240,246,252), TextDim   = Color3.fromRGB(168,182,200),
    TextFaint  = Color3.fromRGB(112,126,146), AccentA   = Color3.fromRGB(125,205,255),
    AccentB    = Color3.fromRGB(64,148,235), Green     = Color3.fromRGB(95,225,165),
    GreenDark  = Color3.fromRGB(35,165,110), CardBg    = Color3.fromRGB(8,10,14),
    BlackBar   = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),
}

-- Apply glass noir + falling stars to any panel Frame
_G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle = function(panel, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    if not panel or not panel.Parent then return end
    pcall(function()
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = opts.transparency or 0.38
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        panel.ClipsDescendants = true

        local cr = opts.corner or 18
        local cornerObj = panel:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
        if not cornerObj then
            cornerObj = Instance.new("UICorner")
            cornerObj.Parent = panel
        end
        cornerObj.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, cr)

        local st = panel:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if not st then
            st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            st.Parent = panel
        end
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(96, 128, 168)
        st.Transparency = 0.25
        st.Thickness = 1.2
        st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        -- Soft gradient (don't cover transparency)
        for _, g in ipairs(panel:GetChildren()) do
            if g:IsA("UIGradient") then
                g.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(34, 40, 54), Color3.fromRGB(2, 3, 6))
                g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.15),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.35),
                })
            end
        end

        -- Stars (visible) — NEVER expand AutomaticSize panels
        -- StarSnow height must stay 0 so UIListLayout ignores it.
        -- Stars are positioned in pixels from panel.AbsoluteSize.
        local oldStars = panel:FindFirstChild("StarSnow")
        if oldStars then oldStars:Destroy() end
        if opts.stars ~= false then
            local starLayer = Instance.new("Frame")
            starLayer.Name = "StarSnow"
            starLayer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) -- 0 height = no layout expansion
            starLayer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            starLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            starLayer.BorderSizePixel = 0
            starLayer.ZIndex = 1
            starLayer.Active = false
            starLayer.Selectable = false
            starLayer.ClipsDescendants = false
            starLayer.Parent = panel
            local glyphs = {"✦", "✧", "·", "⋆", "∗"}
            local stars = {}
            local n = opts.starCount or 18
            for i = 1, n do
                local s = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                s.Text = glyphs[(i % #glyphs) + 1]
                s.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                s.TextSize = 9 + (i % 5)
                s.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(216, 233, 255)
                s.TextTransparency = 0.25
                s.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                s.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
                s.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                s.ZIndex = 1
                s.Active = false
                s.Selectable = false
                s.Parent = starLayer
                stars[i] = {
                    lbl = s,
                    x = math.random(),
                    y = math.random(),
                    speed = 0.025 + math.random() * 0.05,
                    drift = (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.012,
                    phase = math.random() * 6.28,
                }
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local acc = 0
                while starLayer and starLayer.Parent and panel and panel.Parent do
                    local dt = task.wait(0.04)
                    acc = acc + dt
                    local pw = panel.AbsoluteSize.X
                    local ph = panel.AbsoluteSize.Y
                    if pw < 1 or ph < 1 then continue end
                    for _, stt in ipairs(stars) do
                        stt.y = stt.y + stt.speed * 0.35
                        stt.x = stt.x + stt.drift * 0.18
                        if stt.y > 1.1 then stt.y = -0.06; stt.x = math.random() end
                        if stt.x < -0.06 then stt.x = 1.06 elseif stt.x > 1.06 then stt.x = -0.06 end
                        stt.lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, stt.x * pw - 7, 0, stt.y * ph - 7)
                        stt.lbl.TextTransparency = 0.15 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(acc * 2.2 + stt.phase))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Style thin top bars
        for _, ch in ipairs(panel:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Frame") and ch.Name ~= "StarSnow" then
                local h = ch.Size.Y.Offset
                if h > 0 and h <= 10 and ch.Size.X.Scale >= 0.85 then
                    ch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    ch.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                    local bst = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                    if bst then
                        bst.Color = Color3.fromRGB(104, 126, 158)
                        bst.Transparency = 0.3
                    end
                    local bg = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                    if bg then
                        bg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(58, 70, 92)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(18, 22, 30)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(2, 3, 6)),
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Force glass on ALL zeno panels (runs after everything is built)
_G.ZenoRestyleAllPanels = function()
    local names = {
        "ZENOTPTOBEST", "ZENO_PLAYER_CONTROLS", "ZENO_INVIS_STEAL",
        "ZENO_STEAL_TARGET", "ZENO_UI_PRINCIPAL", "ZENO_HUB_PAID_BANNER",
        "ZENO_ADMIN_PANEL", "ZENO_UI_OPEN", "ZENO_NEAREST_PET",
    }
    local function scan(parent)
        if not parent then return end
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
            local g = parent:FindFirstChild(n)
            if g then
                -- main frame of each gui
                for _, ch in ipairs(g:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("Frame") and (ch.Name == "Panel" or ch.Name == "Main" or ch.Name == "MainPanel" or ch.Name == "Banner" or ch.Name == "Square") then
                        _G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle(ch, { transparency = 0.38, starCount = 14, corner = 18 })
                    end
                end
                -- also direct frames
                if g:IsA("Frame") then
                    _G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle(g, { transparency = 0.38, starCount = 12, corner = 16 })
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function() scan(playerGui) end)
    pcall(function() if gethui then scan(gethui()) end end)
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        scan(cg)
    end)
end

-- Applique le thème sur TOUS les panels Zeno
local _zenoThemeRainbowConn = nil
local function zenoApplyThemeEverywhere(th)
    if type(th) ~= "table" then return end
    Theme.Panel      = th.bg or Theme.Panel
    Theme.PanelAlt   = th.card or Theme.PanelAlt
    Theme.PanelField = th.field or Theme.PanelField
    if th.field then
        Theme.PanelField2 = Color3.new(
            math.min(1, th.field.R * 1.28),
            math.min(1, th.field.G * 1.28),
            math.min(1, th.field.B * 1.28)
        )
    end
    Theme.Border     = th.border or Theme.Border
    Theme.Outline    = th.accent or Theme.Outline
    Theme.Text       = th.text or Theme.Text
    Theme.TextDim    = th.mute or Theme.TextDim
    Theme.AccentA    = th.accent or Theme.AccentA
    Theme.AccentB    = th.accent2 or Theme.AccentB
    Theme.CardBg     = th.card or Theme.CardBg

    local names = {
        "ZENOTPTOBEST", "ZENO_PLAYER_CONTROLS", "ZENO_INVIS_STEAL",
        "ZENO_STEAL_TARGET", "ZENO_UI_PRINCIPAL", "ZENO_HUB_PAID_BANNER",
        "ZENO_UI_OPEN", "ZENO_STEAL_PROGRESS",
    }
    local roots = {}
    local function collect(parent)
        if not parent then return end
        for _, n in ipairs(names) do
            local g = parent:FindFirstChild(n)
            if g then roots[#roots + 1] = g end
        end
    end
    pcall(function() collect(playerGui) end)
    pcall(function() if gethui then collect(gethui()) end end)
    pcall(function() collect(game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)

    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
        pcall(function()
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    local c = d.Color
                    local avg = (c.R + c.G + c.B) / 3
                    if avg < 0.35 then
                        d.Color = Theme.Border
                    else
                        d.Color = Theme.AccentA
                    end
                elseif d:IsA("UIGradient") then
                    pcall(function()
                        d.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentA, Theme.AccentB)
                    end)
                elseif d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
                    if d.BackgroundTransparency < 0.85 then
                        local c = d.BackgroundColor3
                        local avg = (c.R + c.G + c.B) / 3
                        if avg < 0.08 then
                            d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
                        elseif avg < 0.14 then
                            d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelAlt
                        elseif avg < 0.22 then
                            d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField
                        elseif avg < 0.30 then
                            d.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField2
                        end
                    end
                elseif d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
                    local c = d.TextColor3
                    local avg = (c.R + c.G + c.B) / 3
                    if avg > 0.75 then
                        d.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                    elseif avg > 0.45 and avg < 0.75 then
                        d.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, ch in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("Frame") and ch.Name ~= "StarSnow" then
                    -- keep glass transparency, only nudge color
                    if ch.BackgroundTransparency < 0.85 then
                        ch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                        if ch.BackgroundTransparency < 0.15 then
                            ch.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if _zenoThemeRainbowConn then
        pcall(function() _zenoThemeRainbowConn:Disconnect() end)
        _zenoThemeRainbowConn = nil
    end
    if th.rainbow then
        local hue = 0
        local _zenoRainbowAcc = 0
        _zenoThemeRainbowConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            _zenoRainbowAcc = _zenoRainbowAcc + dt
            if _zenoRainbowAcc < 0.12 then return end
            _zenoRainbowAcc = 0
            hue = (hue + dt * 0.22) % 1
            local c1 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.85, 1)
            local c2 = Color3.fromHSV((hue + 0.33) % 1, 0.85, 1)
            Theme.AccentA, Theme.AccentB = c1, c2
            for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
                pcall(function()
                    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("UIGradient") then
                            d.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2)
                        elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                            local avg = (d.Color.R + d.Color.G + d.Color.B) / 3
                            if avg >= 0.35 then d.Color = c1 end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end
_G.ZenoApplyThemeEverywhere = zenoApplyThemeEverywhere

local function new(cl,props,parent)
    local i=Instance.new(cl) for k,v in pairs(props or {}) do i[k]=v end
    if parent then i.Parent=parent end return i
end
local function corner(p,r)  return new("UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,r or 10)},p) end
local function strokeOn(p,c,t,tr) return new("UIStroke",{Color=c or Theme.Border,Thickness=t or 1,Transparency=tr or 0},p) end
local function grad(p,cs,rot)    return new("UIGradient",{Color=cs,Rotation=rot or 0},p) end
local function accentGrad(p,r)   return grad(p,ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentA,Theme.AccentB),r or 0) end
local function bgDepthGrad(p,r)  return grad(p,ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(24,30,42), Color3.fromRGB(7,9,13)),r or 105) end
local function titleUnderline(p,w)
    local b=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(0,w or 34,0,2),Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,3),BackgroundColor3=Theme.AccentA,BorderSizePixel=0},p)
    corner(b,2); accentGrad(b,0); return b
end
local function tw(i,pr,t,s,d)
    local t2=TweenService:Create(i,TweenInfo.new(t or 0.2,s or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,d or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),pr) t2:Play(); return t2
end

do
    local function getSynchronizer()
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Synchronizer)
        if ok then return mod end
        return nil
    end

    local function getTable(name)
        local sync = getSynchronizer()
        if not sync then return nil end
        local ok, data = pcall(sync.GetTableFromChannel, sync, name)
        if ok then return data end
        return nil
    end

    _G.XenSyncAll = function()
        return {}
    end

    _G.XenSyncGet = function(name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
        return getTable(name)
    end

    _G.sProp = function(ch, key)
        if ch == nil or key == nil then return nil end
        -- Force load channel key if Synchronizer exposes :Get
        if type(ch) == "table" or typeof(ch) == "Instance" or type(ch) == "userdata" then
            pcall(function()
                if type(ch.Get) == "function" then
                    ch:Get(key)
                end
            end)
        end
        local ok, val = pcall(function()
            if type(ch) == "table" then
                return ch[key]
            end
            return nil
        end)
        if ok then return val end
        return nil
    end

    local _AD, _MD, _TD
    local function _data()
        if _AD then return true end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local d = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Datas")
            _AD = require(d:WaitForChild("Animals"))
            _MD = require(d:WaitForChild("Mutations"))
            _TD = require(d:WaitForChild("Traits"))
        end)
        return ok and _AD ~= nil
    end
    _G._xenGen = function(index, mutation, traits)
        if not _data() then return 0 end
        local info = _AD[index]
        if not info or not info.Generation then return 0 end
        local mult = 1
        if mutation and mutation ~= "None" and mutation ~= "" then
            local m = _MD[mutation]
            if m and m.Modifier then mult = mult + m.Modifier end
        end
        if type(traits) == "table" then
            for _, tr in ipairs(traits) do
                local t = _TD[tr]
                if t and t.MultiplierModifier then mult = mult + t.MultiplierModifier end
            end
        end
        return info.Generation * mult
    end
    _G._xenAnimShim = setmetatable({
        GetGeneration = function(_, index, mutation, traits)
            return _G._xenGen(index, mutation, traits)
        end
    }, {
        __index = function(_, k)
            local ok, real = pcall(function()
                return require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared"):WaitForChild("Animals"))
            end)
            if ok and type(real) == "table" then return rawget(real, k) end
            return nil
        end
    })
end

task.spawn(function()
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat
        plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then task.wait(0.05) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    local done, warmT0 = {}, os.clock()
    while (os.clock() - warmT0) < 15 do
        local pending = false
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            if not done[plot.Name] then
                local ch = _G.XenSyncGet and _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name)
                if ch then
                    pcall(function()
                        if ch.Get then ch:Get("AnimalList"); ch:Get("Owner") end
                    end)
                    if _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList") ~= nil then
                        done[plot.Name] = true
                    else
                        pending = true
                    end
                else
                    pending = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not pending and #plots:GetChildren() > 0 then break end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    if not Workspace.StreamingEnabled then return end
    local plots
    local t0 = os.clock()
    repeat
        plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then task.wait(0.1) end
    until plots or (os.clock() - t0) > 25
    if not plots then return end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local pos
        pcall(function()
            pos = plot:GetPivot().Position
        end)
        if pos then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function() player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos) end)
            end)
        end
    end
end)

do
    local RS = ReplicatedStorage
    local function resolveRemote(name)
        local pkgs = RS:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        local Net = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Net")
        if not Net then return nil end
        local slots, byLeaf = {}, {}
        for i, c in ipairs(Net:GetChildren()) do
            local p, rest = string.match(c.Name, "^(%a+)/(.+)$")
            if p and #rest == 64 and string.match(rest, "^%x+$") then
                slots[i] = c
                byLeaf[rest] = byLeaf[rest] or {}
                table.insert(byLeaf[rest], { slot = i, class = c.ClassName })
            end
        end
        if name then
            for leaf, list in pairs(byLeaf) do
                if leaf == name then
                    for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                        if e.class == "RemoteEvent" then
                            return slots[e.slot]
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local anchor, dual = nil, 0
        for _, list in pairs(byLeaf) do
            if #list > 1 then
                dual = dual + 1
                for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                    if e.class == "RemoteEvent" then anchor = e.slot end
                end
            end
        end
        if dual ~= 1 or not anchor then return nil end
        local t = slots[anchor - 1]
        if t and t.ClassName == "RemoteEvent" and t.Parent == Net then return t end
        return nil
    end

    local remoteCache = {}
    local function getRemote(name)
        local key = name or "__UseItem"
        if remoteCache[key] and remoteCache[key].Parent then return remoteCache[key] end
        local r = resolveRemote(name)
        if r then remoteCache[key] = r end
        return r
    end

    _G.Net = {
        RemoteEvent = function(_, name) return getRemote(name) end,
        RemoteFunction = function(_, name) return nil end,
        UnreliableRemoteEvent = function(_, name) return nil end,
    }

    _G.sabcomFireGrapple2 = function(pos)
        local r = getRemote()
        if not r then return false end
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        pos = pos or (hrp.Position + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * 100)
        local dist = (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude
        local a = math.clamp(dist / 120, 0, 2)
        return pcall(function()
            r:FireServer(a, pos)
        end)
    end
    _G.sabcomGrappleReady = function()
        return getRemote() ~= nil
    end
end

_G.sabcomInstantClone = function()
    local c = player.Character
    if not c then return end
    local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then return end
    local cl = player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner") or c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
    if not cl then return end
    pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end)
    task.wait()
    if cl.Parent ~= c then h:EquipTool(cl); task.wait() end
    local tf = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
    local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
    local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
    if not tb then return end

    _G.isCloning = true
    cl:Activate()
    task.wait(0.05)
    tb.Visible = true

    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Click) end)
    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Up) end)
    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.Activated) end)

    task.delay(0.55, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
end

local function disableHRPAntiCheat()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    if not getconnections then return end
    for _, sigName in ipairs({"CFrame", "Position"}) do
        local sig = hrp:GetPropertyChangedSignal(sigName)
        if sig then
            for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(sig)) do
                if conn.Enabled and conn.Function then
                    local ok, info = pcall(getinfo, conn.Function)
                    if ok and info and tostring(info.source):find("ReplicatedFirst") and tostring(info.source):find("test") then
                        pcall(function() conn:Disable() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        pcall(disableHRPAntiCheat)
    end
end)

pcall(function()
    local function silenceFTUE()
        if not (getconnections and getinfo) then return end
        local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
        for _, sig in ipairs({ RS.RenderStepped, RS.Heartbeat, RS.Stepped }) do
            pcall(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(sig)) do
                    local f = c.Function
                    if f and c.Enabled then
                        local ok, info = pcall(getinfo, f)
                        if ok and info and tostring(info.source):find("FTUEController") then
                            pcall(function() c:Disable() end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    LogService.MessageOut:Connect(function(msg, msgType)
        if msgType == Enum.MessageType.MessageError and tostring(msg):find("TutorialArrow", 1, true) then
            task.defer(silenceFTUE)
        end
    end)
end)

-- ================================================================
-- INSTANT RESET (JEW / Rose style: velocity fling + camera hold)
-- ================================================================
do
    local connections = {}
    local active, busy, queuedUntil = true, false, 0
    local releaseHold = nil

    local function restoreCamera(character)
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then camera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
    end

    local function holdCamera(character)
        if releaseHold then releaseHold() end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local cf, focus = camera.CFrame, camera.Focus
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        local connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if Workspace.CurrentCamera ~= camera then return end
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
            camera.CFrame, camera.Focus = cf, focus
        end)
        local released = false
        local function release()
            if released then return end
            released = true
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
            restoreCamera(player.Character)
            releaseHold = nil
        end
        releaseHold = release
        task.delay(3, release)
    end

    local function flingBody(character)
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local previousStand = humanoid and humanoid.PlatformStand
        if humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
            end)
        end
        local function pulse()
            if not active or not root.Parent or player.Character ~= character then return false end
            return pcall(function()
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000000, 0)
            end)
        end
        local ok = pulse()
        if ok then
            task.spawn(function()
                local elapsed = 0
                while active and elapsed < 1.5 do
                    local dt = RunService.PreSimulation:Wait()
                    elapsed = elapsed + (typeof(dt) == "number" and dt or 0.016)
                    if not pulse() then break end
                end
            end)
        end
        task.delay(2, function()
            if active and player.Character == character and humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                pcall(function() humanoid.PlatformStand = previousStand end)
            end
        end)
        return ok
    end

    local function doInstantReset()
        if not active then return false end
        if busy then return true end
        local character = player.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not character or not humanoid or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then
            queuedUntil = os.clock() + 15
            return true
        end
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            flingBody(character)
            queuedUntil = os.clock() + 15
            return true
        end
        busy = true
        holdCamera(character)
        local ok = flingBody(character)
        if not ok and releaseHold then releaseHold() end
        task.delay(3, function()
            if player.Character == character then busy = false end
        end)
        return ok
    end

    table.insert(connections, player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        if releaseHold then releaseHold() end
    end))
    table.insert(connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        busy = false
        if releaseHold then releaseHold() end
        task.spawn(function()
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not active or player.Character ~= character then return end
            restoreCamera(character)
            if queuedUntil == 0 then return end
            local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            local pending = os.clock() <= queuedUntil
            queuedUntil = 0
            if active and pending and player.Character == character and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and root then
                doInstantReset()
            end
        end)
    end))

    _G.ZenoInstantReset = doInstantReset
    _G.JAF_DoReset = doInstantReset
    _G.JAF_ExecuteReset = doInstantReset

    -- Override Roblox reset button
    local resetEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
    resetEvent.Event:Connect(function()
        pcall(doInstantReset)
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", resetEvent)
            end)
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)

    -- Keybind (Config.resetKey, default X)
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
        if gp then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        local key = tostring(Config.resetKey or "X"):upper()
        if input.KeyCode.Name:upper() == key then
            pcall(doInstantReset)
        end
    end)
end

-- Floating bar animation (smooth bob + shimmer)
local function zenoFloatBar(bar, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    if not bar or not bar.Parent then return end
    local amp = opts.amp or 3
    local period = opts.period or 1.8
    local shimmer = opts.shimmer ~= false
    local basePos = bar.Position
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while bar and bar.Parent do
            local t = os.clock() - t0
            local y = math.sin(t * (math.pi * 2 / period)) * amp
            bar.Position = UDim2.new(basePos.X.Scale, basePos.X.Offset, basePos.Y.Scale, basePos.Y.Offset + y)
            if shimmer then
                local g = bar:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                if g then
                    g.Rotation = (t * 35) % 360
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
    end)
end
_G.ZenoFloatBar = zenoFloatBar

    end)
end)

local function createToggle(parent,label,order,def,green,key)
    local row=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,15),BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=order},parent)
    new("TextLabel",{Text=label,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=10,TextColor3=Theme.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(0.7,0,1,0)},row)
    local track=new("TextButton",{Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,Size=UDim2.new(0,30,0,15),Position=UDim2.new(1,-30,0.5,-7.5),BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2},row)
    corner(track,9)
    local knob=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(0,12,0,12),Position=UDim2.new(0,2.5,0.5,-6),BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(206, 221, 240)},track)
    corner(knob,7)
    local state={on=def}
    local function apply(anim)
        local onColor  = green and Theme.GreenDark or Theme.AccentB
        local kc       = state.on and Color3.new(1,1,1) or Color3.fromRGB(206, 221, 240)
        local tc       = state.on and onColor or Theme.PanelField2
        local kp       = state.on and UDim2.new(0,17.5,0.5,-6) or UDim2.new(0,2.5,0.5,-6)
        if anim then tw(track,{BackgroundColor3=tc},0.18); tw(knob,{Position=kp,BackgroundColor3=kc},0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        else track.BackgroundColor3=tc; knob.Position=kp; knob.BackgroundColor3=kc end
        local g=track:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if state.on then
            if not g then g=accentGrad(track,90) end
            g.Color=green and ColorSequence.new(Theme.Green,Theme.GreenDark) or ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentA,Theme.AccentB)
        elseif g then g:Destroy() end
    end
    apply(false)
    track.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() state.on=not state.on; apply(true); if key then Config[key]=state.on; saveConfig() end end)
    return {Block=row,SetState=function(on) state.on=on; apply(true) end, GetState=function() return state.on end}
end

local segOptions = {"Gen","Value","Prio","Near"}
local segValueMap = {Gen="gen", Value="value", Prio="priority", Near="nearest"}
local segButtons = {}
local activeSeg  = nil
local function refreshSeg()
    for _,e in ipairs(segButtons) do
        local active = e.Value==activeSeg
        tw(e.Instance,{BackgroundColor3=active and Theme.PanelField2 or Theme.PanelField},0.15)
        e.Stroke.Color = active and Theme.AccentB or Theme.Border
        e.Label.TextColor3 = active and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDim
    end
end
local function applySeg(value)
    activeSeg=(value~="" and value~=nil) and value or nil
    Config.targetMode = segValueMap[activeSeg] or "gen"
    saveConfig(); refreshSeg(); pcall(updateStealTargetDisplay)
end
for i,opt in ipairs(segOptions) do
    local b=new("TextButton",{Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,Size=UDim2.new(0.25,-2,1,0),BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField,LayoutOrder=i},segRow)
    corner(b,5); local stk=strokeOn(b,Theme.Border,1)
    local lbl=new("TextLabel",{Text=opt,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=9,TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)},b)
    table.insert(segButtons,{Instance=b,Label=lbl,Stroke=stk,Value=opt})
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() activeSeg=(activeSeg==opt) and nil or opt; applySeg(activeSeg) end)
end
local initSegMap = {gen="Gen",value="Value",priority="Prio",nearest="Near"}
applySeg(initSegMap[Config.targetMode] or "Gen")

-- createSlider still needed for INVIS panel later
local function createSlider(parent,label,order,min,max,def,dec,suffix,key,liveUpdate)
    local block=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,32),BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=order},parent)
    local head=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,13),BackgroundTransparency=1},block)
    new("TextLabel",{Text=label,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=11,TextColor3=Theme.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(0.62,0,1,0)},head)
    local valLbl=new("TextLabel",{Text=tostring(def)..(suffix or ""),Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=10,TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(0.38,0,1,0),Position=UDim2.new(0.62,0,0,0)},head)
    local track=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,4),Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,19),BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2,Active=true},block)
    corner(track,3)
    local fill=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(0,0,1,0),BackgroundColor3=Theme.AccentB},track); corner(fill,3); accentGrad(fill,0)
    local knob=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(0,13,0,13),Position=UDim2.new(0,-6.5,0.5,-6.5),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),Active=true},track)
    corner(knob,7); strokeOn(knob,Theme.AccentB,1.5)
    local dragging,cur=false,def
    local function setAlpha(a)
        a=math.clamp(a,0,1)
        local raw=min+(max-min)*a
        if dec==0 then raw=math.floor(raw+0.5) else raw=math.floor(raw*10^dec+0.5)/10^dec end
        fill.Size=UDim2.new(a,0,1,0); knob.Position=UDim2.new(a,-6.5,0.5,-6.5)
        valLbl.Text=(dec==0 and tostring(raw) or string.format("%."..dec.."f",raw))..(suffix or "")
        cur=raw; if liveUpdate then liveUpdate(cur) end; return raw
    end
    setAlpha((def-min)/(max-min))
    local function update(inp) if not dragging then return end setAlpha((inp.Position.X-track.AbsolutePosition.X)/track.AbsoluteSize.X) end
    track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=true; update(i) end end)
    knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=true end end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then update(i) end end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and dragging then
            dragging=false; if key then Config[key]=cur; saveConfig() end
        end
    end)
    return {Block=block,GetValue=function() return cur end,SetValue=function(v) setAlpha((v-min)/(max-min)) end}
end
