local function createModal(titleText,w,cr,fixedH)
    local overlay=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(3, 5, 9),BackgroundTransparency=1,Visible=false,Active=true,ZIndex=20},screenGui)
    local box=new("Frame",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0),Size=fixedH and UDim2.new(0,w,0,fixedH) or UDim2.new(0,w,0,0),AutomaticSize=fixedH and Enum.AutomaticSize.None or Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel,Active=true,ZIndex=21},overlay)
    corner(box,cr or 12); strokeOn(box,Theme.Outline,1.2); bgDepthGrad(box,100)
    new("UIPadding",{PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,10),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,10),PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,10),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,10)},box)
    new("UIListLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,7)},box)
    local head=new("Frame",{Name="Header",Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,20),BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=1,ZIndex=21},box)
    local hTitle=new("TextLabel",{Text=titleText,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=13,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(0.75,0,1,0),ZIndex=21},head)
    accentGrad(hTitle,0); titleUnderline(hTitle,28)
    local closeBtn=new("TextButton",{Text="✕",Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=11,TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,AutoButtonColor=false,BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField,Size=UDim2.new(0,22,0,22),Position=UDim2.new(1,-22,0,-1),ZIndex=22},head)
    corner(closeBtn,6); strokeOn(closeBtn,Theme.Border,1)
    closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tw(closeBtn,{BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2},0.12) end)
    closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(closeBtn,{BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField},0.12)  end)
    local content,scroll
    if fixedH then
        content=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,fixedH-40),BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=2,ZIndex=21},box)
        scroll=new("ScrollingFrame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,ScrollBarThickness=3,ScrollBarImageColor3=Theme.AccentB,CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0),AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,Active=true},content)
        new("UIPadding",{PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,2),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,4)},scroll)
        new("UIListLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,3)},scroll)
    else
        content=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=2,ZIndex=21},box)
    end
    local isOpen=false
    local function open()
        if isOpen then return end; isOpen=true; overlay.Visible=true
        box.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,12); box.BackgroundTransparency=1
        pushBlur(); tw(overlay,{BackgroundTransparency=0.45},0.22); tw(box,{Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0),BackgroundTransparency=0},0.22,Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    end
    local function close()
        if not isOpen then return end; isOpen=false; popBlur()
        tw(overlay,{BackgroundTransparency=1},0.18)
        local t2=tw(box,{Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,12),BackgroundTransparency=1},0.18)
        t2.Completed:Connect(function() if not isOpen then overlay.Visible=false end end)
    end
    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(close)
    overlay.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local bp,bs=box.AbsolutePosition,box.AbsoluteSize; local p=i.Position
        if p.X<bp.X or p.X>bp.X+bs.X or p.Y<bp.Y or p.Y>bp.Y+bs.Y then close() end
    end)
    return {Overlay=overlay,Box=box,Header=head,Content=content,ScrollFrame=scroll,Open=open,Close=close}
end

    local dragging,startInput,startPos
    local function isPtr(i) return i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch end
    settingsModal.Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if not isPtr(i) then return end
        if i.Target and i.Target:IsA("TextButton") then return end
        dragging=true; startInput=i.Position; startPos=settingsModal.Box.Position
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if not dragging then return end
        if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local d=i.Position-startInput
        settingsModal.Box.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if isPtr(i) then dragging=false end end)
end

local function createRadioToggle(parent,def)
    local track=new("TextButton",{Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,20),BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2},parent)
    corner(track,10)
    local knob=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(0,14,0,14),Position=UDim2.new(0,2.5,0.5,-7),BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(206, 221, 240)},track)
    corner(knob,7)
    local state=def
    local function apply(anim)
        local kc=state and Color3.new(1,1,1) or Color3.fromRGB(206, 221, 240)
        local tc=state and Theme.AccentB or Theme.PanelField2
        local kp=state and UDim2.new(1,-16.5,0.5,-7) or UDim2.new(0,2.5,0.5,-7)
        if anim then tw(track,{BackgroundColor3=tc},0.18); tw(knob,{Position=kp,BackgroundColor3=kc},0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        else track.BackgroundColor3=tc; knob.Position=kp; knob.BackgroundColor3=kc end
        local g=track:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if state then if not g then g=accentGrad(track,90) end g.Color=ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentA,Theme.AccentB) elseif g then g:Destroy() end
    end
    apply(false)
    return {Track=track,SetState=function(on) state=on; apply(true) end,GetState=function() return state end,ConnectClick=function(cb) track.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb) end}
end

local function addRadioCard(par,label,desc,order,def,group,onSelect)
    local card=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,30),BackgroundColor3=Theme.CardBg,BorderSizePixel=0,LayoutOrder=order},par)
    corner(card,7); grad(card,ColorSequence.new(Theme.PanelField,Theme.PanelField2),90)
    local left=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,-44,1,-4),Position=UDim2.new(0,5,0,2),BackgroundTransparency=1},card)
    new("TextLabel",{Text=label,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=10,TextColor3=Theme.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,38)},left)
    new("TextLabel",{Text=desc,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=8,TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-14),Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,14)},left)
    local toggle=createRadioToggle(card,def); toggle.Track.Position=UDim2.new(1,-38,0.5,-10)
    toggle.ConnectClick(function()
        if toggle.GetState() then return end
        for _,t in ipairs(group) do t.SetState(false) end
        toggle.SetState(true); if onSelect then onSelect() end
    end)
    return toggle
end

local function addSliderCard(par,label,order,mn,mx,def,dec,suf,key,live)

    corner(card,7); grad(card,ColorSequence.new(Theme.PanelField,Theme.PanelField2),90)
    local inner=new("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,1,-4),Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,2),BackgroundTransparency=1},card)
    return createSlider(inner,label,1,mn,mx,def,dec,suf,key,live)
end

    titleUnderline(lbl,38); return f
end

local Synchronizer, AnimalsData, AnimalsShared, NumberUtils
local function loadModules()
    if Synchronizer then return true end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local pkg = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages",5)
        local dat = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas",5)
        local sh  = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared",5)
        local ut  = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Utils",5)
        Synchronizer  = require(pkg:WaitForChild("Synchronizer"))
        AnimalsData   = require(dat:WaitForChild("Animals"))
        AnimalsShared = _G._xenAnimShim
        NumberUtils   = require(ut:WaitForChild("NumberUtils"))
    end)
    return ok and Synchronizer ~= nil
end

local CARPET_NAMES={"Flying Carpet","Carpet","Cloud","Witch's Broom","Cupid's Wings","Santa's Sleigh","Magic Carpet","Waverider"}
local function findTool(name)
    local c=player.Character; local bp=player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    return (c and c:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
end
local function equipCarpet()
    local c=player.Character; local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if not h then return nil end
    for _,n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
        if c:FindFirstChild(n) then return n end
    end
    for _,n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
        local t=findTool(n)
        if t and t:IsA("Tool") then if t.Parent~=c then pcall(function() h:EquipTool(t) end) end return n end
    end
    return nil
end
local function vZero(hrp) if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero; hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end end

local function carpetEngage()
    local t0=os.clock()
    while not findTool("Grapple Hook") and os.clock()-t0<5 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
    local c=player.Character; local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if not c or not h then return nil end
    if not c:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then local g=findTool("Grapple Hook"); if g then pcall(function() h:EquipTool(g) end) end end
    task.wait(0.08)
    _G.sabcomFireGrapple2()
    task.wait(0.15)
    local hh=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hh then pcall(function() hh:UnequipTools() end) end
    task.wait(0.15)
    local cn; local tc=os.clock()
    repeat cn=equipCarpet(); local cc=player.Character; if cn and cc and cc:FindFirstChild(cn) then break end RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() until os.clock()-tc>1.5
    return cn
end

local function doClone()
    if _G.sabcomInstantClone then
        return pcall(_G.sabcomInstantClone)
    end
    return false
end

local allPetsCache = {}
local _PetPosCache = {}

local function getPetPosition(plot, slot)
    local key = plot.Name .. "|" .. tostring(slot)
    local hit = _PetPosCache[key]
    local now = os.clock()
    if hit and now < hit.exp then return hit.pos end

    local function compute()
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return nil end
        local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot))
        if not podium then return nil end
        for _, desc in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("Model") and desc.Name ~= "Claim" and desc.Name ~= "Base" and desc.Name ~= "Decorations" then
                local hasMesh = false
                for _, c in ipairs(desc:GetDescendants()) do
                    if c:IsA("MeshPart") then hasMesh = true; break end
                end
                if hasMesh then
                    local ok, cf = pcall(function() return desc:GetBoundingBox() end)
                    if ok then return cf.Position end
                end
            end
        end
        local ok, cf = pcall(function() return podium:GetPivot() end)
        if ok then return cf.Position end
        return nil
    end

    local pos = compute()
    if pos then
        _PetPosCache[key] = { pos = pos, exp = now + 12 + math.random() * 8 }
    end
    return pos
end

local function resolveOwner(owner)
    if owner == nil then return nil end
    local plr
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" then
            plr = owner:IsA("Player") and owner or Players:FindFirstChild(owner.Name)
        elseif type(owner) == "number" then
            plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner)
        elseif type(owner) == "string" then
            if tonumber(owner) then plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(owner)) end
            if not plr then
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p.Name:lower() == owner:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower() == owner:lower() then
                        plr = p; break
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif type(owner) == "table" then
            if owner.UserId then plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner.UserId) end
            if not plr and owner.Name then plr = Players:FindFirstChild(tostring(owner.Name)) end
        end
    end)
    return plr
end

local function isOwnPlot(plotName)
    local ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plotName)
    if not ch then return false end
    local owner = _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")
    local plr = resolveOwner(owner)
    if plr then return plr.UserId == player.UserId end
    if type(owner) == "string" then
        local o = owner:lower()
        return o == player.Name:lower() or o == player.DisplayName:lower()
    end
    return false
end
_G.ZenoIsOwnPlot = isOwnPlot

local function ownerInGame(channel)
    if not channel then return false end
    local owner = _G.sProp(channel, "Owner")
    if not owner then return false end
    if resolveOwner(owner) then return true end
    if typeof(owner) == "Instance" or type(owner) == "number"
       or (type(owner) == "table" and (owner.UserId or owner.Name)) then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

local function scanAllPets()
    local pets = {}
    pcall(loadModules)
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        allPetsCache = pets
        return pets
    end

    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        -- Skip own plot only
        local own = false
        pcall(function() own = isOwnPlot(plot.Name) end)
        if own then continue end

        local ch = _G.XenSyncGet and _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name)
        if not ch then
            -- Try warm the channel
            pcall(function()
                local sync = nil
                local okS, mod = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Synchronizer)
                if okS then sync = mod end
                if sync and sync.GetTableFromChannel then
                    ch = sync:GetTableFromChannel(plot.Name)
                end
            end)
        end
        if not ch then continue end

        -- Force-load AnimalList + Owner
        pcall(function()
            if type(ch.Get) == "function" then
                ch:Get("AnimalList")
                ch:Get("Owner")
            end
        end)

        -- Soft owner check: only skip if we KNOW owner is offline Instance/UserId not in game
        -- Still allow plots with animals even if owner left (brainrots stay)
        local animalList = _G.sProp(ch, "AnimalList")
        if type(animalList) ~= "table" then continue end

        for slot, data in pairs(animalList) do
            if type(data) ~= "table" then continue end
            local name = data.Index or data.Name or data.Animal or data.Id
            if not name or name == "" then continue end
            name = tostring(name)

            -- Skip machines actively fusing/trading
            if type(data.Machine) == "table" and data.Machine.Active
               and ({Fuse=true, Duel=true, Trade=true, Crafting=true})[data.Machine.Type] then
                continue
            end

            local info = AnimalsData and AnimalsData[name]
            local displayName = (info and (info.DisplayName or info.Name)) or name

            local gen = 0
            pcall(function()
                gen = _G._xenAnimShim:GetGeneration(name, data.Mutation, data.Traits, nil)
            end)
            if gen <= 0 and info and type(info.Generation) == "number" then
                gen = info.Generation
            end
            -- Last resort: still show the pet with gen=1 so it appears in UI
            if gen <= 0 then gen = 1 end

            local pos = nil
            pcall(function() pos = getPetPosition(plot, slot) end)
            -- IMPORTANT: do NOT skip if position is nil (streaming / not loaded yet)

            local ownerName = nil
            pcall(function()
                local owner = _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")
                local plr = resolveOwner(owner)
                if plr then
                    ownerName = plr.DisplayName or plr.Name
                elseif type(owner) == "string" then
                    ownerName = owner:match("^(.+)'s Base$") or owner
                end
            end)

            table.insert(pets, {
                name     = displayName,
                index    = name,
                mps      = gen,
                mutation = data.Mutation or "None",
                position = pos,
                plot     = plot.Name,
                slot     = tostring(slot),
                uid      = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot),
                owner    = ownerName,
            })
        end
    end

    -- Fallback: scan podiums in workspace if sync returned nothing
    if #pets == 0 then
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local own = false
            pcall(function() own = isOwnPlot(plot.Name) end)
            if own then continue end
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not podiums then continue end
            for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                local model = nil
                for _, desc in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("Model") and desc.Name ~= "Claim" and desc.Name ~= "Base" and desc.Name ~= "Decorations" then
                        for _, c in ipairs(desc:GetDescendants()) do
                            if c:IsA("MeshPart") then model = desc; break end
                        end
                        if model then break end
                    end
                end
                if model then
                    local pos = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        local cf = model:GetBoundingBox()
                        pos = cf.Position
                    end)
                    local ownerName = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                        local gui = sign and sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                        local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                        local text = label and label.Text or ""
                        if text ~= "" and not text:lower():find("empty") then
                            ownerName = text:match("^(.+)'s Base$") or text
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(pets, {
                        name     = model.Name,
                        index    = model.Name,
                        mps      = 1,
                        mutation = "None",
                        position = pos,
                        plot     = plot.Name,
                        slot     = tostring(podium.Name),
                        uid      = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(podium.Name),
                        owner    = ownerName,
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(pets, function(a, b) return (a.mps or 0) > (b.mps or 0) end)
    allPetsCache = pets
    _G.ZenoAllPetsCache = pets
    return pets
end

local function getBestPet(mode)
    local pets = allPetsCache
    if type(pets) ~= "table" or #pets == 0 then
        pcall(scanAllPets)
        pets = allPetsCache
    end
    if type(pets) ~= "table" or #pets == 0 then return nil end
    local minG = tonumber(Config.minGen) or 0
    local function ok(p)
        return p and (tonumber(p.mps) or 0) >= minG
    end
    -- force nearest from stealMode / stealNearest
    local sm = tostring(Config.stealMode or "normal"):lower()
    if Config.stealNearest == true or sm == "nearest" then
        mode = "nearest"
    end
    mode = mode or Config.targetMode or "gen"

    if mode == "nearest" then
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then
            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if ok(p) then return p end end
            return nil
        end
        local best, bestD, bestMps = nil, math.huge, -1
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
            if ok(p) and typeof(p.position) == "Vector3" then
                local d = (p.position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                local m = tonumber(p.mps) or 0
                -- prefer closer; if similar distance, prefer higher gen
                if d < bestD - 2 or (math.abs(d - bestD) <= 2 and m > bestMps) then
                    bestD, bestMps, best = d, m, p
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    if mode == "gen" or mode == "value" then
        -- pets already sorted by mps desc in scanAllPets
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if ok(p) then return p end end
        return nil
    elseif mode == "priority" then
        for _, pname in ipairs(priorityList or {}) do
            local s = tostring(pname):lower()
            for _, p in ipairs(pets) do
                if ok(p) and ((p.name and p.name:lower() == s) or (p.index and tostring(p.index):lower() == s)) then
                    return p
                end
            end
        end
        for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if ok(p) then return p end end
        return nil
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(pets) do if ok(p) then return p end end
    return nil
end

local SelectedStealTarget = nil
local function getTargetPetData()
    if SelectedStealTarget then
        for _,p in ipairs(allPetsCache) do
            if p.uid == SelectedStealTarget.uid then
                return p
            end
        end
        SelectedStealTarget = nil
    end
    return getBestPet(Config.targetMode)
end

updateStealTargetDisplay = function()
    local pet = getBestPet(Config.targetMode)
    stealTargetLabel.Text = "Target: " .. (pet and pet.name or "None")
end

local InternalStealCache = {}
local function getStealHold()
    local mode = tostring(Config.stealMode or "normal"):lower()
    if mode == "ultra" then return 0.35 end
    if mode == "nearest" then return 0.85 end
    return 1.3 -- normal / moyen
end
local function getStealRange()
    local mode = tostring(Config.stealMode or "normal"):lower()
    if mode == "ultra" then return 110 end
    if mode == "nearest" then return 90 end
    return 65
end
local STEAL_HOLD = 1.3 -- legacy fallback; prefer getStealHold()


-- ================================================================
-- STEAL PROGRESS BAR
-- ================================================================
do
    -- Petit carre: nom du brainrot le plus proche (pas de barre stealing)
    local gui, panel, nameLbl, genLbl
    local conn
    local lastT = 0

    local function fmtGen(mps)
        mps = tonumber(mps) or 0
        if mps >= 1e12 then return string.format("%.1fT/s", mps / 1e12) end
        if mps >= 1e9 then return string.format("%.1fB/s", mps / 1e9) end
        if mps >= 1e6 then return string.format("%.1fM/s", mps / 1e6) end
        if mps >= 1e3 then return string.format("%.1fK/s", mps / 1e3) end
        return tostring(math.floor(mps + 0.5)) .. "/s"
    end

    local function getNearest()
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local cache = rawget(_G, "ZenoAllPetsCache")
        if type(cache) ~= "table" then
            cache = (type(allPetsCache) == "table" and allPetsCache) or nil
        end
        if type(cache) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
        local hp = hrp.Position
        for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
            local pos = pet.position
            if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
                local d = (pos - hp).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then bestD, best = d, pet end
            end
        end
        return best, bestD
    end

    local function ensure()
        if gui and gui.Parent then return end
        local parent = playerGui
        if not parent then return end
        local old = parent:FindFirstChild("ZENO_NEAREST_PET")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        -- detruire ancienne barre stealing si presente
        local oldBar = parent:FindFirstChild("ZENO_STEAL_PROGRESS")
        if oldBar then oldBar:Destroy() end

        gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "ZENO_NEAREST_PET"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.DisplayOrder = 1400
        gui.Parent = parent

        panel = Instance.new("Frame")
        panel.Name = "Square"
        panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        do
            local p = Config.posNearest or { X=0, XOffset=14, Y=0, YOffset=120 }
            panel.Position = UDim2.new(p.X or 0, p.XOffset or 14, p.Y or 0, p.YOffset or 120)
        end
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 54)
        panel.Visible = Config.showNearest ~= false
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        panel.ClipsDescendants = true
        panel.Parent = gui
        Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(96, 128, 168)
        st.Thickness = 1.15
        st.Transparency = 0.25
        st.Parent = panel
        pcall(function()
            if type(_G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle(panel, { transparency = 0.35, starCount = 8, corner = 14 })
            end
        end)

        local _zl = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        _zl.Name = "ZenoLogoMark"
        _zl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _zl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _zl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26,26)
        _zl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
        _zl.ZIndex = 5
        _zl.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        _zl.Image = "rbxassetid://92530687076466"
        _zl.Parent = panel
        do local _c=Instance.new("UICorner"); _c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10); _c.Parent=_zl end
        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 12)
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 6)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        title.TextSize = 9
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 180)
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.Text = "NEAREST"
        title.Parent = panel

        nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 16)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 16)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLbl.TextSize = 11
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 255)
        nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        nameLbl.Text = "—"
        nameLbl.Parent = panel

        genLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        genLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 14)
        genLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 34)
        genLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        genLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        genLbl.TextSize = 10
        genLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 80)
        genLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        genLbl.Text = ""
        genLbl.Parent = panel

        -- drag
        do
            local dragging, startIn, startPos
            panel.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    startIn = i.Position
                    startPos = panel.Position
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if not dragging then return end
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    local d = i.Position - startIn
                    panel.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if dragging then
                        Config.posNearest = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
                        pcall(saveConfig)
                    end
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function tick()
        ensure()
        if panel then panel.Visible = Config.showNearest ~= false end
        local pet, dist = getNearest()
        if pet then
            local n = pet.displayName or pet.name or pet.index or "?"
            nameLbl.Text = tostring(n)
            local g = fmtGen(pet.mps or pet.gen or 0)
            local d = dist and string.format("%.0fm", dist) or ""
            genLbl.Text = g .. (d ~= "" and (" · " .. d) or "")
        else
            nameLbl.Text = "—"
            genLbl.Text = "no pet"
        end
    end

    task.defer(function()
        ensure()
        if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastT < 0.3 then return end
            lastT = now
            pcall(tick)
        end)
    end)
end

-- ================================================================
-- ANTI-ROLLBACK (soft — ne snap PAS le CFrame = moins de fight serveur)
-- ================================================================
do
    local arOn = Config.antiRollback ~= false
    local arConn = nil
    local lastGoodPos = nil
    local lastGoodVel = nil
    local lastSamplePos = nil
    local lastSampleT = 0
    local notifyCD = 0
    local lastFixT = 0

    local function isBusyTP()
        if _G.isTeleporting or _G.SabcomIsTeleporting or _G.sabcomIsTeleporting then return true end
        if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        local c = player.Character
        if c and c:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        -- aussi pendant auto buy follow
        if rawget(_G, "ZenoAutoBuyFollowing") then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function getHRP()
        local c = player.Character
        return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function softNotify(msg)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - notifyCD < 2.0 then return end
        notifyCD = now
        if type(_G.ZenoNotify) == "function" then
            _G.ZenoNotify(msg, Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 80))
        end
    end

    local lastGoodCF = nil
    local recoverUntil = 0
    local recoverPos = nil

    local function tickAntiRollback()
        if not arOn then return end
        local hrp = getHRP()
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
            lastGoodPos, lastGoodVel, lastSamplePos, lastGoodCF = nil, nil, nil, nil
            return
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        -- Pendant steal / TP / buy follow : ne RIEN forcer
        if isBusyTP() then
            lastGoodPos = hrp.Position
            lastGoodVel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            lastGoodCF = hrp.CFrame
            lastSamplePos = lastGoodPos
            lastSampleT = now
            recoverUntil = 0
            return
        end

        local pos = hrp.Position
        local vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local sens = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.antiRollbackSens) or 14, 8, 50)

        -- Phase recovery douce (seulement gros lagbacks)
        if now < recoverUntil and recoverPos then
            pcall(function()
                local cur = hrp.Position
                local target = Vector3.new(recoverPos.X, cur.Y, recoverPos.Z)
                local blended = cur:Lerp(target, 0.35)
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(blended) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                if lastGoodVel then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(lastGoodVel.X, vel.Y, lastGoodVel.Z)
                end
            end)
            lastSamplePos = hrp.Position
            lastSampleT = now
            return
        end

        if lastSamplePos then
            local dt = now - lastSampleT
            if dt > 0.02 and dt < 0.4 then
                local predicted = lastSamplePos
                if lastGoodVel and lastGoodVel.Magnitude > 4 then
                    predicted = lastSamplePos + Vector3.new(lastGoodVel.X, 0, lastGoodVel.Z) * dt
                end
                local flatCur = Vector3.new(pos.X, 0, pos.Z)
                local flatPred = Vector3.new(predicted.X, 0, predicted.Z)
                local backDist = (flatCur - flatPred).Magnitude
                local delta = (pos - lastSamplePos).Magnitude
                local wasMoving = lastGoodVel and lastGoodVel.Magnitude > 8

                -- 1) petit lagback: vitesse only
                if wasMoving and backDist > sens * 0.55 and delta > sens * 0.4 and (now - lastFixT) > 0.25 then
                    lastFixT = now
                    pcall(function()
                        if lastGoodVel then
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(lastGoodVel.X, vel.Y, lastGoodVel.Z)
                        end
                    end)
                end

                -- 2) GROS lagback (> 22 studs): micro-lerp vers prediction (1 seule fois)
                if wasMoving and backDist > math.max(sens * 1.6, 22) and delta > 18 and (now - lastFixT) > 0.2 then
                    lastFixT = now
                    recoverPos = predicted
                    if lastGoodPos then
                        recoverPos = Vector3.new(predicted.X, lastGoodPos.Y, predicted.Z)
                    end
                    recoverUntil = now + 0.12
                    pcall(function()
                        local blended = pos:Lerp(recoverPos, 0.45)
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(blended) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                        if lastGoodVel then
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(lastGoodVel.X, vel.Y, lastGoodVel.Z)
                        end
                    end)
                    softNotify("Anti-Lag • recovery")
                end
            end
        end

        -- update samples (ignorer si on vient d'etre teleporte de loin)
        if not lastSamplePos or (pos - lastSamplePos).Magnitude < sens * 3.5 then
            lastGoodPos = pos
            lastGoodVel = vel
            lastGoodCF = hrp.CFrame
        end
        lastSamplePos = pos
        lastSampleT = now
    end

    local function setAntiRollback(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.antiRollback = on
        arOn = on
        _G.ZenoAntiRollback = on
        saveConfig()
        if not on then
            if arConn then pcall(function() arConn:Disconnect() end) arConn = nil end
            return
        end
        if not arConn then
            local last = 0
            arConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 0.05 then return end
                last = now
                pcall(tickAntiRollback)
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetAntiRollback = setAntiRollback
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if Config.antiRollback ~= false then
            setAntiRollback(true)
        end
    end)
end



local function findStealPrompt(pet)
    if pet.plot and pet.slot then
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pet.plot)
        local pods = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        local pod = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.slot))
        if pod then
            local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            local att = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
            if att then for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return p end end end
            for _, d in ipairs(pod:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return d end end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
    if InternalStealCache[prompt] then return end
    local data = {holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true}
    local function grab(sig, into)
        local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, sig)
        if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(into, c.Function) end end
        end
    end
    grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan, data.holdCallbacks)
    grab(prompt.Triggered, data.triggerCallbacks)
    grab(prompt.PromptButtonHoldEnded, data.holdEndCallbacks)
    if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 or #data.triggerCallbacks > 0 or #data.holdEndCallbacks > 0 then
        InternalStealCache[prompt] = data
    end
end
local function executeStealAsync(prompt)
    local data = InternalStealCache[prompt]
    if not data or not data.ready then return false end
    data.ready = false
    task.spawn(function()
        for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
        pcall(function()
            local st = prompt:GetAttribute("State")
            if st ~= nil and st ~= "Steal" then
                if not _G._xenStealRemote then
                    _G._xenStealRemote = _G.Net.RemoteEvent(nil, "f40f7d9e-2f0d-4167-b250-899273f46874")
                end
                local r = _G._xenStealRemote
                if r then
                    local t = Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + 124
                    r:FireServer(t, "68c86eb7-eb7e-4b4d-96ae-cf7cd847c5b0")
                    r:FireServer(t, "07b9cc25-2a1f-4a26-a0ec-f2fab578d8bd")
                end
            end
        end)
        task.wait(getStealHold())
        if prompt and prompt.Parent then for _, fn in ipairs(data.triggerCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end end
        for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdEndCallbacks) do task.spawn(fn) end
        task.wait(0.05)
        data.ready = true
    end)
    return true
end
local function autoStealLoop()
    local lastScan = 0
    while true do
        task.wait(0.12)
        if not Config.autoSteal then continue end
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then continue end
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            local st = hum:GetState()
            if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then continue end
        end
        -- refresh pet cache periodically for better TP/steal targets
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastScan > 1.2 then
            lastScan = now
            pcall(scanAllPets)
        end
        local target = getTargetPetData()
        if not target then continue end
        local prompt = findStealPrompt(target)
        if prompt and prompt.Parent then
            local pp = prompt.Parent
            local ppPos = (pp:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Position)
                or (pp:IsA("Attachment") and pp.Parent and pp.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Parent.Position)
                or (pp.Parent and pp.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and pp.Parent.Position)
            local range = getStealRange()
            if Config.stealNearest == true or Config.targetMode == "nearest" or Config.stealMode == "nearest" then
                range = math.max(range, 90)
            end
            if ppPos and (hrp.Position - ppPos).Magnitude <= range then
                buildStealCallbacks(prompt)
                executeStealAsync(prompt)
            end
        end
    end
end


local invis_animPlaying = false
local invis_tracks = {}
local invis_clonePart = nil
local invis_oldRootPart = nil
local invis_hipHeight = nil
local invis_connection = nil
local invis_folderConnections = {}
local invis_serverGhosts = {}
local invis_ghostEnabled = true
local invis_lagbackCallCount = 0
local invis_lagbackWindowStart = 0
local invis_lastLagbackTime = 0
local invis_errorOrbActive = false
local invis_recoveryInProgress = false
local invis_antiDieConn = nil
local invis_noCollisionConn = nil

local function invis_clearErrorOrb() invis_errorOrbActive = false end
local function invis_createErrorOrb()
    if invis_errorOrbActive then return end
    invis_errorOrbActive = true
    for _, g in pairs(invis_serverGhosts) do if g and g.Parent then g:Destroy() end end
    invis_serverGhosts = {}
end
local function invis_createServerGhost(pos)
    if not invis_ghostEnabled or invis_errorOrbActive then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - invis_lastLagbackTime < 0.05 then return end
    invis_lastLagbackTime = now
    if now - invis_lagbackWindowStart > 1 then
        invis_lagbackCallCount = 0
        invis_lagbackWindowStart = now
    end
    invis_lagbackCallCount = invis_lagbackCallCount + 1
    if invis_lagbackCallCount >= 5 then invis_createErrorOrb(); return end
    for _, g in pairs(invis_serverGhosts) do if g and g.Parent then g:Destroy() end end
    invis_serverGhosts = {}
    local ghost = Instance.new("Part")
    ghost.Name = "LagbackGhost"
    ghost.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    ghost.Size = Vector3.new(3, 3, 3)
    ghost.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    ghost.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
    ghost.Transparency = 0.3
    ghost.CanCollide = false
    ghost.Anchored = true
    ghost.CastShadow = false
    ghost.Position = pos + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    ghost.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    table.insert(invis_serverGhosts, ghost)
end
local function invis_clearAllGhosts()
    for _, g in pairs(invis_serverGhosts) do pcall(function() if g and g.Parent then g:Destroy() end end) end
    invis_serverGhosts = {}
    invis_clearErrorOrb()
    invis_lagbackCallCount = 0
    invis_lastLagbackTime = 0
    pcall(function()
        if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
            for _, c in pairs(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
                if c.Name == "LagbackGhost" then c:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)
end
local function invis_removeFolders()
    local pf = Workspace:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
    if not pf then return end
    local dr = pf:FindFirstChild("DoubleRig")
    if dr then
        local rr = dr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or dr:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        if rr and invis_ghostEnabled then invis_createServerGhost(rr.Position) end
        dr:Destroy()
    end
    local cs = pf:FindFirstChild("Constraints")
    if cs then cs:Destroy() end
    local conn = pf.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "DoubleRig" then
            task.defer(function()
                local rr = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if rr and invis_ghostEnabled then invis_createServerGhost(rr.Position) end
                child:Destroy()
            end)
        elseif child.Name == "Constraints" then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end)
    table.insert(invis_folderConnections, conn)
end

-- FIXED CLONE : plus de tmp.Parent = game (patch anti-detect Roblox 2025 bloque ça)
local function invis_doClone()
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    invis_hipHeight = hum.HipHeight
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return false end
    -- clean beams
    for _, v in pairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
        if v:IsA("Beam") or (v:IsA("Attachment") and v.Name:lower():find("beam")) then
            pcall(function() v:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    invis_oldRootPart = hrp
    -- clone BEFORE moving
    local success, clone = pcall(function() return hrp:Clone() end)
    if not success or not clone then return false end
    invis_clonePart = clone
    invis_clonePart.Name = "HumanoidRootPart"
    invis_clonePart.Parent = char
    invis_clonePart.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
    -- move real to camera - c'est ça qui rend invisible serveur
    pcall(function() hrp.Parent = Workspace.CurrentCamera end)
    if hrp.Parent ~= Workspace.CurrentCamera then
        -- fallback si Camera bloque, essaye workspace
        hrp.Parent = Workspace
    end
    char.PrimaryPart = invis_clonePart
    -- rewire Motor6D/Welds
    for _, v in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("Motor6D") or v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
            if v.Part0 == invis_oldRootPart then v.Part0 = invis_clonePart end
            if v.Part1 == invis_oldRootPart then v.Part1 = invis_clonePart end
        end
    end
    return true
end

local function invis_revertClone()
    local c = player.Character
    if not invis_oldRootPart then return end
    if not c then return end
    -- si oldRoot a été détruit, abandon
    if not invis_oldRootPart.Parent then
        -- essaie de retrouver dans CurrentCamera
        local found = nil
        pcall(function()
            for _, x in pairs(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
                if x.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and x ~= invis_clonePart then found = x end
            end
        end)
        if found then invis_oldRootPart = found end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local fake = invis_clonePart
        local real = invis_oldRootPart
        if not real then return end
        -- remettre le vrai
        if fake and fake.Parent then
            real.CFrame = fake.CFrame
        end
        real.Parent = c
        c.PrimaryPart = real
        real.CanCollide = true
        -- rewire inverse
        for _, v in pairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Motor6D") or v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                if v.Part0 == fake then v.Part0 = real end
                if v.Part1 == fake then v.Part1 = real end
            end
        end
        if fake then fake:Destroy() end
    end)
    invis_clonePart = nil
    invis_oldRootPart = nil
    if c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and invis_hipHeight then
        c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").HipHeight = invis_hipHeight
    end
    invis_clearAllGhosts()
end

local function invis_animationTrickery()
    local c = player.Character
    if not (c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then return end
    if c.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
    -- ID FIX : ancien 18537363391 delete, on utilise 180426354 (R15 glitch) + fallback
    local animIds = {"rbxassetid://180426354", "rbxassetid://18537363391", "rbxassetid://113940239174530"}
    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim.AnimationId = animIds[1]
    local hum = c.Humanoid
    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", hum)
    local ok, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(anim) end)
    if not ok or not track then
        anim.AnimationId = animIds[3]
        ok, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(anim) end)
    end
    if ok and track then
        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
        pcall(function() track:Play(0, 1, 0) end)
        table.insert(invis_tracks, track)
        track.Stopped:Connect(function()
            if invis_animPlaying then invis_animationTrickery() end
        end)
        task.delay(0, function()
            pcall(function() track.TimePosition = 0.7 end)
            task.delay(0.2, function() if track then pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(100) end) end end)
        end)
    end
    anim:Destroy()
end

local invis_turnOff, invis_turnOn
invis_turnOff = function()
    invis_clearAllGhosts()
    if not invis_animPlaying then
        -- meme si pas playing, nettoie orphelin
        pcall(invis_revertClone)
        return
    end
    local c = player.Character
    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    invis_animPlaying = false
    for _, t in ipairs(invis_tracks) do pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end) end
    invis_tracks = {}
    if invis_connection then invis_connection:Disconnect(); invis_connection = nil end
    for _, cn in ipairs(invis_folderConnections) do if cn then cn:Disconnect() end end
    invis_folderConnections = {}
    if invis_antiDieConn then invis_antiDieConn:Disconnect(); invis_antiDieConn = nil end
    if invis_noCollisionConn then invis_noCollisionConn:Disconnect(); invis_noCollisionConn = nil end
    invis_revertClone()
    invis_clearAllGhosts()
    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            local anim = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if anim then
                for _, t in ipairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if t.Priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 then t:Stop(0) end
                end
            end
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            task.defer(function() if hum and hum.Parent then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end end)
        end)
    end
end
invis_turnOn = function()
    if invis_animPlaying then return end
    local c = player.Character
    if not c then return end
    local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end
    invis_animPlaying = true
    invis_tracks = {}
    invis_removeFolders()
    if invis_doClone() then
        task.wait(0.08)
        invis_animationTrickery()
        local lastSetPos = nil
        local skipFrames = 6
        -- FIX : PreSimulation -> Heartbeat (plus stable depuis maj Roblox)
        invis_connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if c and c:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and c.Humanoid.Health > 0 and invis_oldRootPart then
                local root = c.PrimaryPart or c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    if skipFrames > 0 then skipFrames = skipFrames - 1; lastSetPos = nil
                    elseif lastSetPos and invis_ghostEnabled then
                        local curPos = invis_oldRootPart.Position
                        if (curPos - lastSetPos).Magnitude > 6 and not invis_recoveryInProgress and player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                            lastSetPos = nil
                            invis_createServerGhost(curPos)
                            if Config.invisAutoRecover ~= false then
                                invis_recoveryInProgress = true
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    pcall(invis_turnOff)
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") and Config.invisOnSteal then pcall(invis_turnOn) end
                                    invis_recoveryInProgress = false
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if invis_clonePart then invis_clonePart.CanCollide = true end
                    if invis_oldRootPart and invis_oldRootPart.Parent then
                        for _, v in pairs(invis_oldRootPart:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("Attachment") or v:IsA("Beam") then v:Destroy() end end
                        local sink = (Config.invisDepth or 4.2) * 0.5
                        invis_oldRootPart.CFrame = (root.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, sink, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(Config.invisRotation or 180), 0, 0)
                        invis_oldRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                        invis_oldRootPart.CanCollide = false
                        lastSetPos = invis_oldRootPart.Position
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        invis_antiDieConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            if hum.Health <= 0 then hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth; hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
        end)
        invis_noCollisionConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if not invis_animPlaying then return end
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= player and p.Character then
                    for _, part in pairs(p.Character:GetDescendants()) do if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        invis_animPlaying = false
        warn("[ZENO][INVIS] doClone failed")
    end
end

local currentTargetFloorY = 0

-- Global API (JEW/Rose compatible) + portage ROSE : anti-orphelin camera + smart revert
local function invis_orphanInCamera()
    local found = nil
    pcall(function()
        if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
            for _, c in ipairs(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("BasePart") and c.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then found = c; break end
            end
        end
    end)
    return found
end

local function invis_smartRevert()
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return false end
    local orphans = {}
    pcall(function()
        if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
            for _, c in ipairs(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("BasePart") and c.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then table.insert(orphans, c) end
            end
        end
    end)
    if #orphans == 0 then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local fakeHRP = character.PrimaryPart
        if not fakeHRP or fakeHRP.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then fakeHRP = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end
        local realHRP = orphans[1]
        realHRP.Parent = character
        character.PrimaryPart = realHRP
        realHRP.CanCollide = true
        if fakeHRP and fakeHRP ~= realHRP then
            for _, v in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("Motor6D") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                    if v.Part0 == fakeHRP then v.Part0 = realHRP end
                    if v.Part1 == fakeHRP then v.Part1 = realHRP end
                end
            end
            local p = fakeHRP.CFrame
            fakeHRP:Destroy()
            realHRP.CFrame = p
        end
        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if camera then camera.CameraSubject = hum; camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end
            end)
        end
    end)
    return ok
end

_G.toggleInvisibleSteal = function()
    local invisLooksOn = invis_animPlaying
        or _G.invisibleStealEnabled
        or (invis_oldRootPart and invis_oldRootPart.Parent ~= nil)
        or (invis_clonePart and invis_clonePart.Parent ~= nil)
        or (invis_orphanInCamera() ~= nil)
    if invisLooksOn then
        pcall(invis_turnOff)
        if invis_orphanInCamera() then pcall(invis_smartRevert) end
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            if invis_orphanInCamera() then
                pcall(function() player:LoadCharacter() end)
            end
        end)
    else
        pcall(invis_turnOn)
    end
    _G.invisibleStealEnabled = invis_animPlaying == true
    return invis_animPlaying
end

 task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    if invis_orphanInCamera() then pcall(invis_smartRevert) end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        _G.invisibleStealEnabled = invis_animPlaying == true
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

-- Portage ROSE v2 : garde "pas d'invis auto sur sa propre base"
-- Mecanisme remplace : au lieu de PlotSign.YourBase + StealHitbox (souvent absents
-- ou desactives selon l'etat de la partie), on reutilise le detecteur natif de Zeno
-- (isOwnPlot via canal synchronis

-- Auto invis during steal (JEW-style: delay + heartbeat watchdog + stuck recovery)
do
    local invisAutoActive = false
    local notStealTicks = 0
    local lastStealing = false
    local invisDelaySeq = 0

    local function isStealingNow()
        if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        local c = player.Character
        if c and c:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function startInvisWithDelay()
        if not Config.invisOnSteal then return end
        -- Portage ROSE v2 : jamais d'invis auto sur sa propre base (avec log console)
        if _G.ZenoOnOwnPlotSteal and _G.ZenoOnOwnPlotSteal() then
            warn("[ZENO][INVIS] auto invis bloque : vol sur ta propre base")
            return
        end
        if invis_animPlaying or invis_recoveryInProgress then return end
        invisAutoActive = true
        local delayMs = tonumber(Config.invisDelayMs) or 0
        if delayMs > 0 then
            invisDelaySeq = invisDelaySeq + 1
            local seq = invisDelaySeq
            task.delay(delayMs / 1000, function()
                if seq ~= invisDelaySeq then return end
                if Config.invisOnSteal and isStealingNow() and not invis_animPlaying then
                    if currentTargetFloorY > 23.15 then
                        task.delay(0.25, function()
                            if Config.invisOnSteal and isStealingNow() and not invis_animPlaying then
                                pcall(invis_turnOn)
                            end
                        end)
                    else
                        pcall(invis_turnOn)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if currentTargetFloorY > 23.15 then
                task.delay(0.25, function()
                    if Config.invisOnSteal and isStealingNow() and not invis_animPlaying then
                        pcall(invis_turnOn)
                    end
                end)
            else
                pcall(invis_turnOn)
            end
        end
    end

    local function stopInvisAuto()
        invisDelaySeq = invisDelaySeq + 1
        if invis_animPlaying and invisAutoActive then
            pcall(invis_turnOff)
        end
        invisAutoActive = false
        notStealTicks = 0
    end

    player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if not Config.invisOnSteal then return end
        local stealing = isStealingNow()
        if stealing and not lastStealing then
            startInvisWithDelay()
        elseif (not stealing) and lastStealing then
            -- end of steal: turn off after short grace
            task.delay(0.2, function()
                if not isStealingNow() then stopInvisAuto() end
            end)
        end
        lastStealing = stealing
    end)

    -- Heartbeat watchdog (JEW-style stuck recovery)
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Config.invisOnSteal then
            if invis_animPlaying and invisAutoActive then stopInvisAuto() end
            return
        end
        local stealing = isStealingNow()
        if stealing and not invis_animPlaying and not invis_recoveryInProgress and invisAutoActive then
            -- re-engage if dropped mid-steal
            startInvisWithDelay()
        end
        if (not stealing) and invis_animPlaying then
            notStealTicks = notStealTicks + 1
            if (notStealTicks >= 4 and not invis_recoveryInProgress) or notStealTicks >= 20 then
                pcall(invis_turnOff)
                invisAutoActive = false
                notStealTicks = 0
            end
        else
            notStealTicks = 0
        end
        lastStealing = stealing
    end)
end

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    invis_clearErrorOrb()
    invis_clearAllGhosts()
    invis_lagbackCallCount = 0
    pcall(function()
        for _, c in pairs(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("BasePart") and c.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then c:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    if invis_oldRootPart then pcall(function() invis_oldRootPart:Destroy() end); invis_oldRootPart = nil end
    if invis_clonePart   then pcall(function() invis_clonePart:Destroy() end); invis_clonePart = nil end
    invis_animPlaying = false
    _G.invisibleStealEnabled = false
end)

local antiRagConn
local function startAntiRagdoll()
    if antiRagConn then return end
    antiRagConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hum then
            local st = hum:GetState()
            local isRag = st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            if isRag then
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                pcall(function()
                    local pm = player.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                    if pm then require(pm:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")):Enable() end
                end)
                if root then root.Velocity = Vector3.zero; root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end
            end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and not obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = true end
        end
    end)
end
startAntiRagdoll()

local function startPermanentDeathShield()
    local function shieldCharacter(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        local maxHp = hum.MaxHealth
        hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
        local conn1 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if hum and hum.Parent then
                if hum.Health < maxHp then hum.Health = maxHp end
                local st = hum:GetState()
                if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                end
            end
        end)
        local conn2 = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            if hum.Health <= 0 then hum.Health = maxHp end
        end)
        char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent then conn1:Disconnect(); conn2:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end
    shieldCharacter(player.Character)
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(shieldCharacter)
end
startPermanentDeathShield()

local tpDeathConnection = nil
local function resetTPStateOnDeath()
    if tpDeathConnection then tpDeathConnection:Disconnect() end
    tpDeathConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if isTeleporting then
            isTeleporting = false
            autoBackActive = false
            lastTargetData = nil
            setStatus("Died – TP aborted")
            for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if obj.Name == "NavTmpPart" then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
    end)
end
resetTPStateOnDeath()

local MOVE_ACTIONS = {
    "MoveForward", "MoveBackward", "MoveLeft", "MoveRight",
    "Thumbstick1", "DpadUp", "DpadDown", "DpadLeft", "DpadRight"
}
local function lockMovement()
    for _, action in ipairs(MOVE_ACTIONS) do
        ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(action, function() return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end, false, 200)
    end
    if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        player.Character.Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
    end
end
local function unlockMovement()
    for _, action in ipairs(MOVE_ACTIONS) do
        pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindAction(action) end)
    end
    if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        player.Character.Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
    end
end

local function getTargetHeight(petY)
    if petY > 23.15 then return 21
    elseif petY >= 11 then return 14.5
    elseif petY <= 8.9 then return -4
    else return petY end
end

local PLOT_SQUARE_SIZE_FIRST  = 26
local PLOT_SQUARE_SIZE_SECOND = 25

local function getPlotCenter(plotName)
    local pf = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not pf then return nil end
    local plot = pf:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then return nil end
    if plot:IsA("Model") and plot.PrimaryPart then return plot.PrimaryPart.Position
    elseif plot:IsA("BasePart") then return plot.Position end
    return nil
end

local function isInsidePlotSquare(pos, plotName, squareSize)
    local c = getPlotCenter(plotName)
    if not c then return false end
    return math.abs(pos.X - c.X) <= squareSize and math.abs(pos.Z - c.Z) <= squareSize
end

local UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD = 7
local TALL_PETS    = {["La Secret Combinasion"] = true, ["La Jolly Grande"] = true}
local TALL_OFFSET  = 3
local COLUMN_SPLIT_X = -410
local FRONT_Z_CLAMP  = 18
local SIDE_NEAR_Z    = 45

local UPPER = {
    B = {{coord = Vector3.new(-487.921448, 16.850713, -75.768013), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-332.379730, 16.850722, -75.762100), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-487.134918, 16.850713, -18.094154), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-316.300171, 16.850713, -17.845898), facing = "SOUTH"}},
    C = {{coord = Vector3.new(-330.765381, 16.850713, 31.424425), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-502.989349, 16.850713, 31.172430), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-489.077087, 16.850713, 89.010147), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-330.908936, 16.850713, 88.930145), facing = "SOUTH"}},
    D = {{coord = Vector3.new(-331.264893, 16.850713, 138.209167), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-487.935181, 16.850713, 138.026321), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-487.774933, 16.850713, 195.882538), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-330.799133, 16.850575, 196.022354), facing = "SOUTH"}},
}
local LOWER = {
    B = {{coord = Vector3.new(-335.725586, -3.048217, -74.984589), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-503.214233, -3.048217, -75.043137), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-483.619385, -3.718430, -18.844337), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-316.147095, -3.048218, -18.818844), facing = "SOUTH"}},
    C = {{coord = Vector3.new(-335.985413, -3.048218, 32.051426), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-503.277008, -3.048217, 31.956175), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-483.749390, -3.048218, 88.147003), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-315.793823, -3.048217, 88.163979), facing = "SOUTH"}},
    D = {{coord = Vector3.new(-335.476654, -3.048218, 139.001083), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-503.710083, -3.048218, 138.989883), facing = "NORTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-315.654938, -3.048218, 195.302444), facing = "SOUTH"}, {coord = Vector3.new(-483.859253, -3.048218, 195.269043), facing = "SOUTH"}},
}
local BASES_LOW  = {[1] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 220.94), [2] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 113.77), [3] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, 6.18), [4] = Vector3.new(-476.52, -2, -101.07), [5] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 221.45), [6] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 113.41), [7] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, 6.25), [8] = Vector3.new(-342.66, -2, -99.73)}
local BASES_HIGH = {[1] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 220.94), [2] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 113.77), [3] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, 6.18), [4] = Vector3.new(-479.51, 18, -101.07), [5] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 221.45), [6] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 113.41), [7] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, 6.25), [8] = Vector3.new(-339.48, 18, -99.73)}
local FRONT_Y_HIGH = 17; local FRONT_Y_LOW = -2

local function getClosestBaseIdx(pos)
    local closest, dist = 1, math.huge
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local b = BASES_LOW[i]
        local d = (pos.X - b.X)^2 + (pos.Z - b.Z)^2
        if d < dist then dist = d; closest = i end
    end
    return closest
end
local function buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, playerZ)
    local base   = isUpper and BASES_HIGH[idx] or BASES_LOW[idx]
    local frontY = isUpper and FRONT_Y_HIGH or FRONT_Y_LOW
    local frontZ = math.clamp(playerZ - base.Z, -FRONT_Z_CLAMP, FRONT_Z_CLAMP) + base.Z
    local faceDir = (idx <= 4) and Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    return Vector3.new(base.X, frontY, frontZ), faceDir
end
local function plotSides(coordTable, idx)
    local base = BASES_LOW[idx]
    local isWest = idx <= 4
    local out = {}
    for _, coords in pairs(coordTable) do
        for _, data in ipairs(coords) do
            if ((data.coord.X < COLUMN_SPLIT_X) == isWest) and math.abs(data.coord.Z - base.Z) < SIDE_NEAR_Z then
                out[#out + 1] = {coord = data.coord, facing = data.facing}
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

local function chooseStableApproachTarget(hrpPos, petPos, sideApproach, idx)
    local baseTable = (petPos.Y > 23.15) and UPPER or LOWER
    local baseIdx = idx or getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
    local frontCoord, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(baseIdx, baseTable == UPPER, hrpPos.Z)
    local sideCandidates = plotSides(baseTable, baseIdx)

    local function scorePosition(pos, label)
        local delta = hrpPos - pos
        local dist = delta.Magnitude
        local zBias = label == "side" and (math.abs(delta.Z) * 0.9) or 0
        local xBias = math.abs(delta.X) > 14 and 4 or 0
        local zClamp = math.abs(delta.Z) > 14 and 4 or 0
        return dist + zBias + xBias + zClamp
    end

    if sideApproach == "front" then
        return frontCoord, frontFace
    end

    if sideApproach == "side" then
        local bestDest, bestFace, bestScore = frontCoord, frontFace, math.huge
        for _, d in ipairs(sideCandidates) do
            local pos = d.coord
            local face = (d.facing == "NORTH") and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
            local score = scorePosition(pos, "side")
            if score < bestScore then
                bestScore = score
                bestDest = pos
                bestFace = face
            end
        end
        return bestDest, bestFace
    end

    local bestDest, bestFace, bestScore = frontCoord, frontFace, scorePosition(frontCoord, "front")
    for _, d in ipairs(sideCandidates) do
        local pos = d.coord
        local face = (d.facing == "NORTH") and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        local score = scorePosition(pos, "side")
        if score < bestScore then
            bestScore = score
            bestDest = pos
            bestFace = face
        end
    end
    return bestDest, bestFace
end

_G._isTargetPlotUnlocked = function(plotName)
    local ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plotName)
    if not ch then return false end
    local blockEnd = _G.sProp(ch, "BlockEndTimeFirstFloor")
    return blockEnd == nil
end

local function spawnFollowPlatform(hrp, hum, targetY)
    local hipH = (hum and hum.HipHeight) or 2.3
    local platY = targetY ~= nil and targetY or (hrp.Position.Y - hipH - 0.4)

    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    plat.Name = "FollowPlat"
    plat.Size = Vector3.new(4, 0.55, 4)
    plat.Anchored = true
    plat.CanCollide = true
    plat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    plat.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 120, 220)
    plat.Transparency = 1
    plat.CastShadow = false
    plat.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, platY, hrp.Position.Z)
    plat.Parent = Workspace

    local active = true
    local followConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not active or not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not plat or not plat.Parent then return end
        plat.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, platY, hrp.Position.Z)
    end)

    local owConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not active or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
        plat.CanCollide = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y <= 0.5 and hrp.Position.Y > platY
    end)

    local stealConn
    local function destroy()
        active = false
        followConn:Disconnect()
        owConn:Disconnect()
        if stealConn then stealConn:Disconnect() end
        if plat and plat.Parent then plat:Destroy() end
    end

    stealConn = player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then destroy() end
    end)

    return plat, destroy
end

local autoBackActive = false
local autoBackCoroutine = nil

local function startAutoBackBrainrot(data)
    if autoBackCoroutine then autoBackActive = false; autoBackCoroutine = nil end
    autoBackActive = true
    autoBackCoroutine = task.spawn(function()
        local char = player.Character; local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then autoBackActive = false; return end
        local petPos = data.petPos; local plotName = data.plotName; local squareSize = data.squareSize; local targetY = data.targetY

        local function ensureUnlock()
            unlockMovement()
            autoBackActive = false
        end

        while autoBackActive do
            while autoBackActive and not player:GetAttribute("Stealing") do
                if not isInsidePlotSquare(hrp.Position, plotName, squareSize) then
                    setStatus("Left plot – auto-back aborted")
                    ensureUnlock(); return
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            while autoBackActive and player:GetAttribute("Stealing") do
                if not isInsidePlotSquare(hrp.Position, plotName, squareSize) then
                    setStatus("Left plot – auto-back aborted")
                    ensureUnlock(); return
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            if not isInsidePlotSquare(hrp.Position, plotName, squareSize) then
                setStatus("Left plot – auto-back aborted")
                ensureUnlock(); return
            end

            setStatus("Auto-back to pet...")
            local dest = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)

            local followPlat = nil
            local function destroyPlat()
                if followPlat and followPlat.Parent then followPlat:Destroy() end
            end
            local isThird = petPos.Y > 23.15
            if isThird then
                followPlat = Instance.new("Part")
                followPlat.Name = "FollowPlat"
                followPlat.Size = Vector3.new(5, 0.5, 5)
                followPlat.Anchored = true
                followPlat.CanCollide = true
                followPlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                followPlat.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 120, 220)
                followPlat.Transparency = 0.5
                followPlat.CastShadow = false
                followPlat.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, dest.Y - 0.5, hrp.Position.Z)
                followPlat.Parent = Workspace
                task.delay(5, destroyPlat)
            end

            lockMovement()
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            unlockMovement()

            if followPlat then task.delay(30, destroyPlat) end
        end
        ensureUnlock()
    end)
end

local CHARACTER_RADIUS      = 4
local SAFE_RADIUS           = 3.1
local HARD_MIN_CLEARANCE    = 1.35
local LOOKAHEAD_DISTANCE    = 80
local MAX_PATH_ITERATIONS   = 30
local SIDE_DETOUR_DISTANCE  = 25
local MAX_VERTICAL_EFFORT   = 50
local SPLINE_SUBDIVISIONS   = 50
local WAYPOINT_ARRIVE_DIST  = 4
local VEL_BLEND             = 1.0
local REPLAN_STUCK_TIME     = 1.2

local SUPPORTS_SPHERECAST = false
pcall(function()
    local p = RaycastParams.new()
    Workspace:Spherecast(Vector3.new(0, 1e6, 0), 1, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), p)
    SUPPORTS_SPHERECAST = true
end)

local function buildParams(ignoreList)
    local filter = {}
    if player.Character then table.insert(filter, player.Character) end
    for _, nm in ipairs({"NavTmpPart", "FollowPlat", "AnchorPlat"}) do
        local f = Workspace:FindFirstChild(nm)
        if f then table.insert(filter, f) end
    end
    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if pl ~= player and pl.Character then table.insert(filter, pl.Character) end
    end
    if ignoreList then
        for _, v in ipairs(ignoreList) do
            if typeof(v) == "Instance" then table.insert(filter, v) end
        end
    end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    params.RespectCanCollide = true
    return params
end

local function basisOf(dir)
    local d = dir.Unit
    local up = (math.abs(d.Y) > 0.95) and Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) or Vector3.yAxis
    local right = d:Cross(up)
    if right.Magnitude < 1e-4 then right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) else right = right.Unit end
    local realUp = right:Cross(d).Unit
    return right, realUp
end

local function castVolume(origin, dir, dist, radius, params)
    if dist <= 0 then return nil end
    local d = dir
    if d.Magnitude < 1e-5 then return nil end
    d = d.Unit
    radius = radius or SAFE_RADIUS
    if SUPPORTS_SPHERECAST then
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            return Workspace:Spherecast(origin, radius, d * dist, params)
        end)
        if ok then return res end
    end
    local right, up = basisOf(d)
    local diag = 0.7071 * radius
    local offs = {
        Vector3.zero,
        right * radius, -right * radius, up * radius, -up * radius,
        (right + up) * diag, (right - up) * diag, (-right + up) * diag, (-right - up) * diag,
    }
    local best
    for _, o in ipairs(offs) do
        local r = Workspace:Raycast(origin + o, d * dist, params)
        if r and (not best or r.Distance < best.Distance) then best = r end
    end
    return best
end

local function isSegmentClear(a, b, params, radius)
    local delta = b - a
    local dist = delta.Magnitude
    if dist < 1e-3 then return true end
    return castVolume(a, delta, dist, radius or SAFE_RADIUS, params) == nil
end

local function clearanceAt(pt, params)
    local dirs = {
        Vector3.new(1,0,0), Vector3.new(-1,0,0), Vector3.new(0,0,1), Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
        Vector3.new(0,1,0), Vector3.new(0,-1,0),
    }
    local minD = 30
    for _, d in ipairs(dirs) do
        local r = Workspace:Raycast(pt, d * 30, params)
        if r and r.Distance < minD then minD = r.Distance end
    end
    return minD
end

local function nudgeToOpenSpace(pt, params)
    local probe = {
        Vector3.new(1,0,0), Vector3.new(-1,0,0), Vector3.new(0,0,1), Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
        Vector3.new(0,1,0), Vector3.new(0,-1,0),
    }
    local push = Vector3.zero
    for _, d in ipairs(probe) do
        local r = Workspace:Raycast(pt, d * (SAFE_RADIUS + 2), params)
        if r then
            local pen = (SAFE_RADIUS + 2) - r.Distance
            push = push - d * pen
        end
    end
    if push.Magnitude > 0.05 then
        return pt + push
    end
    return pt
end

local function planPath(startPos, destination, ignoreList)
    local params = buildParams(ignoreList)
    local waypoints = { startPos }
    local current = startPos
    local iter = 0
    local visitedPenalty = {}

    while iter < MAX_PATH_ITERATIONS do
        local toDest = destination - current
        local dist = toDest.Magnitude
        if dist < WAYPOINT_ARRIVE_DIST + 1 then
            table.insert(waypoints, destination)
            break
        end
