        local dir = toDest.Unit
        local lookOrigin = current + Vector3.new(0, CHARACTER_RADIUS * 0.25, 0)
        local scanLen = math.min(dist, LOOKAHEAD_DISTANCE)
        local hit = castVolume(lookOrigin, dir, scanLen, SAFE_RADIUS, params)

        if not hit then
            if scanLen >= dist then
                table.insert(waypoints, destination)
                break
            else
                local step = current + dir * (scanLen - 2)
                table.insert(waypoints, step)
                current = step
                iter = iter + 1
            end
        else
            local obstaclePos = hit.Position
            local inst = hit.Instance
            local obstacleTop = obstaclePos.Y + 4
            pcall(function()
                if inst and inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                    obstacleTop = inst.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, inst.Size.Y / 2, 0)).Y
                end
            end)
            local clearanceY   = obstacleTop + CHARACTER_RADIUS + 2
            local ascentNeeded = clearanceY - current.Y
            local canGoOver    = ascentNeeded <= MAX_VERTICAL_EFFORT

            local rightDir = (CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, math.rad(90)) * dir)
            rightDir = Vector3.new(rightDir.X, 0, rightDir.Z)
            if rightDir.Magnitude < 1e-3 then rightDir = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            rightDir = rightDir.Unit
            local leftDir = -rightDir

            local candidates = {}
            local function consider(pt, extraCost)
                pt = nudgeToOpenSpace(pt, params)
                if not isSegmentClear(current, pt, params) then return end
                local key = string.format("%d_%d_%d", math.floor(pt.X/4), math.floor(pt.Y/4), math.floor(pt.Z/4))
                local pen = visitedPenalty[key] and 400 or 0
                local openness = clearanceAt(pt, params)
                local cost = (pt - destination).Magnitude + (extraCost or 0) + pen + math.max(0, (12 - openness)) * 3
                table.insert(candidates, { pt = pt, cost = cost, key = key })
            end

            if canGoOver then
                consider(Vector3.new(obstaclePos.X, clearanceY, obstaclePos.Z) + dir * 6, ascentNeeded * 0.5)
            end
            for _, mult in ipairs({ 0.6, 1.0, 1.6 }) do
                for _, sideDir in ipairs({ rightDir, leftDir }) do
                    consider(current + sideDir * (SIDE_DETOUR_DISTANCE * mult) + dir * 8, SIDE_DETOUR_DISTANCE * mult * 0.25)
                end
            end
            if canGoOver then
                for _, sideDir in ipairs({ rightDir, leftDir }) do
                    consider(current + sideDir * SIDE_DETOUR_DISTANCE + Vector3.new(0, ascentNeeded, 0) + dir * 8,
                             ascentNeeded * 0.5 + SIDE_DETOUR_DISTANCE * 0.5)
                end
            end
            consider(current + Vector3.new(0, -14, 0) + dir * 8, 30)
            consider(current + Vector3.new(0, 26, 0), 45)

            if #candidates == 0 then
                local up = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                local upHit = castVolume(current, up, 30, SAFE_RADIUS, params)
                local rise = upHit and math.max(0, upHit.Distance - SAFE_RADIUS - 1) or 30
                if rise < 2 then
                    local backHit = castVolume(current, -dir, 20, SAFE_RADIUS, params)
                    local back = backHit and math.max(0, backHit.Distance - SAFE_RADIUS - 1) or 20
                    if back < 2 then break end
                    current = current - dir * back
                else
                    current = current + up * rise
                end
                table.insert(waypoints, current)
            else
                table.sort(candidates, function(a, b) return a.cost < b.cost end)
                local best = candidates[1]
                visitedPenalty[best.key] = true
                table.insert(waypoints, best.pt)
                current = best.pt
            end

            iter = iter + 1
        end
    end

    if (waypoints[#waypoints] - destination).Magnitude > 0.01 then
        table.insert(waypoints, destination)
    end

    local pulled = { waypoints[1] }
    local i = 1
    while i < #waypoints do
        local anchor = pulled[#pulled]
        local furthest = i + 1
        for j = #waypoints, i + 1, -1 do
            if isSegmentClear(anchor, waypoints[j], params) then furthest = j break end
        end
        table.insert(pulled, waypoints[furthest])
        i = furthest
    end

    return pulled, params
end

local function catmullRom(p0, p1, p2, p3, t)
    local t2, t3 = t * t, t * t * t
    return 0.5 * (
        (2 * p1) +
        (-p0 + p2) * t +
        (2 * p0 - 5 * p1 + 4 * p2 - p3) * t2 +
        (-p0 + 3 * p1 - 3 * p2 + p3) * t3
    )
end

local function smoothPath(pts, params)
    if #pts < 3 then return pts end
    local out = { pts[1] }
    for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
        local p0 = pts[math.max(i - 1, 1)]
        local p1 = pts[i]
        local p2 = pts[i + 1]
        local p3 = pts[math.min(i + 2, #pts)]
        local segment = {}
        local valid = true
        local prev = p1
        for s = 1, SPLINE_SUBDIVISIONS do
            local t = s / SPLINE_SUBDIVISIONS
            local pt = catmullRom(p0, p1, p2, p3, t)
            if not isSegmentClear(prev, pt, params) then valid = false break end
            table.insert(segment, pt)
            prev = pt
        end
        if valid then
            for _, pt in ipairs(segment) do table.insert(out, pt) end
        else
            table.insert(out, p2)
        end
    end
    if (out[#out] - pts[#pts]).Magnitude > 0.01 then table.insert(out, pts[#pts]) end
    return out
end

local FEELERS = {}
do
    for _, yaw in ipairs({ 0, 22, -22, 45, -45, 70, -70 }) do
        table.insert(FEELERS, { yaw = math.rad(yaw), pitch = 0 })
    end
    table.insert(FEELERS, { yaw = 0, pitch = math.rad(30) })
    table.insert(FEELERS, { yaw = 0, pitch = math.rad(-30) })
end

local function avoidanceVector(origin, travelDir, speed, params)
    local probeLen = math.clamp(speed * 0.10, 8, 32)
    local right, up = basisOf(travelDir)
    local steer = Vector3.zero
    local nearest = math.huge
    for _, f in ipairs(FEELERS) do
        local d = (CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, f.yaw) * travelDir)
        if f.pitch ~= 0 then d = (CFrame.fromAxisAngle(right, f.pitch) * d) end
        d = d.Unit
        local hit = castVolume(origin, d, probeLen, SAFE_RADIUS * 0.9, params)
        if hit then
            local weight = 1 - (hit.Distance / probeLen)
            steer = steer + hit.Normal * (weight * weight) * 1.2
            local tangent = hit.Normal:Cross(Vector3.yAxis)
            if tangent.Magnitude > 1e-3 then
                tangent = tangent.Unit
                if tangent:Dot(right) < 0 then tangent = -tangent end
                steer = steer + tangent * weight * 1.1
            end
            if hit.Distance < nearest then nearest = hit.Distance end
        end
    end
    return steer, nearest
end

local function predictiveObstacleFly(hrp, destination, speed, onTick, ignoreList)
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
        return
    end

    local raw, params = planPath(hrp.Position, destination, ignoreList)
    local waypoints = smoothPath(raw, params)
    local wpIndex = 2
    local stuckTimer = 0
    local lastPos = hrp.Position
    local currentVel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local flightStart = os.clock()
    local replanCooldown = 0

    while hrp and hrp.Parent do
        if onTick then pcall(onTick) end
        local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
        if os.clock() - flightStart > 45 then
            break
        end
        replanCooldown = math.max(0, replanCooldown - dt)

        local myPos = hrp.Position
        if (myPos - destination).Magnitude < 3.5 then
            break
        end

        if wpIndex > #waypoints then break end
        local targetWP = waypoints[wpIndex]

        while wpIndex < #waypoints do
            local d = (waypoints[wpIndex] - myPos).Magnitude
            if d < WAYPOINT_ARRIVE_DIST then
                wpIndex = wpIndex + 1
            else
                break
            end
        end
        targetWP = waypoints[math.min(wpIndex, #waypoints)]

        local diff = targetWP - myPos
        local dist = diff.Magnitude
        if dist < 0.05 then
            wpIndex = wpIndex + 1
            continue
        end
        local seekDir = diff.Unit

        local blended = seekDir

        local speedScale = 1
        local arriveScale = math.clamp(dist / 20, 0.5, 1)
        if wpIndex >= #waypoints then
            speedScale = arriveScale
        end

        local desiredVel = blended * (speed * speedScale)
        currentVel = desiredVel
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = desiredVel
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        local posDelta = (myPos - lastPos).Magnitude
        if posDelta < 0.5 then
            stuckTimer = stuckTimer + dt
        else
            stuckTimer = 0
            lastPos = myPos
        end
        if stuckTimer > REPLAN_STUCK_TIME then
            raw, params = planPath(myPos, destination, ignoreList)
            waypoints = smoothPath(raw, params)
            wpIndex = 2
            stuckTimer = 0
            lastPos = myPos
            local upHit = castVolume(myPos, Vector3.yAxis, 24, SAFE_RADIUS, params)
            if not upHit then currentVel = Vector3.new(0, speed * 0.6, 0) end
        end
    end
    vZero(hrp)
end

local function directFlyTo(hrp, destination, speed, onTick, ignoreList)
    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
    local params = buildParams(ignoreList)
    local currentVel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local stuckTimer = 0
    local lastPos = hrp.Position
    local t0 = os.clock()

    while hrp and hrp.Parent do
        if onTick then pcall(onTick) end
        local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then break end
        if os.clock() - t0 > 25 then break end

        local myPos = hrp.Position
        local diff = destination - myPos
        local dist = diff.Magnitude
        if dist < 2 then
            break
        end
        local dir = diff.Unit

        local arriveScale = math.clamp(dist / 12, 0.3, 1)
        local desiredVel = dir * (speed * arriveScale)
        currentVel = currentVel:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.25)

        local frameMove = currentVel * math.max(dt, 1 / 60)
        local moveLen = frameMove.Magnitude
        if moveLen > 1e-4 then
            local hit = castVolume(myPos, frameMove, moveLen + HARD_MIN_CLEARANCE, SAFE_RADIUS * 0.8, params)
            if hit then
                local allowed = math.max(0, hit.Distance - HARD_MIN_CLEARANCE)
                if allowed <= 0.05 then
                    local n = hit.Normal
                    local slide = currentVel - n * currentVel:Dot(n)
                    currentVel = (slide.Magnitude > 1) and slide.Unit * math.min(slide.Magnitude, speed * 0.5) or Vector3.zero
                else
                    local maxSpeed = allowed / math.max(dt, 1 / 60)
                    if currentVel.Magnitude > maxSpeed then currentVel = currentVel.Unit * maxSpeed end
                end
            end
        end

        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = currentVel
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        if (myPos - lastPos).Magnitude < 0.8 then
            stuckTimer = stuckTimer + dt
        else
            stuckTimer = 0
            lastPos = myPos
        end
        if stuckTimer > 2 then
            local upHit = castVolume(myPos, Vector3.yAxis, 48, SAFE_RADIUS, params)
            local rise = upHit and math.max(0, upHit.Distance - SAFE_RADIUS - 1) or 45
            if rise > 3 then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, speed, 0)
                local riseStart = myPos.Y
                while hrp and hrp.Parent do
                    if hrp.Position.Y - riseStart > math.min(45, rise) then break end
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            else
                predictiveObstacleFly(hrp, destination, speed, onTick, ignoreList)
                return
            end
            stuckTimer = 0
            lastPos = hrp.Position
        end
    end
    vZero(hrp)
end

local function disableAllAnimations(char)
    if not char then return end
    local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animate then animate.Disabled = true end
    for _, animator in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if animator:IsA("Animator") then
            pcall(function()
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    track:Stop(0)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function enableAllAnimations(char)
    if not char then return end
    local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if animate then animate.Disabled = false end
end

local isTeleporting = false
local lastTargetData = nil
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    isTeleporting = false
    autoBackActive = false
end)
-- exposed for auto re-TP on miss
_G.ZenoIsTeleporting = function() return isTeleporting end


local _lastPetScanAt = 0
local function executeTP()
    -- Allow re-press: force unlock if a previous TP got stuck
    if isTeleporting then
        isTeleporting = false
        autoBackActive = false
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    isTeleporting = true
    autoBackActive = false
    if autoBackCoroutine then autoBackCoroutine = nil end

    -- Safety watchdog: never stay locked more than 12s
    local tpToken = tostring(os.clock()) .. math.random(1, 9999)
    _G._ZenoTpToken = tpToken
    task.delay(12, function()
        if _G._ZenoTpToken == tpToken and isTeleporting then
            isTeleporting = false
            pcall(function() setStatus("TP unlock (timeout)") end)
        end
    end)

    -- Light scan only if cache is empty or stale (>0.8s) — reduces lag
    local now = os.clock()
    if #allPetsCache == 0 or (now - _lastPetScanAt) > 0.8 then
        pcall(scanAllPets)
        _lastPetScanAt = os.clock()
    end

    local char = player.Character
    if not char then
        isTeleporting = false
        setStatus("No character")
        return
    end
    local hrp  = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then isTeleporting = false; setStatus("No HRP"); return end
    if hum.Health <= 0 then setStatus("Died – aborting"); isTeleporting = false; return end

    disableAllAnimations(char)
    pcall(disableHRPAntiCheat)

    local safetyPlat = Instance.new("Part")
    safetyPlat.Name = "NavTmpPart"; safetyPlat.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8); safetyPlat.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y-5, hrp.Position.Z)
    safetyPlat.Anchored = true; safetyPlat.CanCollide = true; safetyPlat.Transparency = 1; safetyPlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; safetyPlat.Parent = Workspace
    task.delay(8, function() if safetyPlat and safetyPlat.Parent then safetyPlat:Destroy() end end)

    local cleaned = false
    local function cleanup()
        if cleaned then return end
        cleaned = true
        if safetyPlat and safetyPlat.Parent then safetyPlat:Destroy() end
        isTeleporting = false
        if char and char.Parent then enableAllAnimations(char) end
    end

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if #allPetsCache==0 then pcall(scanAllPets) end
        local target = getTargetPetData()
        if not target then setStatus("No target"); cleanup(); return end

        local petPos   = target.position; local petName  = target.name; local plotName = target.plot
        setStatus("→ " .. petName)
        currentTargetFloorY = petPos.Y

        local speed       = Config.tpMode == "grapple" and Config.grappleTweenSpeed or Config.carpetTweenSpeed
        local cloneDelay  = Config.tpMode == "grapple" and Config.grappleCloneDelay  or Config.carpetCloneDelay
        local isLowerFloor = petPos.Y <= 8.9
        local squareSz    = isLowerFloor and PLOT_SQUARE_SIZE_FIRST or PLOT_SQUARE_SIZE_SECOND
        lastTargetData = { petPos = petPos, plotName = plotName, squareSize = squareSz, uid = target.uid }

        local isFirstFloor = petPos.Y <= 8.9
        local isUnlocked = isFirstFloor and _G._isTargetPlotUnlocked(plotName)

        if isUnlocked then
            setStatus("Unlocked – direct entry")
            if Config.tpMode == "grapple" then carpetEngage() else equipCarpet() end
            vZero(hrp)

            local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
            local frontPos, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, false, hrp.Position.Z)
            local frontDest = frontPos + Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)

            local plotModel = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
            local ignoreList = {Workspace:FindFirstChild("NavTmpPart")}
            if plotModel then table.insert(ignoreList, plotModel) end

            predictiveObstacleFly(hrp, frontDest, math.max(speed * 0.85, 220), equipCarpet, ignoreList)
            if (hrp.Position - frontDest).Magnitude > 1 then
                directFlyTo(hrp, frontDest, math.max(speed * 0.7, 200), equipCarpet, ignoreList)
            end
            vZero(hrp)

            local adjY = petPos.Y
            if TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end
            local targetY = getTargetHeight(adjY)
            local dest = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)
            directFlyTo(hrp, dest, speed, equipCarpet)

            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            setStatus("Arrived – free to steal")
            startAutoBackBrainrot(lastTargetData)
            cleanup()
            return
        end

        -- === TP V2 (port HAZE) : route 2e etage MED -> TP1..TP12 ===
        if Config.tpV2 == true and _G.ZenoTPV2 and type(_G.ZenoTPV2.run) == "function" then
            setStatus("TP V2 – route 2e étage...")
            local okV2, resV2 = pcall(_G.ZenoTPV2.run, petPos, hrp)
            if okV2 and resV2 then
                setStatus("V2 OK (" .. tostring(resV2) .. ") – steal libre")
                startAutoBackBrainrot(lastTargetData)
                cleanup()
                return
            end
            setStatus("V2 échec (" .. tostring(resV2) .. ") – retour tapis")
        end

        if Config.tpMode == "grapple" then carpetEngage() else equipCarpet() end
        vZero(hrp)

        local adjY = petPos.Y
        if TALL_PETS[petName] then adjY = petPos.Y - TALL_OFFSET end

        local function getPlotEdge(plotName, hrpPos, petPos, sideApproach)
            local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if not plots then return nil, nil end
            local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
            if not plot then return nil, nil end
            local center, size
            pcall(function()
                local bbox = plot:GetBoundingBox()
                center = bbox.Position
                size = bbox.Size
            end)
            if not center or not size then
                local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
                local coordTable = petPos.Y > UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD and UPPER or LOWER
                local isUpper = coordTable == UPPER
                local frontCoord, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, hrpPos.Z)
                return frontCoord, frontFace
            end
            local dirToCenter = (center - Vector3.new(hrpPos.X, 0, hrpPos.Z)).Unit
            local halfX = size.X / 2
            local halfZ = size.Z / 2
            local edgePos
            if sideApproach == "front" or sideApproach == "auto" then
                local frontZ = center.Z + (hrpPos.Z < center.Z and halfZ or -halfZ)
                edgePos = Vector3.new(center.X, center.Y, frontZ)
            else
                local sideX = center.X + (hrpPos.X < center.X and halfX or -halfX)
                edgePos = Vector3.new(sideX, center.Y, center.Z)
            end
            edgePos = Vector3.new(edgePos.X, petPos.Y > 23.15 and 17 or -2, edgePos.Z)
            local faceDir = (center - edgePos).Unit
            return edgePos, faceDir
        end

        local approachDest, approachFace = getPlotEdge(plotName, hrp.Position, petPos, Config.sideApproach)
        if not approachDest then
            local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
            local coordTable = petPos.Y > UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD and UPPER or LOWER
            local isUpper = coordTable == UPPER
            approachDest, approachFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, hrp.Position.Z)
        end
        local stableDest = approachDest + Vector3.new(0, 0.15, 0)

        local plotModel = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        local approachIgnore = {Workspace:FindFirstChild("NavTmpPart")}
        if plotModel then table.insert(approachIgnore, plotModel) end

        setStatus("Stabilizing approach...")
        predictiveObstacleFly(hrp, stableDest, math.max(speed * 0.85, 220), equipCarpet, approachIgnore)

        local settleDist = (hrp.Position - stableDest).Magnitude
        if settleDist > 1 then
            directFlyTo(hrp, stableDest, math.max(speed * 0.7, 200), equipCarpet, approachIgnore)
        end

        vZero(hrp)

        local exactEdgePos = approachDest
        local faceVec = approachFace or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local finalPos = exactEdgePos + faceVec * 0.0
        local targetCF = CFrame.new(finalPos, finalPos + faceVec)
        hrp.CFrame = targetCF
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        for i = 1, 8 do
            hrp.CFrame = targetCF
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            task.wait(0.01)
        end

        local finalOffset = (approachFace or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)) * 0.2
        local finalPos2 = stableDest + finalOffset
        local finalPos2CF = CFrame.new(finalPos2, finalPos2 + (approachFace or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)))

        local hipH = hum.HipHeight or 2.3
        local stopPlat = Instance.new("Part")
        stopPlat.Name = "AnchorPlat"
        stopPlat.Size = Vector3.new(2.6, 0.3, 2.6)
        stopPlat.Anchored = true
        stopPlat.CanCollide = true
        stopPlat.Transparency = 1
        stopPlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        stopPlat.Position = Vector3.new(stableDest.X, stableDest.Y - hipH - 0.1, stableDest.Z)
        stopPlat.Parent = Workspace

        hrp.Anchored = true
        setStatus("Anchored – waiting clone...")

        if cloneDelay > 0 then
            task.wait(cloneDelay)
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.CFrame = finalPos2CF
            vZero(hrp)
            task.wait(0.05)
            hrp.Anchored = true
        end

        local cloneSuccess = false
        local retryCount = 0
        local startPos = hrp.Position

        while retryCount < 2 and not cloneSuccess do
            if retryCount > 0 then
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.CFrame = finalPos2CF
                vZero(hrp)
                task.wait(0.1)
                hrp.Anchored = true
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            doClone()
            local waitStart = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - waitStart < 2 and hrp.Parent do
                if (hrp.Position - startPos).Magnitude > 2.5 then cloneSuccess = true; break end
                task.wait()
            end
            if not cloneSuccess then retryCount = retryCount + 1; startPos = hrp.Position end
        end

        if hrp and hrp.Parent then hrp.Anchored = false end
        if stopPlat and stopPlat.Parent then stopPlat:Destroy() end

        if not cloneSuccess then setStatus("Clone failed – aborting"); cleanup(); return end
        if not isInsidePlotSquare(hrp.Position, plotName, squareSz) then setStatus("Clone missed base – aborting"); cleanup(); return end

        task.wait(0.15)
        char = player.Character; if not char then cleanup(); return end
        hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then cleanup(); return end

        setStatus("Teleporting to pet...")
        local targetY = getTargetHeight(adjY)
        lastTargetData.targetY = targetY
        local dest = Vector3.new(petPos.X, targetY, petPos.Z)

        local followPlat = nil
        local function destroyPlat()
            if followPlat and followPlat.Parent then followPlat:Destroy() end
        end
        if petPos.Y > 23.15 then
            followPlat = Instance.new("Part")
            followPlat.Name = "FollowPlat"
            followPlat.Size = Vector3.new(5, 0.5, 5)
            followPlat.Anchored = true
            followPlat.CanCollide = true
            followPlat.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            followPlat.Color = Color3.fromRGB(30, 120, 220)
            followPlat.Transparency = 0.5
            followPlat.CastShadow = false
            followPlat.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, dest.Y - 0.5, hrp.Position.Z)
            followPlat.Parent = Workspace
            task.delay(5, destroyPlat)
        end

        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        if followPlat then task.delay(30, destroyPlat) end
        setStatus("Arrived – free to steal")
        startAutoBackBrainrot(lastTargetData)
    end)

    cleanup()
    if not ok then setStatus("Err: " .. tostring(err):sub(1,32)) end
end

_G.ZenoExecuteTP = function()
    autoBackActive = false
    task.spawn(executeTP)
end

manualTPBtn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
    autoBackActive = false
    task.spawn(executeTP)
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if gp then return end
    if keybindListening then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[Config.keybind] end)
    if ok and kc and input.KeyCode == kc then
        autoBackActive = false
        task.spawn(executeTP)
    end
end)

local scanLoopRunning = false
local function startRapidScanning()
    if scanLoopRunning then return end
    scanLoopRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.55) -- etait 0.05 = source majeure de lag
            pcall(function()
                scanAllPets()
                pcall(updateStealTargetDisplay)
            end)
        end
    end)
end
startRapidScanning()

task.spawn(function()
    loadModules()
    local char = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 20); char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 20)

    pcall(scanAllPets)
    while #allPetsCache == 0 do
        pcall(scanAllPets)
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    if Config.autoTPOnExecute then
        setStatus("Auto TP triggered...")
        executeTP()
    end

    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
        local newCache = {}
        for _, pet in ipairs(allPetsCache) do
            local plotName = pet.plot
            if plotName then
                local ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plotName)
                if ch then
                    local owner = _G.sProp(ch, "Owner")
                    local ownerId = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then ownerId = owner.UserId
                        elseif type(owner) == "number" then ownerId = owner
                        elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.UserId then ownerId = owner.UserId end
                    end)
                    if ownerId and ownerId ~= plr.UserId then
                        table.insert(newCache, pet)
                    end
                end
            else
                table.insert(newCache, pet)
            end
        end
        allPetsCache = newCache
        pcall(updateStealTargetDisplay)
    end)
end)

task.spawn(autoStealLoop)


-- ================================================================
-- ZENO PANEL RESIZERS
-- ================================================================
local function addZenoResizer(target, scaleObject, minScale, maxScale)
    if not target or not target.Parent then return end
    local old = target:FindFirstChild("ZENO_RESIZE_ARROW")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local arrow = Instance.new("TextButton")
    arrow.Name = "ZENO_RESIZE_ARROW"
    arrow.Text = "↘"
    arrow.Font = FONT_BOLD
    arrow.TextSize = 12
    arrow.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim
    arrow.AutoButtonColor = false
    arrow.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField2
    arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0,20,0,20)
    arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,1)
    arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1,-4,1,-4)
    arrow.ZIndex = 50
    arrow.Parent = target
    corner(arrow,6)
    strokeOn(arrow,Theme.Border,1)

    local busy=false
    arrow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if busy then return end
        busy=true
        local current = scaleObject.Scale
        local nextScale = current + 0.1
        if nextScale > maxScale then nextScale = minScale end
        tw(scaleObject,{Scale=nextScale},0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
        task.delay(0.2,function() busy=false end)
    end)
end

addZenoResizer(mainPanel, guiScaleObj, 0.8, 1.35)

        local function actionRow(key, label, callback, icon, color)
            local r=rowBase(key,label,40,icon)
            local b=mk("TextButton",{
                Text="GO",

                task.wait(0.07)

                pcall(callback)
            end)
            return r
        end

        actionRow("Rejoin","REJOIN",function()
            TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, player)
        end, nil, Color3.fromRGB(80,80,255))

        actionRow("ServerHop","SERVER HOP",function()
            local url="https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..tostring(game.PlaceId).."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
            local body
            pcall(function()
                if request then
                    local r=request({Url=url,Method="GET"})
                    body=r and (r.Body or r.body)
                elseif http and http.request then
                    local r=http.request({Url=url,Method="GET"})
                    body=r and (r.Body or r.body)
                end
            end)
            if not body then return end
            local good,data=pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
            if not good or type(data)~="table" then return end
            for _,s in ipairs(data.data or {}) do
                if s.id and s.id~=game.JobId and (s.playing or 0)<(s.maxPlayers or 0) then
                    pcall(function()
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,s.id,player)
                    end)
                    break
                end
            end
        end, nil, Color3.fromRGB(120,70,255))

        local ps=rowBase("JoinPS","PRIVATE SERVER",62,nil)
        local psBox=mk("TextBox",{
            Text="",
            PlaceholderText="Lien du serveur privé...",
            ClearTextOnFocus=false,
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=8,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            PlaceholderColor3=DIM,
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,38),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,28),
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-56,0,26),
            ZIndex=24
        },ps)
        round(psBox,8)
        outline(psBox,Theme.Border,1)

        local psGo=mk("TextButton",{
            Text="GO",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(100,80,255),
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-44,0,28),
            Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,26),
            ZIndex=24
        },ps)
        round(psGo,8)
        gradient(psGo)

        local function psError()
            psBox.TextColor3=RED
            psBox.PlaceholderText="Lien invalide"
            task.delay(1.5,function()
                if psBox.Parent then
                    psBox.TextColor3=WHITE
                    psBox.PlaceholderText="Lien du serveur privé..."
                end
            end)
        end

        psGo.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local link=psBox.Text:gsub("%s+","")
            if link=="" then psError(); return end
            local code=link:match("[?&]code=([%w%-_]+)") or link:match("privateServerLinkCode=([%w%-_]+)")
            if code and #code>=5 then
                pcall(function()
                    if setclipboard then setclipboard(link) end
                    if request then request({Url=link,Method="GET"}) end
                end)
            elseif link:match("^%d+$") then
                pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,link,player) end)
            else psError() end
        end)

        actionRow("InstaReset","INSTA RESET",function()
            if type(_G.ZenoInstantReset) == "function" then
                pcall(_G.ZenoInstantReset)
            else
                local c=player.Character
                local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h then
                    pcall(function() h.Health=0 end)
                    pcall(function() h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
                    pcall(function() c:BreakJoints() end)
                end
            end
        end, nil, Color3.fromRGB(255,80,80))

        actionRow("Kick","KICK SERVER",function()
            pcall(function() player:Kick("ZENO Player Controls") end)
        end, nil, Color3.fromRGB(50,50,60))

        -- Infinity Jump
        local inf=rowBase("InfinityJump","INFINITY JUMP",40,nil)
        local infBtn=mk("TextButton",{
            Text=infinityEnabled and "ON" or "OFF",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            BackgroundColor3=infinityEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(56,224,137) or Color3.fromRGB(35,35,42),
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,46,0,26),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-54,0.5,-13),
            ZIndex=24
        },inf)
        round(infBtn,8)
        outline(infBtn,Theme.Border,1)
        infBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            infinityEnabled=not infinityEnabled
            Config.infinityJump = infinityEnabled
            saveConfig()
            infBtn.Text=infinityEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
            tw(infBtn,{BackgroundColor3=infinityEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(56,224,137) or Color3.fromRGB(35,35,42)},0.15)
        end)
        -- Infinity Jump stable (no ragdoll / reset)
        -- Disables FallingDown/Ragdoll while ON, boosts only when Space held, no aggressive ChangeState spam
        local infConn
        local function applyInfStates(on)
            local c = player.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not h then return end
            pcall(function()
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, not on)
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, not on)
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying, not on)
                if on then
                    h.PlatformStand = false
                end
            end)
        end
        infBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            -- already toggled above; sync states
            applyInfStates(infinityEnabled)
        end)
        -- re-apply on character respawn
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.3)
            if infinityEnabled then applyInfStates(true) end
        end)
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not infinityEnabled then return end
            local c = player.Character
            if not c then return end
            local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not h or not hrp or h.Health <= 0 then return end
            -- only boost while Space / jump is held (mobile jump button counts via JumpRequest flag)
            local holding = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)
                or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
            if not holding then return end
            local vy = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
            if vy < 40 then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                        50,
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                    )
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
            if not infinityEnabled then return end
            local c = player.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not h or not hrp or h.Health <= 0 then return end
            pcall(function()
                applyInfStates(true)
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                    math.max(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 50),
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                )
            end)
        end)

        -- FOV slider stylé
        local fovRow=rowBase("FOV","FOV PLAYER",62,nil)
        local fovValue=mk("TextLabel",{
            Text=tostring(fov).."°",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=10,
            TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(130,90,255),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-46,0,6),
            Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,16),
            TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            ZIndex=24
        },fovRow)
        local track=mk("TextButton",{
            Text="",
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(35,35,42),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,36),
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,8),
            ZIndex=24
        },fovRow)
        round(track,4)
        local fovA0 = (fov-40)/80
        local fill=mk("Frame",{
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(120,80,255),
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            Size=UDim2.new(fovA0,0,1,0),
            ZIndex=25
        },track)
        round(fill,4)
        gradient(fill)
        local knob=mk("Frame",{
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,14,0,14),
            Position=UDim2.new(fovA0,-7,0.5,-7),
            ZIndex=26
        },track)
        round(knob,7)
        outline(knob,Color3.fromRGB(120,80,255),1.5)
        pcall(function() if Workspace.CurrentCamera then Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView=fov end end)
        local dragging=false
        local function setFovFromX(x)
            local a=math.clamp((x-track.AbsolutePosition.X)/math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X,1),0,1)
            fov=math.floor(40+(120-40)*a+0.5)
            Config.fovValue = fov
            fill.Size=UDim2.new(a,0,1,0)
            knob.Position=UDim2.new(a,-7,0.5,-7)
            fovValue.Text=tostring(fov).."°"
            pcall(function() if Workspace.CurrentCamera then Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView=fov end end)
        end
        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging=true
                setFovFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                setFovFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if dragging then saveConfig() end
                dragging=false
            end
        end)

        -- Auto Steal stylé
        local autoRow=rowBase("AutoSteal","AUTO STEAL",40,nil)
        local autoBtn=mk("TextButton",{
            Text=autoStealEnabled and "ON" or "OFF",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            BackgroundColor3=autoStealEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(56,224,137) or Color3.fromRGB(35,35,42),
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,46,0,26),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-54,0.5,-13),
            ZIndex=24
        },autoRow)
        round(autoBtn,8)
        outline(autoBtn,Theme.Border,1)
        autoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            autoStealEnabled=not autoStealEnabled
            Config.autoSteal=autoStealEnabled
            autoBtn.Text=autoStealEnabled and "ON" or "OFF"
            tw(autoBtn,{BackgroundColor3=autoStealEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(56,224,137) or Color3.fromRGB(35,35,42)},0.15)
            pcall(saveConfig)
            if autoStealToggle then autoStealToggle.SetState(autoStealEnabled) end
        end)

        -- FPS BOOST (exécute ZENO LOADER optimisations)
        local fpsBoostEnabled = Config.fpsBoost == true
        local fpsBoostConns = {}
        local fpsBoostOrigShadows = nil

        local function stopFpsBoost()
            for _, c in ipairs(fpsBoostConns) do
                pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
            end
            fpsBoostConns = {}
            pcall(function()
                if fpsBoostOrigShadows ~= nil then
                    Lighting.GlobalShadows = fpsBoostOrigShadows
                end
            end)
            fpsBoostEnabled = false
        end

        local function startFpsBoost()
            stopFpsBoost()
            fpsBoostEnabled = true

            -- FFlags giga-pixel (si disponible)
            pcall(function()
                if setfflag then
                    setfflag("DebugForceAllTextures1x1", "True")
                    setfflag("RomRenderTextureScale", "10")
                end
            end)

            -- Shadows off
            pcall(function()
                fpsBoostOrigShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            end)

            local function nukeTexture(obj)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
                        obj.ImageTransparency = 0
                        pcall(function() obj.ResampleMode = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated end)
                        obj.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 40)
                    end
                    if obj.Name == "Avatar" or obj.Name == "ProfilePicture" or obj.Name == "SettingsButton" then
                        obj.Visible = false
                    end
                end)
            end

            local function nuke3D(obj)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                        obj.TextureID = ""
                        obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                        obj.TextureId = ""
                    elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                        obj.Texture = ""
                    elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- UI nuke
            task.spawn(function()
                local pGui = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
                if not pGui then return end
                for _, obj in pairs(pGui:GetDescendants()) do
                    if not fpsBoostEnabled then return end
                    nukeTexture(obj)
                end
                local c = pGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(nukeTexture)
                table.insert(fpsBoostConns, c)
            end)

            -- 3D world nuke
            task.spawn(function()
                for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if not fpsBoostEnabled then return end
                    nuke3D(obj)
                end
                local c = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(nuke3D)
                table.insert(fpsBoostConns, c)
            end)

            -- Preload léger plots (comme le loader)
            task.spawn(function()
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if not plots then plots = Workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 3) end
                if not plots or not fpsBoostEnabled then return end
                local ids, seen, count = {}, {}, 0
                local propMap = {
                    MeshPart = {"MeshId", "TextureID"},
                    SpecialMesh = {"MeshId", "TextureId"},
                    Decal = {"Texture"},
                    Texture = {"Texture"},
                }
                pcall(function()
                    for _, obj in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do
                        if count >= 150 or not fpsBoostEnabled then break end
                        local props = propMap[obj.ClassName]
                        if props then
                            for _, prop in ipairs(props) do
                                pcall(function()
                                    local id = obj[prop]
                                    if id and id ~= "" and not seen[id] then
                                        local b = id:byte(1)
                                        if b == 114 or b == 104 then
                                            seen[id] = true
                                            count = count + 1
                                            ids[count] = id
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if count > 0 and fpsBoostEnabled then
                    local ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
                    local idx = 1
                    while idx <= count and fpsBoostEnabled do
                        local batch = {}
                        for j = idx, math.min(idx + 24, count) do
                            batch[#batch + 1] = ids[j]
                        end
                        pcall(ContentProvider.PreloadAsync, ContentProvider, batch)
                        idx = idx + 25
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local fpsRow = rowBase("FpsBoost", "FPS BOOST", 40, nil)
        local fpsBtn = mk("TextButton", {
            Text = fpsBoostEnabled and "ON" or "OFF",
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 9,
            TextColor3 = WHITE,
            BackgroundColor3 = fpsBoostEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
            ZIndex = 24
        }, fpsRow)
        round(fpsBtn, 8)
        outline(fpsBtn, Theme.Border, 1)
        fpsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if fpsBoostEnabled then
                stopFpsBoost()
                Config.fpsBoost = false
                saveConfig()
                fpsBtn.Text = "OFF"
                tw(fpsBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
            else
                startFpsBoost()
                Config.fpsBoost = true
                saveConfig()
                fpsBtn.Text = "ON"
                tw(fpsBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137)}, 0.15)
            end
        end)
        if fpsBoostEnabled then
            task.defer(startFpsBoost)
        end

        -- SLOW FALL (SAFE) : plus de BodyVelocity (souvent ban)
        -- Limite seulement une chute tres rapide, pas de hover permanent
        local slowFallOn = Config.floatOn == true
        local slowFallConn = nil
        local function stopSlowFall()
            slowFallOn = false
            if slowFallConn then pcall(function() slowFallConn:Disconnect() end) slowFallConn = nil end
        end
        local function startSlowFall()
            stopSlowFall()
            slowFallOn = true
            slowFallConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not slowFallOn then return end
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if v.Y < -55 then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, -35, v.Z)
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetFloat = function(on)
            Config.floatOn = on and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if on then startSlowFall() else stopSlowFall() end
        end
        do
            local fr = rowBase("Float", "SLOW FALL (SAFE)", 40, nil)
            local fb = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = slowFallOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = slowFallOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, fr)
            round(fb, 8)
            outline(fb, Theme.Border, 1)
            fb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                slowFallOn = not slowFallOn
                _G.ZenoSetFloat(slowFallOn)
                fb.Text = slowFallOn and "ON" or "OFF"
                tw(fb, {BackgroundColor3 = slowFallOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
            end)
            if slowFallOn then task.defer(startSlowFall) end
        end

        -- ANTI VOID
        local antiVoidOn = Config.antiVoid ~= false
        local lastSafe = nil
        do
            local av = rowBase("AntiVoid", "ANTI VOID", 40, nil)
            local ab = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = antiVoidOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = antiVoidOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, av)
            round(ab, 8)
            outline(ab, Theme.Border, 1)
            ab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                antiVoidOn = not antiVoidOn
                Config.antiVoid = antiVoidOn
                saveConfig()
                ab.Text = antiVoidOn and "ON" or "OFF"
                tw(ab, {BackgroundColor3 = antiVoidOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
            end)
            local lastRescue = 0
            RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                if hrp.Position.Y > -10 and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                    lastSafe = hrp.CFrame
                end
                if antiVoidOn and hrp.Position.Y < -50 and lastSafe and (os.clock() - lastRescue) > 2 then
                    lastRescue = os.clock()
                    pcall(function()
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.CFrame = lastSafe + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                    end)
                    if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Anti-Void save", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60)) end
                end
            end)
        end

        -- LOCK PANEL DRAG
        do
            local lockOn = Config.lockPlayerUi == true
            local lr = rowBase("LockUI", "LOCK PANEL DRAG", 40, nil)
            local lb = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = lockOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = lockOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, lr)
            round(lb, 8)
            outline(lb, Theme.Border, 1)
            _G.ZenoPlayerUiLocked = lockOn
            lb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                lockOn = not lockOn
                Config.lockPlayerUi = lockOn
                _G.ZenoPlayerUiLocked = lockOn
                saveConfig()
                lb.Text = lockOn and "ON" or "OFF"
                tw(lb, {BackgroundColor3 = lockOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
            end)
        end

        -- AUTO GRAB
        local grabOn = Config.autoGrab == true
        local grabConn = nil
        local function stopGrab()
            grabOn = false
            if grabConn then pcall(function() grabConn:Disconnect() end) grabConn = nil end
        end
        local function startGrab()
            stopGrab()
            grabOn = true
            local last = 0
            grabConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not grabOn then return end
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 0.55 then return end
                last = now
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum then return end
                local radius = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.autoGrabRadius) or 25, 5, 80)
                local root = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") or Workspace
                for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and inst.Enabled then
                        local part = inst.Parent
                        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local d = (part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if d <= radius then
                                local n = (inst.ActionText or "") .. " " .. (inst.ObjectText or "") .. " " .. inst.Name
                                local nl = n:lower()
                                if nl:find("steal") or nl:find("grab") or nl:find("collect") or nl:find("pick") then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        if fireproximityprompt then
                                            fireproximityprompt(inst)
                                        else
                                            inst:InputHoldBegin()
                                            task.wait(0.05)
                                            inst:InputHoldEnd()
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetAutoGrab = function(on)
            Config.autoGrab = on and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if on then startGrab() else stopGrab() end
        end
        do
            local gr = rowBase("AutoGrab", "AUTO GRAB", 40, nil)
            local gb = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = grabOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = grabOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, gr)
            round(gb, 8)
            outline(gb, Theme.Border, 1)
            gb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                grabOn = not grabOn
                _G.ZenoSetAutoGrab(grabOn)
                gb.Text = grabOn and "ON" or "OFF"
                tw(gb, {BackgroundColor3 = grabOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
            end)
            if grabOn then task.defer(startGrab) end
        end

        -- MIN GEN (filtre)
        do
            local minG = math.max(0, tonumber(Config.minGen) or 0)
            local mr = rowBase("MinGen", "MIN GEN FILTER", 62, nil)
            local valLbl = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = tostring(minG),
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 90, 255),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 6),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 16),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, mr)
            local track = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 36),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 8),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, mr)
            round(track, 4)
            -- scale log-ish: 0 to 50M
            local maxG = 50000000
            local a0 = math.clamp(minG / maxG, 0, 1)
            local fill = mk("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 80, 255),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.new(a0, 0, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 25,
            }, track)
            round(fill, 4)
            local dragging = false
            local function fmt(v)
                if v >= 1e6 then return string.format("%.1fM", v / 1e6) end
                if v >= 1e3 then return string.format("%.0fK", v / 1e3) end
                return tostring(math.floor(v))
            end
            valLbl.Text = fmt(minG)
            local function setFromX(x)
                local a = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
                minG = math.floor(a * maxG + 0.5)
                Config.minGen = minG
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
                valLbl.Text = fmt(minG)
            end
            track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    setFromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    setFromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if dragging then
                        saveConfig()
                        playClickSound()
                        pcall(updateStealTargetDisplay)
                    end
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end

        -- AUTO KICK AFTER STEAL (Player Controls)
        do
            local akOn = Config.autoKickAfterSteal == true
            local ar = rowBase("AutoKick", "AUTO KICK AFTER STEAL", 40, nil)
            local ab = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = akOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = akOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, ar)
            round(ab, 8)
            outline(ab, Theme.Border, 1)
            ab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                akOn = not akOn
                Config.autoKickAfterSteal = akOn
                saveConfig()
                ab.Text = akOn and "ON" or "OFF"
                tw(ab, {BackgroundColor3 = akOn and Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)}, 0.15)
                if _G.ZenoNotify then
                    _G.ZenoNotify(akOn and "Auto Kick After Steal ON" or "Auto Kick After Steal OFF", Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100))
                end
            end)
        end

        -- DROP from player controls
        do
            local dr = rowBase("Drop", "DROP BRAINROT", 40, nil)
            local db = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = "DROP",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -13),
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, dr)
            round(db, 8)
            db.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                if type(_G.ZenoDropBrainrot) == "function" then
                    _G.ZenoDropBrainrot()
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Settings popup (identique mais stylé)
        local overlay=mk("Frame",{
            Name="PlayerSettingsOverlay",
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0),
            BackgroundTransparency=0.5,
            Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
            Visible=false,
            ZIndex=100
        },gui)
        local popup=mk("Frame",{
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),
            Size=UDim2.new(0,220,0,320),
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,15),
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            ClipsDescendants=true,
            ZIndex=101
        },overlay)
        round(popup,14)
        outline(popup,Color3.fromRGB(80,60,120),1.2)
        mk("UIGradient",{Color=ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(20,20,26), Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12)), Rotation=90}, popup)

        mk("TextLabel",{
            Text="PLAYER • SETTINGS",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=11,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,30),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,6),
            ZIndex=102
        },popup)

        local settings=mk("ScrollingFrame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,230),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,38),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0),
            AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ScrollBarThickness=5,
            ScrollBarImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(140,100,255),
            ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
            Active=true,
            ZIndex=102
        },popup)
        mk("UIPadding",{PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,2),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,8),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,4)},settings)
        mk("UIListLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding=UDim.new(0,6)}, settings)

        for _,entry in ipairs(settingEntries) do
            local erow=mk("Frame",{
                Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,32),
                BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(24,24,30),
                LayoutOrder=_,
                ZIndex=103
            },settings)
            round(erow,8)
            outline(erow,Theme.Border,1)
            mk("TextLabel",{
                Text=entry.label,
                Font=FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize=8,
                TextColor3=WHITE,
                TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency=1,
                Size=UDim2.new(1,-60,1,0),
                Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,0),
                TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                ZIndex=104
            },erow)
            local sw=mk("TextButton",{
                Text="",
                AutoButtonColor=false,
                BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(64,148,235),
                Size=UDim2.new(0,34,0,18),
                Position=UDim2.new(1,-42,0.5,-9),
                ZIndex=104
            },erow)
            round(sw,9)
            local knob2=mk("Frame",{
                BackgroundColor3=WHITE,
                BorderSizePixel=0,
                Size=UDim2.new(0,12,0,12),
                Position=UDim2.new(1,-15,0.5,-6),
                ZIndex=105
            },sw)
            round(knob2,6)
            local enabled=true
            sw.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                enabled=not enabled
                entry.row.Visible=enabled
                sw.BackgroundColor3=enabled and Color3.fromRGB(64,148,235) or Color3.fromRGB(40,40,48)
                knob2.Position=enabled and UDim2.new(1,-15,0.5,-6) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-6)
            end)
        end

        local close=mk("TextButton",{
            Text="FERMER",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=10,
            TextColor3=WHITE,
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(64,148,235),
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,32),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,1,-42),
            ZIndex=103
        },popup)
        round(close,8)
        gradient(close)

        overlay.Visible = false
        gear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible = not overlay.Visible
            if overlay.Visible then pushBlur() else popBlur() end
        end)
        close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible = false
            popBlur()
        end)

        -- Dragging on mobile.
        do
            local draggingPanel=false
            local startInput
            local startPos
            header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if _G.ZenoPlayerUiLocked then return end
                if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    draggingPanel=true
                    startInput=i.Position
                    startPos=panel.Position
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if draggingPanel and (i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local d=i.Position-startInput
                    panel.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if draggingPanel then
                        Config.posPlayer = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
                        saveConfig()
                    end
                    draggingPanel=false
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Keep FOV after camera replacement.
        RunService2.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
                    Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView=fov
                end
            end)
        end)
    end)

    if not ok then
        warn("[ZENO PLAYER CONTROLS] "..tostring(err))
    end
end)


-- ================================================================
-- ZENO INVIS STEAL
-- ================================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local ok,err=pcall(function()
        local old=playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_INVIS_STEAL")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name="ZENO_INVIS_STEAL"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn=false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
        gui.DisplayOrder=998
        gui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        gui.Parent=playerGui

        local panel=new("Frame",{
            Name="Panel",
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position=posToUDim2(Config.posInvis or {X=0.5,XOffset=-235,Y=0.5,YOffset=25}),
            -- Fixed height prevents the panel collapsing to 0px on some clients.
            Size=UDim2.new(0,192,0,300),
            AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.None,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel,
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            ZIndex=20
        },gui)
        corner(panel,18)
        strokeOn(panel,Theme.Border,1)
        bgDepthGrad(panel)
        pcall(function()
            if type(_G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle(panel, { transparency = 0.38, starCount = 12, corner = 18 })
            end
        end)
        local scale=new("UIScale",{Scale=Config.guiScale or 1},panel)

        local header=new("Frame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,18),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,10),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            ZIndex=21
        },panel)

        local _zl = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        _zl.Name = "ZenoLogoMark"
        _zl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _zl.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _zl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28,28)
        _zl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -14)
        _zl.ZIndex = 22
        _zl.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        _zl.Image = "rbxassetid://92530687076466"
        _zl.Parent = header
        do local _c=Instance.new("UICorner"); _c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10); _c.Parent=_zl end
        local title=new("TextLabel",{
            Text="ZENO INVIS STEAL",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=13,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-44,1,0),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,20,0,0),
            ZIndex=22
        },header)
        accentGrad(title,0)

        local gear=new("TextButton",{
            Text="⚙",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=14,
            TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-20,0.5,-10),
            ZIndex=23
        },header)

        local drag=new("Frame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,1,0),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Active=true,
            ZIndex=22
        },header)
        local dragging,startInput,startPos
        local function ptr(i)
            return i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        end
        drag.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if not ptr(i) then return end
            dragging=true
            startInput=i.Position
            startPos=panel.Position
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not dragging then return end
            if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local d=i.Position-startInput
            panel.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if ptr(i) then
                if dragging then
                    Config.posInvis = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
                    saveConfig()
                end
                dragging=false
            end
        end)

        local body=new("Frame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,1,-44),
            AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.None,
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,34),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            ZIndex=21
        },panel)
        new("UIListLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,6)},body)

        -- These are the three invisibility controls moved out of TP TO BEST.
        local invisToggle=createToggle(body,"Invis on Steal",1,Config.invisOnSteal,false,"invisOnSteal")
        invisOnStealToggle=invisToggle

        local depth=createSlider(body,"Invis Depth",2,1,10,Config.invisDepth,1,"","invisDepth")
        invisDepthSlider=depth

        local rot=createSlider(body,"Invis Rot",3,1,360,Config.invisRotation,0,"°","invisRotation")
        invisRotationSlider=rot

        local delaySlider=createSlider(body,"Invis Delay",4,0,1000,tonumber(Config.invisDelayMs) or 0,0,"ms","invisDelayMs")

        local recoverToggle=createToggle(body,"Auto Recover Lagback",5,Config.invisAutoRecover ~= false,false,"invisAutoRecover")

        new("Frame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1),
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.Border,
            BackgroundTransparency=0.3,
            LayoutOrder=6
        },body)

        local info=new("TextLabel",{
            Text="INVIS • IDLE",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=10,
            TextColor3=Theme.TextFaint,
            TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,38),
            LayoutOrder=7
        },body)
        task.spawn(function()
            while info and info.Parent do
                local on = _G.invisibleStealEnabled == true
                local auto = Config.invisOnSteal ~= false
                if on then
                    info.Text = "INVIS • ACTIVE"
                    info.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137)
                elseif auto then
                    info.Text = "INVIS • AUTO ON STEAL"
                    info.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 160, 255)
                else
                    info.Text = "INVIS • OFF"
                    info.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 170)
                end
                task.wait(0.25)
            end
        end)

        -- Dedicated settings popup, same visual language as the other panels.
        local overlay=new("Frame",{
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0),
            BackgroundTransparency=0.45,
            Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
            Visible=false,
            ZIndex=100
        },gui)
        local popup=new("Frame",{
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
            Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),
            Size=UDim2.new(0,192,0,118),
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.Panel,
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            ZIndex=101
        },overlay)
        corner(popup,13)
        strokeOn(popup,Theme.Border,1.5)
        new("TextLabel",{
            Text="INVIS STEAL • SETTINGS",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=10,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,26),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,8),
            ZIndex=102
        },popup)

        local close=new("TextButton",{
            Text="FERMER",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.AccentB,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,1,-38),
            ZIndex=103
        },popup)
        corner(close,7)
        accentGrad(close,90)

        local showToggle=new("TextButton",{
            Text="Invis panel: ON",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,40),
            ZIndex=103
        },popup)
        corner(showToggle,7)

        gear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible=not overlay.Visible
            if overlay.Visible then pushBlur() else popBlur() end
        end)
        close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible=false
            popBlur()
        end)
        showToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            panel.Visible=not panel.Visible
            showToggle.Text=panel.Visible and "Invis panel: ON" or "Invis panel: OFF"
            if not panel.Visible then
                overlay.Visible=false
                popBlur()
            end
        end)

        local arrow=new("TextButton",{
            Name="ZENO_RESIZE_ARROW",
            Text="↘",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=12,
            TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2,
            BackgroundTransparency=0.15,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20),
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,1),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-4,1,-4),
            ZIndex=60
        },panel)
        corner(arrow,6)
        strokeOn(arrow,Theme.Border,1)
        arrow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local n=scale.Scale+0.1
            if n>1.35 then n=0.8 end
            tw(scale,{Scale=n},0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
        end)
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[ZENO INVIS STEAL] "..tostring(err)) end
end)
