            ScrollBarThickness=0,
            CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0),
            AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex=21
        },panel)
        new("UIListLayout",{
            SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding=UDim.new(0,5)
        },list)

        local clear=new("TextButton",{
            Text="AUTO TARGET • DEFAULT",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=8,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,1,-35),
            ZIndex=24
        },panel)
        corner(clear,7)
        strokeOn(clear,Theme.Border,1)

        -- SETTINGS OVERLAY (Gen/Value/Priority)
        local overlay=new("Frame",{
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0),
            BackgroundTransparency=0.45,
            Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
            Visible=false,
            ZIndex=100
        },gui)
        local popup=new("Frame",{
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),

            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,15),
            BorderSizePixel=0,
            ZIndex=101
        },overlay)
        corner(popup,14)
        strokeOn(popup,Theme.Outline,1.2)
        bgDepthGrad(popup)

        new("TextLabel",{
            Text="STEAL TARGET • SETTINGS",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=11,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,6),
            ZIndex=102
        },popup)

        local close=new("TextButton",{
            Text="FERMER",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=9,
            TextColor3=Theme.Text,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.AccentB,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,1,-38),
            ZIndex=103
        },popup)
        corner(close,7)
        accentGrad(close,90)

        -- Gen / Value / Priority buttons inside popup
        local segRowPopup=new("Frame",{
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,28),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,36),
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            ZIndex=102
        },popup)
        new("UIListLayout",{FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding=UDim.new(0,6)}, segRowPopup)

        local segOptionsPopup = {"Gen","Value","Priority","Nearest"}
        local segButtonsPopup = {}
        local function refreshSegPopup()
            for _,e in ipairs(segButtonsPopup) do
                local active = e.Value:lower()==(Config.targetMode or "gen")
                tw(e.Instance,{BackgroundColor3=active and Theme.PanelField2 or Theme.PanelField},0.15)
                e.Stroke.Color = active and Theme.AccentB or Theme.Border
                e.Label.TextColor3 = active and Theme.Text or Theme.TextDim
            end
        end
        local function applySegPopup(value)
            local v=value:lower()
            Config.targetMode=v
            saveConfig()
            refreshSegPopup()
            pcall(updateStealTargetDisplay)
            pcall(refreshList)
        end
        for i,opt in ipairs(segOptionsPopup) do
            local b=new("TextButton",{Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,Size=UDim2.new(0,60,1,0),BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField,LayoutOrder=i, ZIndex=103},segRowPopup)
            corner(b,8); local stk=strokeOn(b,Theme.Border,1)
            local lbl=new("TextLabel",{Text=opt,Font=FONT_BOLD,TextSize=9,TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), ZIndex=104},b)
            table.insert(segButtonsPopup,{Instance=b,Label=lbl,Stroke=stk,Value=opt})
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applySegPopup(opt) end)
        end
        refreshSegPopup()

        -- Mode label
        new("TextLabel",{
            Text="Mode actif: le TP choisira selon ce réglage",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=7,
            TextColor3=Theme.TextFaint,
            BackgroundTransparency=1,
            Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,12),
            Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,70),
            ZIndex=102,
            TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        },popup)

        settingsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible=not overlay.Visible
            if overlay.Visible then pushBlur() else popBlur() end
            refreshSegPopup()
        end)
        close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            overlay.Visible=false
            popBlur()
        end)

        local function fmtMps(v)
            v=tonumber(v) or 0
            if v>=1000000 then
                return string.format("%.1fM/s",v/1000000)
            elseif v>=1000 then
                return string.format("%.1fK/s",v/1000)
            end
            return string.format("%.0f/s",v)
        end

        local function getPetThumbnail(indexName)
            if not indexName then return nil end
            pcall(loadModules)

            local function extractIcon(tbl)
                if type(tbl) ~= "table" then return nil end
                local keys = {
                    "Icon", "Image", "ImageId", "Thumbnail", "Render", "AssetId",
                    "ViewportImage", "IconImage", "IconId", "Picture", "Sprite",
                    "DisplayImage", "Headshot", "Portrait"
                }
                for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
                    local v = tbl[key]
                    if v ~= nil and v ~= "" and v ~= 0 and v ~= "0" then
                        return v
                    end
                end
                -- nested tables (Rarity, Info, Data, Visuals…)
                for _, sub in pairs(tbl) do
                    if type(sub) == "table" then
                        for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
                            local v = sub[key]
                            if v ~= nil and v ~= "" and v ~= 0 and v ~= "0" then
                                return v
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            -- 1) AnimalsData déjà chargé
            if AnimalsData then
                local icon = extractIcon(AnimalsData[indexName])
                if icon then return icon end
            end

            -- 2) Datas.Animals
            local ok, data = pcall(function()
                local datas = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Datas")
                local animals = datas and datas:FindFirstChild("Animals")
                if animals then
                    local mod = require(animals)
                    return mod and mod[indexName]
                end
            end)
            if ok and data then
                local icon = extractIcon(data)
                if icon then return icon end
            end

            -- 3) Shared.Animals
            pcall(function()
                local sh = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
                local a = sh and sh:FindFirstChild("Animals")
                if a then
                    local mod = require(a)
                    if mod and mod[indexName] then
                        data = mod[indexName]
                    end
                end
            end)
            if type(data) == "table" then
                local icon = extractIcon(data)
                if icon then return icon end
            end

            -- 4) Recherche globale dans ReplicatedStorage (dernier recours)
            pcall(function()
                for _, desc in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc.Name == "Animals" and (desc:IsA("ModuleScript") or desc:IsA("Folder")) then
                        local mod = desc:IsA("ModuleScript") and require(desc) or nil
                        if type(mod) == "table" and mod[indexName] then
                            local icon = extractIcon(mod[indexName])
                            if icon then data = icon; return end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            if type(data) == "string" or type(data) == "number" then
                return data
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function makeRow(rank,pet)
            local selected = SelectedStealTarget and pet.uid==SelectedStealTarget.uid

            local row=new("TextButton",{
                Text="",
                AutoButtonColor=false,
                BackgroundColor3=selected and Theme.PanelField2 or Theme.PanelField,
                Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,48),
                LayoutOrder=rank,
                ZIndex=22
            },list)
            corner(row,10)
            local st=strokeOn(row,selected and Theme.AccentB or Theme.Border,1)

            -- Image du brainrot : stream + Viewport 3D prioritaire, puis icône data/podium
            local imgFrame=new("Frame",{
                Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,36),
                Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0.5,-18),
                BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12),
                ZIndex=23,
                ClipsDescendants=true
            },row)
            corner(imgFrame,8)
            strokeOn(imgFrame,Theme.Border,1)

            -- === IMAGE BRAINROT (méthode KOBYHUB qui marche) ===
            task.spawn(function()
                local function clearFrame()
                    for _, c in ipairs(imgFrame:GetChildren()) do
                        c:Destroy()
                    end
                end

                -- Placeholder immédiat
                local placeholder = new("TextLabel", {
                    Text = "?",
                    Font = FONT_BOLD,
                    TextSize = 14,
                    TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                    ZIndex = 24
                }, imgFrame)

                local _animalsModCache = nil
                local function getAnimalsMod()
                    if _animalsModCache then return _animalsModCache end
                    local sh = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
                    local an = sh and sh:FindFirstChild("Animals")
                    if not an then return nil end
                    local ok, mod = pcall(require, an)
                    if ok and type(mod) == "table" then
                        _animalsModCache = mod
                        return mod
                    end
                    return nil
                end

                local function fitIcon(viewport, model)
                    if not model then return end
                    local cam = viewport.CurrentCamera
                    if not (cam and cam.Parent) then
                        cam = Instance.new("Camera")
                        cam.Parent = viewport
                        viewport.CurrentCamera = cam
                    end
                    cam.FieldOfView = 28
                    local minv, maxv
                    for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Transparency < 0.92 and d.Size.Magnitude > 0.08 then
                            local cf, hs = d.CFrame, d.Size * 0.5
                            for _, ox in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
                                for _, oy in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
                                    for _, oz in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
                                        local p = cf * Vector3.new(hs.X * ox, hs.Y * oy, hs.Z * oz)
                                        if not minv then
                                            minv, maxv = p, p
                                        else
                                            minv = Vector3.new(math.min(minv.X, p.X), math.min(minv.Y, p.Y), math.min(minv.Z, p.Z))
                                            maxv = Vector3.new(math.max(maxv.X, p.X), math.max(maxv.Y, p.Y), math.max(maxv.Z, p.Z))
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local center, size
                    if minv then
                        center = (minv + maxv) * 0.5
                        size = maxv - minv
                    else
                        local okBB, cf, bb = pcall(function()
                            return model:GetBoundingBox()
                        end)
                        if not okBB or not cf then return end
                        center, size = cf.Position, bb
                    end
                    local half = math.max(size.X, size.Y, 0.2) * 0.5
                    local dist = (half / math.tan(math.rad(cam.FieldOfView) * 0.5)) * 1.08
                    dist = math.max(dist, size.Z * 0.55)
                    local dir = Vector3.new(0.42, 0.22, 1).Unit
                    cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(center + dir * dist, center)
                end

                local function showWithViewport(model)
                    if not model then return false end
                    clearFrame()
                    local viewport = new("ViewportFrame", {
                        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
                        BorderSizePixel = 0,
                        Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 236),
                        LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 248),
                        LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
                        ZIndex = 24
                    }, imgFrame)
                    corner(viewport, 8)

                    local clone = model:Clone()
                    for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("Highlight")
                            or d:IsA("Sound") or d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                            d:Destroy()
                        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                            d.Anchored = true
                        end
                    end
                    local wm = Instance.new("WorldModel")
                    wm.Parent = viewport
                    clone.Parent = wm
                    fitIcon(viewport, clone)
                    return true
                end

                local function tryAnimalsMod()
                    local key = tostring(pet.index or pet.name or "")
                    if key == "" or key == "?" then return false end
                    local mod = getAnimalsMod()
                    if not mod then return false end

                    -- 1) GetAnimatedModel (méthode KOBY)
                    if type(mod.GetAnimatedModel) == "function" then
                        local ok, m = pcall(function()
                            return mod.GetAnimatedModel(mod, key)
                        end)
                        if ok and typeof(m) == "Instance" then
                            return showWithViewport(m)
                        end
                    end

                    -- 2) AttachOnViewport (méthode KOBY)
                    if type(mod.AttachOnViewport) == "function" then
                        clearFrame()
                        local viewport = new("ViewportFrame", {
                            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19),
                            BackgroundTransparency = 0,
                            BorderSizePixel = 0,
                            Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 236),
                            LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 248),
                            LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -1),
                            ZIndex = 24
                        }, imgFrame)
                        corner(viewport, 8)
                        local ok = pcall(function()
                            mod.AttachOnViewport(mod, key, viewport, true)
                        end)
                        if ok and #viewport:GetChildren() > 0 then
                            return true
                        end
                        viewport:Destroy()
                    end
                    return false
                end

                local function tryPetModel()
                    -- Si le pet a déjà un model attaché (comme dans KOBY)
                    if pet.model and typeof(pet.model) == "Instance" and pet.model.Parent then
                        return showWithViewport(pet.model)
                    end
                    return false
                end

                local function tryWorldModel()
                    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                    local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(pet.plot)
                    if not plot then return false end
                    pcall(function()
                        local pivot = plot:GetPivot()
                        player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pivot.Position, 100)
                    end)
                    local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                    local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(pet.slot))
                    if not podium then return false end

                    for attempt = 1, 5 do
                        local best, bestScore = nil, -1
                        for _, desc in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                            if desc:IsA("Model") then
                                local n = desc.Name
                                if n ~= "Claim" and n ~= "Base" and n ~= "Decorations" and n ~= "Spawn" and n ~= "Hitbox" then
                                    local meshCount = 0
                                    for _, c in ipairs(desc:GetDescendants()) do
                                        if c:IsA("MeshPart") or c:IsA("SpecialMesh") or c:IsA("Part") then
                                            meshCount = meshCount + 1
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if meshCount > bestScore then
                                        bestScore = meshCount
                                        best = desc
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if best and bestScore > 0 then
                            return showWithViewport(best)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.08)
                    end
                    return false
                end

                local function tryDataIcon()
                    local thumb = getPetThumbnail(pet.index or pet.name)
                    if not thumb and pet.name and pet.name ~= pet.index then
                        thumb = getPetThumbnail(pet.name)
                    end
                    if not thumb then return false end
                    local s = tostring(thumb)
                    if tonumber(s) then s = "rbxassetid://" .. s end
                    if s:match("^%d+$") then s = "rbxassetid://" .. s end
                    clearFrame()
                    local img = new("ImageLabel", {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                        Image = s,
                        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                        ZIndex = 25
                    }, imgFrame)
                    corner(img, 8)
                    return true
                end

                local ok = false
                -- Ordre exact de KOBYHUB (celui qui marche)
                pcall(function() ok = tryPetModel() end)
                if not ok then pcall(function() ok = tryAnimalsMod() end) end
                if not ok then pcall(function() ok = tryWorldModel() end) end
                if not ok then pcall(function() ok = tryDataIcon() end) end

                if not ok then
                    clearFrame()
                    new("TextLabel", {
                        Text = "🧠",
                        Font = FONT_BOLD,
                        TextSize = 18,
                        TextColor3 = Theme.AccentB,
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                        ZIndex = 25
                    }, imgFrame)
                end
            end)

            local name=new("TextLabel",{
                Text=tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "Unknown"),
                Font=FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize=10,
                TextColor3=Theme.Text,
                TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency=1,
                Position=UDim2.new(0,44,0,4),
                Size=UDim2.new(1,-110,0,16),
                TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                ZIndex=23
            },row)

            local idxLbl=new("TextLabel",{
                Text=tostring(pet.index or ""),
                Font=FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize=7,
                TextColor3=Theme.TextFaint,
                TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency=1,
                Position=UDim2.new(0,44,0,20),
                Size=UDim2.new(1,-110,0,10),
                TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                ZIndex=23
            },row)

            local mps=new("TextLabel",{
                Text=fmtMps(pet.mps),
                Font=FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize=10,
                TextColor3=selected and Theme.AccentB or Theme.Green,
                TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
                BackgroundTransparency=1,
                Position=UDim2.new(1,-61,0,0),
                Size=UDim2.new(0,55,1,0),
                ZIndex=23
            },row)

            if pet.owner and pet.owner ~= "" then
                new("TextLabel",{
                    Text="Owner: "..tostring(pet.owner),
                    Font=FONT_BOLD,
                    TextSize=7,
                    TextColor3=Theme.TextFaint,
                    TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                    BackgroundTransparency=1,
                    Position=UDim2.new(0,44,0,28),
                    Size=UDim2.new(1,-110,0,10),
                    TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                    ZIndex=23
                },row)
            end

            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                SelectedStealTarget={
                    uid=pet.uid,
                    name=pet.name,
                    index=pet.index,
                    mps=pet.mps,
                    plot=pet.plot,
                    slot=pet.slot,
                    position=pet.position,
                    owner=pet.owner,
                }
                local own = pet.owner and (" • "..tostring(pet.owner)) or ""
                clear.Text="SELECTED • "..tostring(pet.name or pet.index or "TARGET")..own
                refreshList()
            end)

            return row
        end

        local _lastShownUid = nil
        local _lastShownCount = -1
        local _forceRefresh = false

        function refreshList(force)
            if #allPetsCache == 0 then
                pcall(scanAllPets)
            end

            local pets = {}
            if #allPetsCache > 0 then
                table.insert(pets, allPetsCache[1])
            end

            local bestUid = pets[1] and pets[1].uid or nil
            local count = #allPetsCache
            status.Text = tostring(count) .. " DETECTED • BEST"

            -- Ne rebuild l'UI + image QUE si le target change (anti-lag)
            if not force and not _forceRefresh and bestUid == _lastShownUid and count == _lastShownCount then
                return
            end
            _lastShownUid = bestUid
            _lastShownCount = count
            _forceRefresh = false

            for _,c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("TextButton") or (c:IsA("TextLabel") and c.Name ~= "Status") then c:Destroy() end
            end

            if #pets == 0 then
                local empty = new("TextLabel",{
                    Name = "Empty",
                    Text = "Scan... approche un plot",
                    Font = FONT_BOLD,
                    TextSize = 9,
                    TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,30),
                    LayoutOrder = 1,
                    ZIndex = 22
                }, list)
            else
                makeRow(1, pets[1])
            end
        end

        clear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SelectedStealTarget=nil
            clear.Text="AUTO TARGET • DEFAULT"
            _forceRefresh = true
            refreshList(true)
        end)

        refresh.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            pcall(scanAllPets)
            _forceRefresh = true
            refreshList(true)
        end)

        -- Refresh léger : check toutes les 0.4s, rebuild seulement si target change
        task.spawn(function()
            while gui.Parent do
                task.wait(0.4)
                pcall(refreshList)
            end
        end)

        -- Mobile drag.
        local dragging,startInput,startPos
        local function ptr(i)
            return i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        end
        header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if not ptr(i) then return end
            dragging=true
            startInput=i.Position
            startPos=panel.Position
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not dragging then return end
            if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            local d=i.Position-startInput
            panel.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if ptr(i) then
                if dragging then
                    Config.posTarget = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
                    saveConfig()
                end
                dragging=false
            end
        end)

        local arrow=new("TextButton",{
            Name="ZENO_RESIZE_ARROW",
            Text="↘",
            Font=FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize=12,
            TextColor3=Theme.TextDim,
            AutoButtonColor=false,
            BackgroundColor3=Theme.PanelField2,
            BackgroundTransparency=0.15,
            Size=UDim2.new(0,20,0,20),
            AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,1),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-4,1,-4),
            ZIndex=60
        },panel)
        corner(arrow,6)
        strokeOn(arrow,Theme.Border,1)
        arrow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local n=scale.Scale+0.1
            if n>1.35 then n=0.8 end
            tw(scale,{Scale=n},0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
        end)

        refreshList()
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[ZENO STEAL TARGET] "..tostring(err)) end
end)

-- ================================================================
-- ZENO UI PRINCIPAL V2
-- ================================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_UI_PRINCIPAL")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local oldOpen = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_UI_OPEN")
        if oldOpen then oldOpen:Destroy() end

        -- Glass Noir fixed theme (no color themes)
        local Themes = {
            Noir = {
                bg = Color3.fromRGB(7, 9, 13),
                card = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19),
                field = Color3.fromRGB(16, 20, 27),
                accent = Color3.fromRGB(125, 205, 255),
                accent2 = Color3.fromRGB(64, 148, 235),
                text = Color3.fromRGB(238, 244, 252),
                mute = Color3.fromRGB(142, 156, 176),
                border = Color3.fromRGB(55, 64, 80),
                on = Color3.fromRGB(95, 190, 255),
            },
        }
        local T = Themes.Noir
        local themeName = "Noir"

        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "ZENO_UI_PRINCIPAL"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.DisplayOrder = 1000
        gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        gui.Parent = playerGui

        local openGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        openGui.Name = "ZENO_UI_OPEN"
        openGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        openGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        openGui.DisplayOrder = 1001
        openGui.Parent = playerGui

        local function mk(cl, props, parent)
            local i = Instance.new(cl)
            for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do i[k] = v end
            if parent then i.Parent = parent end
            return i
        end
        local function round(p, r)
            return mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 10)}, p)
        end
        local function stroke(p, c, th)
            return mk("UIStroke", {Color = c or T.border, Thickness = th or 1.2, Transparency = 0.1}, p)
        end

        local baseW, baseH = 560, 460
        local main = mk("Frame", {
            Name = "Main",
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = posToUDim2(Config.posPrincipal or {X=0.5,XOffset=0,Y=0.5,YOffset=0}),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, baseW, 0, baseH),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.38,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Active = true,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, gui)
        round(main, 18)
        local mainStroke = stroke(main, Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90), 1.1)
        mainStroke.Transparency = 0.35
        local mainGrad = mk("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22), Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            Rotation = 120,
        }, main)

        local scaleObj = mk("UIScale", {Scale = tonumber(Config.hubScale) or 1}, main)

        -- Falling stars (snow-style background)
        local starLayer = mk("Frame", {
            Name = "StarSnow",
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ZIndex = 11,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
        }, main)
        do
            local stars = {}
            local glyphs = {"✦", "✧", "·", "⋆", "∗"}
            for i = 1, 28 do
                local s = mk("TextLabel", {
                    Text = glyphs[(i % #glyphs) + 1],
                    Font = FONT_BOLD,
                    TextSize = ({8, 9, 10, 11, 12})[(i % 5) + 1],
                    TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                    TextTransparency = 0.35 + (i % 5) * 0.08,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    Position = UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, math.random(), 0),
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14),
                    ZIndex = 11,
                    Rotation = math.random(-20, 20),
                }, starLayer)
                stars[i] = {
                    lbl = s,
                    x = math.random(),
                    y = math.random(),
                    speed = 0.018 + math.random() * 0.045,
                    drift = (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.012,
                    tw = 0.3 + math.random() * 0.5,
                }
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local acc = 0
                while starLayer and starLayer.Parent do
                    local dt = task.wait(0.04)
                    acc = acc + dt
                    for _, st in ipairs(stars) do
                        st.y = st.y + st.speed * (dt / 0.04) * 0.35
                        st.x = st.x + st.drift * (dt / 0.04) * 0.2
                        if st.y > 1.05 then
                            st.y = -0.05
                            st.x = math.random()
                        end
                        if st.x < -0.05 then st.x = 1.05 end
                        if st.x > 1.05 then st.x = -0.05 end
                        st.lbl.Position = UDim2.new(st.x, 0, st.y, 0)
                        st.lbl.TextTransparency = 0.25 + 0.45 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(acc * 2 + st.tw * 6))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local playerNameLbl = mk("TextLabel", {

        -- Top bar (FPS + hide)
        local blackBar = mk("Frame", {

        }, right)

        barStroke.Transparency = 0.35
        local barGrad = mk("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 35)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            }),
            Rotation = 0,
        }, blackBar)

        local fpsLabel = mk("TextLabel", {
            Text = "-- FPS",
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 240),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
            ZIndex = 22,
        }, blackBar)
        do
            local frames, lastT = 0, os.clock()
            RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                frames = frames + 1
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - lastT >= 0.4 then
                    local fps = math.floor(frames / (now - lastT) + 0.5)
                    fpsLabel.Text = tostring(fps) .. " FPS"
                    if fps >= 55 then
                        fpsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137)
                    elseif fps >= 30 then
                        fpsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60)
                    else
                        fpsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
                    end
                    frames = 0
                    lastT = now
                end
            end)
        end

        local hideBtn = mk("TextButton", {
            Text = "HIDE",
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = T.text,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 20),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -10),
            ZIndex = 23,
        }, blackBar)
        round(hideBtn, 7)
        stroke(hideBtn, T.border, 1)
        pcall(function()
            if type(zenoFloatBar) == "function" then
                zenoFloatBar(blackBar, { amp = 2.2, period = 2.2, shimmer = true })
            elseif type(_G.ZenoFloatBar) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoFloatBar(blackBar, { amp = 2.2, period = 2.2, shimmer = true })
            end
        end)

        local function setTab(name)
            activeTab = name
            for n, page in pairs(pages) do
                page.Visible = (n == name)
            end
            for n, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
                local on = (n == name)
                local accentBar = btn:FindFirstChild("AccentBar")
                local grad = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                local icon = btn:FindFirstChild("TabIcon")
                local lbl = btn:FindFirstChild("TabLabel")
                if on then
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 22, 33)
                    if st then st.Color = T.accent; st.Transparency = 0.15; st.Thickness = 1.4 end
                    if accentBar then accentBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0 end
                    if grad then grad.Enabled = true end
                    if icon then icon.TextColor3 = T.accent end
                    if lbl then lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end
                else
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19)
                    if st then st.Color = T.border; st.Transparency = 0.45; st.Thickness = 1 end
                    if accentBar then accentBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
                    if grad then grad.Enabled = false end
                    if icon then icon.TextColor3 = T.mute end
                    if lbl then lbl.TextColor3 = T.mute end
                end
            end
        end

        for i, name in ipairs(tabNames) do
            local b = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = "",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = T.mute,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
                LayoutOrder = i,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, tabRow)
            round(b, 10)
            local st = stroke(b, T.border, 1)
            st.Transparency = 0.45

            -- Left accent bar (visible when active)
            local accentBar = mk("Frame", {
                Name = "AccentBar",
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.62, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.19, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.accent,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, b)
            round(accentBar, 2)

            -- Soft gradient (enabled when active)
            local grad = mk("UIGradient", {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(52, 120, 200)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(14, 22, 38)),
                }),
                Rotation = 90,
                Enabled = false,
            }, b)

            local icon = mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "TabIcon",
                Text = tabIcons[name] or "•",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 14,
                TextColor3 = T.mute,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, b)

            local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {
                Name = "TabLabel",
                Text = tabShort[name] or name,
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = T.mute,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, b)

            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if activeTab ~= name then
                    TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(19, 25, 36) }):Play()
                end
            end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if activeTab ~= name then
                    TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(11, 14, 19) }):Play()
                end
            end)

            tabButtons[name] = b
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setTab(name) end)
        end

        -- Helpers for rows
        local function sectionLabel(parent, text, order)
            local f = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                LayoutOrder = order,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, parent)
            local lbl = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = text,
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = T.text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, f)
            local line = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 2),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.accent,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, f)
            round(line, 1)
            return f
        end

        local function makeToggleRow(parent, label, order, defaultOn, onChange)
            local row = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = order,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, parent)
            round(row, 9)
            stroke(row, T.border, 1)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = label,
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = T.text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            local state = defaultOn and true or false
            local btn = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = state and "ON" or "OFF",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = state and T.on or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 52),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 24),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0.5, -12),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            round(btn, 7)
            local function paint()
                btn.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = state and T.on or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 52)
            end
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                playClickSound()
                state = not state
                paint()
                if onChange then pcall(onChange, state) end
            end)
            return {
                Set = function(v) state = v and true or false; paint() end,
                Get = function() return state end,
                Row = row,
                Btn = btn,
            }
        end

        -- ========== PRINCIPALE ==========
        sectionLabel(pages.PRINCIPALE, "PANELS", 1)

        local panelDefs = {
            {key = "tp", label = "TP TO BEST", find = function()
                local g = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENOTPTOBEST")
                return g and g:FindFirstChild("MainPanel")
            end},

            end, cfg = "showNearest"},

                local g = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_ADMIN_PANEL")
                return g and g:FindFirstChild("Panel")
            end},
            {key = "finder", label = "FREE FINDER", find = function()
                return playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_FINDER") or (gethui and gethui() and gethui():FindFirstChild("ZENO_FINDER"))
                    or game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("ZENO_FINDER")
            end},
        }
        local panelState = {
            tp = Config.showTP ~= false,
            player = Config.showPlayer ~= false,
            invis = Config.showInvis ~= false,
            target = Config.showTarget ~= false,
            admin = Config.showAdmin == true,
            finder = Config.showFreeFinder == true,
            nearest = Config.showNearest ~= false,
        }

        local function setPanelVis(key, on)
            panelState[key] = on
            if key == "tp" then Config.showTP = on
            elseif key == "player" then Config.showPlayer = on
            elseif key == "invis" then Config.showInvis = on
            elseif key == "target" then Config.showTarget = on
            elseif key == "nearest" then Config.showNearest = on
            elseif key == "admin" then
                Config.showAdmin = on
                Config.adminPanelUi = on
            elseif key == "finder" then
                Config.showFreeFinder = on
                if type(_G.ZenoSetFreeFinderVisible) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoSetFreeFinderVisible, on)
                elseif on and type(_G.ZenoLoadFreeFinder) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoLoadFreeFinder)
                    task.defer(function()
                        if type(_G.ZenoSetFreeFinderVisible) == "function" then
                            pcall(_G.ZenoSetFreeFinderVisible, true)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            saveConfig()
            for _, d in ipairs(panelDefs) do
                if d.key == key then
                    if key == "finder" then
                        -- handled via ZenoSetFreeFinderVisible
                    else
                        local p = d.find()
                        if p then p.Visible = on end
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        for i, d in ipairs(panelDefs) do
            makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, d.label, 1 + i, panelState[d.key], function(on)
                setPanelVis(d.key, on)
            end)
        end

        sectionLabel(pages.PRINCIPALE, "TOOLS", 10)

        -- ESP / XRAY flags (visual hooks via globals)
        makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "ESP PLAYER", 11, Config.espPlayer == true, function(on)
            Config.espPlayer = on
            saveConfig()
            if type(_G.ZenoSetEspPlayer) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoSetEspPlayer(on)
            else
                _G.ZenoEspPlayer = on
            end
        end)
        makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "ESP BRAINROT", 12, Config.espBrainrot == true, function(on)
            Config.espBrainrot = on
            saveConfig()
            if type(_G.ZenoSetEspBrainrot) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoSetEspBrainrot(on)
            else
                _G.ZenoEspBrainrot = on
            end
        end)
        -- XRAY BASE (methode KOBY : dossiers cibles + Transparency, anti-ban)
        do
            local xrayOriginal = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local xrayConns = {}
            local xrayLoopId = 0
            local XRAY_ALPHA = 0.5
            local XRAY_FOLDERS = { "Base", "PlotSign", "FriendPanel", "Cash", "Laser", "Decorations", "Skin", "Unlock", "Purchases" }

            local function setTransparency(instance, alpha, loopId)
                if not instance or loopId ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                local function apply(obj)
                    if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
                    if xrayOriginal[obj] == nil then
                        xrayOriginal[obj] = (obj.Transparency == alpha) and 0 or obj.Transparency
                    end
                    local orig = xrayOriginal[obj]
                    if orig < 1 then
                        local target = orig + (1 - orig) * alpha
                        if math.abs(obj.Transparency - target) > 0.01 then
                            obj.Transparency = target
                        end
                    end
                end
                apply(instance)
                for i, child in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
                    apply(child)
                    if i % 400 == 0 then
                        if loopId ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function trackSubtree(root, alpha, loopId)
                if not root or loopId ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                setTransparency(root, alpha, loopId)
                xrayConns[#xrayConns + 1] = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                    if loopId == xrayLoopId then setTransparency(obj, alpha, loopId) end
                end)
            end

            local function processPlot(plot, alpha, loopId)
                if not plot or loopId ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                for _, fname in ipairs(XRAY_FOLDERS) do
                    trackSubtree(plot:FindFirstChild(fname), alpha, loopId)
                end
                xrayConns[#xrayConns + 1] = plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if loopId ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                    for _, fname in ipairs(XRAY_FOLDERS) do
                        if child.Name == fname then
                            trackSubtree(child, alpha, loopId)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end)
                local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                if not podiums then return end
                local function processPodium(podium)
                    for _, child in ipairs(podium:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "Claim" then
                            trackSubtree(child, alpha, loopId)
                        elseif child.Name == "Base" then
                            trackSubtree(child:FindFirstChild("Decorations"), alpha, loopId)
                        elseif child:IsA("Model") and child.Name ~= "Decorations" then
                            trackSubtree(child, alpha, loopId)
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                    processPodium(podium)
                end
                xrayConns[#xrayConns + 1] = podiums.ChildAdded:Connect(function(podium)
                    if loopId == xrayLoopId then processPodium(podium) end
                end)
            end

            local function enableXray()
                for _, c in ipairs(xrayConns) do
                    if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                    end
                end
                xrayConns = {}
                xrayLoopId = xrayLoopId + 1
                local id = xrayLoopId
                local plotsFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if not plotsFolder then return end
                for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if id ~= xrayLoopId then return end
                    processPlot(plot, XRAY_ALPHA, id)
                end
                xrayConns[#xrayConns + 1] = plotsFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot)
                    if id == xrayLoopId then processPlot(plot, XRAY_ALPHA, id) end
                end)
            end

            local function disableXray()
                for _, c in ipairs(xrayConns) do
                    if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                    end
                end
                xrayConns = {}
                xrayLoopId = xrayLoopId + 1
                local snapshot = xrayOriginal
                xrayOriginal = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
                for obj, orig in pairs(snapshot) do
                    if typeof(obj) == "Instance" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function() obj.Transparency = orig end)
                    end
                end
            end

            _G.ZenoEnableXray = enableXray
            _G.ZenoDisableXray = disableXray

            makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "XRAY BASE", 13, Config.xrayBase == true, function(on)
                Config.xrayBase = on
                _G.ZenoXrayBase = on
                saveConfig()
                if on then
                    enableXray()
                else
                    disableXray()
                end
            end)
            if Config.xrayBase == true then
                task.defer(enableXray)
            end
        end

        -- LINE BASE : ligne vers ta base pour te reperer
        do
            local lineOn = false
            local lineConn = nil
            local beamFolder = nil

            local function clearLine()
                if lineConn then
                    pcall(function() lineConn:Disconnect() end)
                    lineConn = nil
                end
                if beamFolder then
                    pcall(function() beamFolder:Destroy() end)
                    beamFolder = nil
                end
            end

            local function findOwnPlot()
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if not plots then return nil end
                for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local isOwn = false
                    pcall(function()
                        if isOwnPlot then
                            isOwn = isOwnPlot(plot.Name)
                        end
                    end)
                    if not isOwn then
                        -- fallback: PlotSign text
                        pcall(function()
                            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                            if sign then
                                local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                                local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                                local text = label and label.Text or ""
                                local name = player.Name
                                local disp = player.DisplayName
                                if text:lower():find(name:lower(), 1, true) or text:lower():find(disp:lower(), 1, true) then
                                    isOwn = true
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    if isOwn then return plot end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function startLine()
                clearLine()
                lineOn = true
                beamFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
                beamFolder.Name = "ZenoLineBase"
                beamFolder.Parent = Workspace

                local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                local part0 = Instance.new("Part")
                part0.Name = "ZenoLineA"
                part0.Anchored = true
                part0.CanCollide = false
                part0.CanQuery = false
                part0.Transparency = 1
                part0.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
                part0.Parent = beamFolder
                att0.Parent = part0

                local part1 = Instance.new("Part")
                part1.Name = "ZenoLineB"
                part1.Anchored = true
                part1.CanCollide = false
                part1.CanQuery = false
                part1.Transparency = 1
                part1.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
                part1.Parent = beamFolder
                att1.Parent = part1

                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                beam.Attachment0 = att0
                beam.Attachment1 = att1
                beam.Width0 = 0.35
                beam.Width1 = 0.35
                beam.FaceCamera = true
                beam.LightEmission = 1
                beam.LightInfluence = 0
                beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 255), Color3.fromRGB(170, 80, 255))
                beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.15)
                beam.Parent = part0

                local lineLast = 0
                lineConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local _n = os.clock()
                    if _n - lineLast < 0.12 then return end
                    lineLast = _n
                    if not lineOn then return end
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then return end
                    local plot = findOwnPlot()
                    local targetPos
                    if plot then
                        local okp, pivot = pcall(function() return plot:GetPivot().Position end)
                        if okp and pivot then
                            targetPos = pivot + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                        end
                    end
                    if not targetPos then
                        -- fallback: nearest PlotSign with your name already checked; else hide beam
                        beam.Enabled = false
                        return
                    end
                    beam.Enabled = true
                    part0.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
                    part1.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                end)
            end

            makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "LINE BASE", 13.5, Config.lineBase == true, function(on)
                Config.lineBase = on
                _G.ZenoLineBase = on
                saveConfig()
                if on then
                    startLine()
                else
                    lineOn = false
                    clearLine()
                end
            end)
            if Config.lineBase == true then
                task.defer(startLine)
            end
        end

        -- CARPET SPEED [FUSION ZENO 80 + ROSE 120 + BUY]
        local carpetSpeedOn = Config.carpetSpeedOn == true
        local carpetSpeedVal = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.carpetSpeedVal) or 80, 20, 200)
        local carpetBuySpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.carpetBuySpeed) or 70, 20, 200) -- NEW de ROSE
        local carpetMinGen = Config.carpetMinGen or "" -- NEW de ROSE
        local carpetAutoBuy = Config.carpetAutoBuy == true -- NEW de ROSE
        local carpetConn = nil

        local function stopCarpetSpeed()
            carpetSpeedOn = false
            if carpetConn then carpetConn:Disconnect(); carpetConn = nil end
            _G.JAF_CarpetSpeedActive = false
        end

        local function startCarpetSpeed()
            stopCarpetSpeed()
            carpetSpeedOn = true
            _G.JAF_CarpetSpeedActive = true
            carpetConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not carpetSpeedOn then return end
                -- ROSE: disable pendant steal/TP
                if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") or _G.JAF_TpActive then
                    return
                end
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

                -- ROSE: auto-equip carpet si pas équipé
                local hasCarpet = false
                for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES or {"Flying Carpet", "Carpet", "Magic Carpet", "Witch's Broom", "Cloud"}) do
                    if char:FindFirstChild(n) then hasCarpet = true; break end
                end
                if not hasCarpet then
                    local bp = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                    if bp then
                        for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES or {"Flying Carpet", "Carpet", "Magic Carpet"}) do
                            local tool = bp:FindFirstChild(n)
                            if tool then
                                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
                                hasCarpet = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not hasCarpet then return end

                local move = hum.MoveDirection
                if move.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    -- FUSION: utilise carpetSpeedVal de Zeno (20-200) au lieu de 120 fixe de Rose
                    local spd = math.clamp(carpetSpeedVal, 20, 200)
                    -- ROSE: AssemblyLinearVelocity pour bypass anti-cheat
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                        move.X * spd,
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,
                        move.Z * spd
                    )
                    
                    -- ROSE BONUS: AutoBuy en carpet si activé
                    if carpetAutoBuy then
                        pcall(function()
                            -- Check MinGen si défini
                            if carpetMinGen ~= "" then
                                local minVal = tonumber(carpetMinGen) or 0
                                -- Ici ton check de gen existant
                            end
                            -- Trigger autoBuy existant
                            if Config.autoBuy and tickAutoBuy then
                                -- tick déjà en heartbeat séparé
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "CARPET SPEED [ROSE FUSION]", 14, carpetSpeedOn, function(on)
            Config.carpetSpeedOn = on
            saveConfig()
            if on then startCarpetSpeed() else stopCarpetSpeed() end
        end)
        if carpetSpeedOn then task.defer(startCarpetSpeed) end

        -- Slider carpet speed (Zeno original 20-200) - GARDÉ
        do
            local row = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = 15,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, pages.PRINCIPALE)
            round(row, 9)
            stroke(row, T.border, 1)
            local valLbl = mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "SPEED  " .. tostring(carpetSpeedVal) .. " [ROSE: 120]",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = T.mute,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            local track = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 6),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 22),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.field2,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            round(track, 3)
            local fill = mk("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = T.accent,
                Size = UDim2.new((carpetSpeedVal - 20) / 180, 0, 1, 0),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 24,
            }, track)
            round(fill, 4)
            mk("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(T.accent, T.accent2), Rotation = 0}, fill)
            local knob = mk("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14),
                Position = UDim2.new((carpetSpeedVal - 20) / 180, -7, 0.5, -7),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 25,
            }, track)
            round(knob, 7)
            stroke(knob, T.accent, 1.2)
            local dragging = false
            local function setFromX(x)
                local a = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
                carpetSpeedVal = math.floor(20 + a * 180 + 0.5)
                Config.carpetSpeedVal = carpetSpeedVal
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
                knob.Position = UDim2.new(a, -7, 0.5, -7)
                valLbl.Text = "SPEED  " .. tostring(carpetSpeedVal) .. " [ROSE: 120]"
            end
            track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    setFromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    setFromX(i.Position.X)
                end
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    if dragging then
                        Config.carpetSpeedVal = carpetSpeedVal
                        saveConfig()
                    end
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end

        -- CARPET AUTO BUY [ROSE] + BUY SPEED removed from UI

        -- UNWALK (no animations)

        local unwalkOn = Config.unwalkOn == true
        local unwalkConn = nil
        local function setUnwalk(on)
            unwalkOn = on
            Config.unwalkOn = on
            saveConfig()
            if unwalkConn then unwalkConn:Disconnect(); unwalkConn = nil end
            local char = player.Character
            if on then
                pcall(function()
                    if disableAllAnimations then disableAllAnimations(char) end
                end)
                unwalkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not unwalkOn then return end
                    local c = player.Character
                    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hum then return end
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                            t:Stop(0)
                        end
                        local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                        if animator then
                            for _, t in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                                t:Stop(0)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if enableAllAnimations then enableAllAnimations(player.Character) end
                end)
            end
        end
        makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "UNWALK (NO ANIM)", 16, unwalkOn, setUnwalk)
        if unwalkOn then task.defer(function() setUnwalk(true) end) end

        -- ===== EXTRA TOOLS =====
        sectionLabel(pages.PRINCIPALE, "EXTRA", 20)

        -- Notifications + steal counter
        local notifGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        notifGui.Name = "ZENO_NOTIFS"
        notifGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        notifGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        notifGui.DisplayOrder = 1200
        notifGui.Parent = playerGui
        local notifList = mk("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 220),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0, 40),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ZIndex = 50,
        }, notifGui)
        mk("UIListLayout", {
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
            HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
            VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top,
        }, notifList)

        local function zenoNotify(text, color)
            text = tostring(text or "")
            color = color or Color3.fromRGB(95, 175, 255)
            local card = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 51,
            }, notifList)
            round(card, 10)
            stroke(card, color, 1.3)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = text,
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 12,
                TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                ZIndex = 52,
            }, card)
            card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.05}):Play()
            task.delay(2.4, function()
                if not card.Parent then return end
                local t = TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
                t:Play()
                t.Completed:Connect(function()
                    pcall(function() card:Destroy() end)
                end)
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoNotify = zenoNotify

        local stealCount = tonumber(Config.stealCount) or 0
        local stealCountLbl = mk("TextLabel", {
            Text = "STEALS: " .. tostring(stealCount),
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = T.mute,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
            LayoutOrder = 21,
            ZIndex = 22,
        }, pages.PRINCIPALE)

        local function bumpSteal(reason)
            stealCount = stealCount + 1
            Config.stealCount = stealCount
            saveConfig()
            stealCountLbl.Text = "STEALS: " .. tostring(stealCount)
            zenoNotify("Steal +" .. tostring(stealCount) .. (reason and (" • " .. reason) or ""), Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137))
        end
        _G.ZenoBumpSteal = bumpSteal

        -- Auto kick UNIQUEMENT si steal CONFIRME (texte "You stole" / livré en base)
        -- PAS quand on se fait taper / perdre le brainrot en chemin
        local stealStartClock = 0
        local stealStartHealth = 0
        local lastSuccessKick = 0
        local pendingSteal = false

        local function doAutoKickAfterSteal(success)
            if not success then return end
            if Config.autoKickAfterSteal ~= true and Config.psOnSteal ~= true then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastSuccessKick < 3 then return end -- anti double-kick
            lastSuccessKick = now
            local delay = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.autoKickDelay) or 0.8, 0.1, 5)
            local usePs = Config.psOnSteal == true and tostring(Config.privateServerCode or "") ~= ""
            if usePs then
                zenoNotify("Steal OK → PS in " .. string.format("%.1f", delay) .. "s…", Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255))
            elseif Config.autoKickAfterSteal == true then
                zenoNotify("Steal OK → Kick in " .. string.format("%.1f", delay) .. "s…", Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100))
            else
                return
            end
            task.delay(delay, function()
                if usePs and type(_G.ZenoPsOnSteal) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoPsOnSteal)
                elseif Config.autoKickAfterSteal == true then
                    pcall(function()
                        player:Kick("ZENO Auto Kick After Steal")
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoAutoKickAfterSteal = doAutoKickAfterSteal

        local function onConfirmedStealSuccess(reason)
            if not pendingSteal and (os.clock() - (stealStartClock or 0)) > 30 then
                -- pas de steal en cours depuis longtemps : ignore faux positifs
            end
            pendingSteal = false
            _G._ZenoRetpTries = 0
            bumpSteal(reason or "confirmed")
            doAutoKickAfterSteal(true)
            -- PS handled inside doAutoKickAfterSteal when psOnSteal + code set
        end
        _G.ZenoConfirmStealSuccess = onConfirmedStealSuccess

        -- 1) Detection principale : message UI "You stole" (seul vrai succes)
        do
            local seen = {}
            local function scanSuccessTexts(root)
                if not root then return end
                for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local t = (d.Text or ""):lower()
                        if t ~= "" and (
                            t:find("you stole", 1, true)
                            or t:find("stole a", 1, true)
                            or t:find("successfully stole", 1, true)
                            or t:find("has been added", 1, true)
                            or t:find("added to your", 1, true)
                        ) then
                            local key = t .. tostring(d:GetFullName())
                            if not seen[key] then
                                seen[key] = true
                                onConfirmedStealSuccess("gui")
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                while true do
                    task.wait(0.9)
                    if Config.autoKickAfterSteal == true or Config.psOnSteal == true then
                        pcall(function() scanSuccessTexts(playerGui) end)
                    end
                    -- prune seen keys occasionally
                    if os.clock() % 20 < 0.4 then seen = {} end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                playerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                    if not (Config.autoKickAfterSteal == true or Config.psOnSteal == true) then return end
                    task.defer(function()
                        pcall(function()
                            if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
                                local t = (d.Text or ""):lower()
                                if t:find("you stole", 1, true) or t:find("stole a", 1, true)
                                    or t:find("successfully stole", 1, true)
                                    or t:find("has been added", 1, true) then
                                    onConfirmedStealSuccess("gui-live")
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end

        -- 2) Attribute Stealing : track only, NEVER kick on cancel / hit
        do
            local wasStealing = false
            local function onStealingChanged(on)
                on = on and true or false
                if on and not wasStealing then
                    stealStartClock = os.clock()
                    pendingSteal = true
                    local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    stealStartHealth = hum and hum.Health or 0
                elseif wasStealing and not on then
                    -- Fin de Stealing sans confirmation GUI = cancel / frappe / drop
                    -- On ne kick PAS
                    local dur = os.clock() - (stealStartClock or 0)
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hp = hum and hum.Health or 0
                    local maxHp = hum and hum.MaxHealth or 100
                    if dur < 1.0 then
                        zenoNotify("Steal cancel — pas de kick", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60))
                    elseif hp <= 0 then
                        zenoNotify("Steal interrupt (mort) — pas de kick", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60))
                    elseif stealStartHealth > 0 and (stealStartHealth - hp) >= math.max(8, maxHp * 0.08) then
                        zenoNotify("Steal interrupt (frappe) — pas de kick", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60))
                    else
                        -- Possible drop sans frappe visible : toujours pas de kick sans conf GUI
                        zenoNotify("Steal rate — pas de kick", Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 200))
                    end
                    pendingSteal = false
                end
                wasStealing = on
            end

            local function hookChar(char)
                if not char then return end
                char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                    onStealingChanged(char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
                end)
            end
            if player.Character then hookChar(player.Character) end
            player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                wasStealing = false
                pendingSteal = false
                stealStartClock = 0
                stealStartHealth = 0
                task.wait(0.2)
                hookChar(c)
            end)
            pcall(function()
                player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                    onStealingChanged(player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
                end)
            end)
        end

        -- WALK SPEED
        local walkOn = Config.walkSpeedOn == true
        local walkVal = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.walkSpeedVal) or 28, 16, 100)
        local walkConn = nil
        local function stopWalk()
            walkOn = false
            if walkConn then walkConn:Disconnect(); walkConn = nil end
            pcall(function()
                local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end
            end)
        end
        local function startWalk()
            stopWalk()
            walkOn = true
            walkConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not walkOn then return end
                local char = player.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                local want = math.clamp(walkVal, 16, 100)
                if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    -- boost via CFrame (moins détecté que WalkSpeed hard)
                    hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (hum.MoveDirection * math.max(want - 16, 0) * dt)
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetWalkSpeed = function(on)
            on = on and true or false
            Config.walkSpeedOn = on
            walkVal = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.walkSpeedVal) or walkVal, 16, 100)
            saveConfig()
            if on then startWalk() else stopWalk() end
        end
        -- WALK SPEED removed from UI
