        -- DROP BRAINROT (bouton action)
        do
            local row = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = 24,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, pages.PRINCIPALE)
            round(row, 9)
            stroke(row, T.border, 1)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = "DROP BRAINROT",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = T.text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            local btn = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = "DROP",
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 24),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -12),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            round(btn, 7)
            local dropBusy = false
            local function dropBrainrot()
                if dropBusy then return end
                dropBusy = true
                zenoNotify("Dropping brainrot...", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 90))
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        -- stop invis si actif
                        if type(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal) == "function" and (_G.invisibleStealEnabled == true) then
                            _G.toggleInvisibleSteal()
                            task.wait(0.15)
                        end
                    end)
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    pcall(function() if hum then hum:UnequipTools() end end)
                    -- petit burst pour drop server-side
                    if hrp then
                        local t0 = os.clock()
                        while os.clock() - t0 < 0.25 do
                            local vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            if vel.Magnitude < 0.1 then vel = hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 60 end
                            local boom = vel * 8000 + Vector3.new(0, 8000, 0)
                            pcall(function()
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = boom
                            end)
                            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                            pcall(function()
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
                            end)
                            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                        pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
                    end
                    dropBusy = false
                    zenoNotify("Drop done", Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90))
                end)
            end
            _G.ZenoDropBrainrot = dropBrainrot
            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(dropBrainrot)
        end

        -- LINE TO BRAINROT
        do
            local lineOn = Config.lineBrainrot == true
            local lineConn = nil
            local beamFolder = nil
            local function clearLine()
                if lineConn then pcall(function() lineConn:Disconnect() end) lineConn = nil end
                if beamFolder then pcall(function() beamFolder:Destroy() end) beamFolder = nil end
            end
            local function startLine()
                clearLine()
                lineOn = true
                beamFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
                beamFolder.Name = "ZenoLineBrainrot"
                beamFolder.Parent = Workspace
                local part0 = Instance.new("Part")
                part0.Anchored = true
                part0.CanCollide = false
                part0.CanQuery = false
                part0.Transparency = 1
                part0.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
                part0.Parent = beamFolder
                local part1 = Instance.new("Part")
                part1.Anchored = true
                part1.CanCollide = false
                part1.CanQuery = false
                part1.Transparency = 1
                part1.Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
                part1.Parent = beamFolder
                local att0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                att0.Parent = part0
                local att1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                att1.Parent = part1
                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                beam.Attachment0 = att0
                beam.Attachment1 = att1
                beam.Width0 = 0.4
                beam.Width1 = 0.4
                beam.FaceCamera = true
                beam.LightEmission = 1
                beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 40), Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 160))
                beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.1)
                beam.Parent = part0
                local lineLast = 0
                lineConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local _n = os.clock()
                    if _n - lineLast < 0.12 then return end
                    lineLast = _n
                    if not lineOn then return end
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then beam.Enabled = false return end
                    local target = SelectedStealTarget
                    local pos = target and target.position
                    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                        -- try resolve from plot/slot
                        pcall(function()
                            if target and target.plot and target.slot then
                                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                                local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(target.plot))
                                local pods = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                                local podium = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(tostring(target.slot))
                                if podium then
                                    local cf = podium:GetPivot()
                                    pos = cf.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    else
                        pos = pos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                    end
                    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                        beam.Enabled = false
                        return
                    end
                    beam.Enabled = true
                    part0.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
                    part1.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                end)
            end
            makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "LINE BRAINROT", 25, lineOn, function(on)
                Config.lineBrainrot = on
                saveConfig()
                if on then startLine() else lineOn = false clearLine() end
                zenoNotify(on and "Line Brainrot ON" or "Line Brainrot OFF", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60))
            end)
            if lineOn then task.defer(startLine) end
        end

        -- FACE AWAY (dos au owner pendant steal)
        do
            local faOn = Config.faceAway == true
            local faConn = nil
            local autoRot = nil
            local function hrpOf(p)
                local c = p and p.Character
                return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            end
            local function ownerNearBase()
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                local me = hrpOf(player)
                if not plots or not me then return nil end
                local bestPlot, bestDist = nil, math.huge
                for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    local pos = sign and ((sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign.Position) or (sign.PrimaryPart and sign.PrimaryPart.Position))
                    if pos then
                        local d = (me.Position - pos).Magnitude
                        if d < bestDist then bestDist, bestPlot = d, plot end
                    end
                end
                if not bestPlot or bestDist > 120 then return nil end
                local ownerName = nil
                pcall(function()
                    local sign = bestPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    local gui = sign and sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                    local label = gui and gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                    local text = label and label.Text or ""
                    if text ~= "" and not text:lower():find("empty") then
                        ownerName = text:match("^(.+)'s Base$") or text
                    end
                end)
                if not ownerName then return nil end
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= player and (p.Name == ownerName or p.DisplayName == ownerName) then
                        return p
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end
            local function stopFA()
                if faConn then faConn:Disconnect(); faConn = nil end
                pcall(function()
                    local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and autoRot ~= nil then hum.AutoRotate = autoRot end
                end)
                autoRot = nil
            end
            local function startFA()
                stopFA()
                faOn = true
                faConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not faOn then return end
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not hrp or not hum then return end
                    local stealing = char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                        or player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                    if not stealing then
                        if autoRot ~= nil then
                            hum.AutoRotate = autoRot
                            autoRot = nil
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    local target = ownerNearBase()
                    local th = hrpOf(target)
                    if not th then return end
                    if autoRot == nil then
                        autoRot = hum.AutoRotate
                        hum.AutoRotate = false
                    end
                    local dir = th.Position - hrp.Position
                    dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                    if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
                    -- face away = look opposite
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position - dir.Unit * 10)
                end)
            end
            makeToggleRow(pages.PRINCIPALE, "FACE AWAY", 26, faOn, function(on)
                Config.faceAway = on
                saveConfig()
                if on then startFA() else faOn = false stopFA() end
                zenoNotify(on and "Face Away ON" or "Face Away OFF", Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 255))
            end)
            if faOn then task.defer(startFA) end
        end

        -- Config profiles A/B/C
        sectionLabel(pages.PARAMETRES, "PROFILS CONFIG", 5)
        do
            local row = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                LayoutOrder = 6,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, pages.PARAMETRES)
            mk("UIListLayout", {
                FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
                Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
                SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            }, row)

            local function profilePath(name)
                return CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/profile_" .. name .. ".json"
            end
            local function saveProfile(name)
                pcall(function()
                    if type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" then
                        if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) end
                    end
                    if type(writefile) == "function" then
                        Config.activeProfile = name
                        writefile(profilePath(name), HttpService:JSONEncode(Config))
                        saveConfig()
                    end
                    if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Profile " .. name .. " saved", Color3.fromRGB(56, 224, 137)) end
                end)
            end
            local function loadProfile(name)
                pcall(function()
                    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
                    if not isfile(profilePath(name)) then
                        if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Profile " .. name .. " empty", Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 60)) end
                        return
                    end
                    local d = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(profilePath(name)))
                    if type(d) ~= "table" then return end
                    for k, v in pairs(d) do
                        Config[k] = v
                    end
                    Config.activeProfile = name
                    saveConfig()
                    if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Profile " .. name .. " loaded • re-execute", Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255)) end
                end)
            end

            for i, name in ipairs({"A", "B", "C"}) do
                local b = mk("TextButton", {
                    Text = "SAVE " .. name,
                    Font = FONT_BOLD,
                    TextSize = 10,
                    TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 32),
                    LayoutOrder = i,
                    ZIndex = 23,
                }, row)
                round(b, 8)
                stroke(b, T.border, 1)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    playClickSound()
                    saveProfile(name)
                end)
            end

                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                LayoutOrder = 7,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, pages.PARAMETRES)

                    TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    BackgroundColor3 = T.accent,
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 32),
                    LayoutOrder = i,
                    ZIndex = 23,
                }, row2)
                round(b, 8)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    playClickSound()
                    loadProfile(name)
                end)
            end
        end

            while sessLbl and sessLbl.Parent do
                local sec = math.floor(os.clock() - t0)
                local m = math.floor(sec / 60)
                local s2 = sec % 60
                sessLbl.Text = string.format("SESSION  %02d:%02d  •  STEALS %d", m, s2, tonumber(Config.stealCount) or 0)
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end)

        -- Interactive keybind rows for main ones
        local function keyRow(parent, label, getKey, setKey, order)
            local row = mk("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
                BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = order,
                ZIndex = 22,
            }, parent)
            round(row, 9)
            stroke(row, T.border, 1)
            mk("TextLabel", {
                Text = label,
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = T.text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            local kb = mk("TextButton", {
                Text = tostring(getKey()),
                Font = FONT_BOLD,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = T.text,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 40),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 24),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, -12),
                ZIndex = 23,
            }, row)
            round(kb, 7)
            stroke(kb, T.border, 1)
            local listening = false
            kb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if listening then return end
                listening = true
                kb.Text = "..."
                local conn
                conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                        local k = input.KeyCode.Name
                        if k ~= "Unknown" then
                            setKey(k)
                            kb.Text = k
                            listening = false
                            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                        end
                    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                        listening = false
                        kb.Text = tostring(getKey())
                        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        keyRow(pages.KEYBINDS, "Manual TP", function() return Config.keybind or "T" end, function(k)

        end, 2)

        local function toggleFlag(key, setterName, label)
            local on = not (Config[key] == true)
            Config[key] = on
            saveConfig()
            local setter = setterName and _G[setterName]
            if type(setter) == "function" then pcall(setter, on) end
            if type(_G.ZenoNotify) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoNotify((label or key) .. " " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF"), Color3.fromRGB(170, 140, 255))
            end
        end

        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if gp then return end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
            local name = input.KeyCode.Name
            if name == (Config.dropKey or "B") then
                if type(_G.ZenoDropBrainrot) == "function" then task.spawn(_G.ZenoDropBrainrot) end
            elseif name == (Config.walkKey or "V") then
                toggleFlag("walkSpeedOn", "ZenoSetWalkSpeed", "Walk")
            elseif name == (Config.faceAwayKey or "F") then
                toggleFlag("faceAway", "ZenoSetFaceAway", "Face Away")
            elseif name == (Config.floatKey or "Z") then
                toggleFlag("floatOn", "ZenoSetFloat", "Slow Fall")
            elseif name == (Config.autoStealKey or "G") then
                toggleFlag("autoSteal", "ZenoSetAutoSteal", "Auto Steal")
                -- fallback
                if type(_G.ZenoSetAutoSteal) ~= "function" then
                    Config.autoSteal = Config.autoSteal == true
                end
            elseif name == (Config.autoBuyKey or "K") then
                toggleFlag("autoBuy", "ZenoSetAutoBuy", "Auto Buy")
            elseif name == (Config.invisKey or "U") then
                toggleFlag("invisOnSteal", nil, "Invis Steal")
                if type(_G.ZenoSetInvis) == "function" then pcall(_G.ZenoSetInvis, Config.invisOnSteal) end
            elseif name == (Config.xrayKey or "X") then
                local on = not (Config.xrayBase == true)
                Config.xrayBase = on
                saveConfig()
                if on and type(_G.ZenoEnableXray) == "function" then pcall(_G.ZenoEnableXray)
                elseif not on and type(_G.ZenoDisableXray) == "function" then pcall(_G.ZenoDisableXray) end
                if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("XRAY " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")) end
            elseif name == (Config.espPlayerKey or "Y") then
                toggleFlag("espPlayer", "ZenoSetEspPlayer", "ESP Player")
            elseif name == (Config.espBrainrotKey or "H") then
                toggleFlag("espBrainrot", "ZenoSetEspBrainrot", "ESP Brainrot")
            elseif name == (Config.antiRagdollKey or "R") then
                toggleFlag("antiRagdoll", "ZenoSetAntiRagdoll", "Anti Ragdoll")
            elseif name == (Config.antiCollisionKey or "C") then
                toggleFlag("antiCollision", "ZenoSetAntiCollision", "Anti Collision")
            elseif name == (Config.autoTurretKey or "J") then
                toggleFlag("autoTurret", "ZenoSetAutoTurret", "Auto Turret")
            elseif name == (Config.unlockDoorKey or "L") then
                toggleFlag("unlockDoor", "ZenoSetUnlockDoor", "Unlock Door")
            elseif name == (Config.adminPanelKey or "N") then
                local on = not (Config.showAdmin == true)
                Config.showAdmin = on
                Config.adminPanelUi = on
                saveConfig()
                if type(_G.ZenoSetAdminPanelVisible) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoSetAdminPanelVisible, on)
                end
                if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Admin Panel " .. (on and "ON" or "OFF")) end
            elseif name == (Config.hubToggleKey or "RightControl") then
                Config.hubHidden = not (Config.hubHidden == true)
                saveConfig()
                if type(_G.ZenoSetHubVisible) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoSetHubVisible, not Config.hubHidden)
                elseif type(setMainVisible) == "function" then
                    pcall(setMainVisible, not Config.hubHidden)
                end
                if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("Hub " .. (Config.hubHidden and "HIDDEN" or "SHOWN")) end
            elseif name == (Config.carpetSpeedKey or "M") then
                toggleFlag("carpetSpeedOn", "ZenoSetCarpetSpeed", "Carpet Speed")
            end
        end)

        -- Resize arrow bottom right
        local resizeBtn = mk("TextButton", {
            Name = "ZENO_RESIZE_ARROW",
            Text = "↘",
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = T.mute,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            BackgroundColor3 = T.field,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6),
            ZIndex = 40,
        }, main)
        round(resizeBtn, 6)
        stroke(resizeBtn, T.border, 1)
        local scales = {0.85, 1.0, 1.15, 1.3}
        local scaleIdx = 2
        do
            local hs = tonumber(Config.hubScale) or 1
            for i, s in ipairs(scales) do
                if math.abs(s - hs) < 0.01 then scaleIdx = i break end
            end
            scaleObj.Scale = scales[scaleIdx]
        end
        resizeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            scaleIdx = scaleIdx % #scales + 1
            Config.hubScale = scales[scaleIdx]
            saveConfig()
            TweenService:Create(scaleObj, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Scale = scales[scaleIdx]}):Play()
        end)

        -- OPEN UI : fixe, SANS drag
        local openPos = Config.posOpenUI or {X=0.5, XOffset=0, Y=0, YOffset=80}
        local openBtn = mk("TextButton", {
            Text = "OPEN UI",
            Font = FONT_BOLD,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 20),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 34),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
            Position = posToUDim2(openPos),
            Visible = false,
            ZIndex = 50,
        }, openGui)
        round(openBtn, 10)
        stroke(openBtn, Color3.fromRGB(95, 175, 255), 1.3)

        local function setMainVisible(vis)
            main.Visible = vis
            openBtn.Visible = not vis
            Config.hubHidden = not vis
            saveConfig()
        end
        _G.ZenoSetMainVisible = setMainVisible
        hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMainVisible(false) end)
        openBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMainVisible(true) end)

        -- Drag main (hub principal uniquement)
        do
            local dragging, startInput, startPos
            local function isPtr(i)
                return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            end
            header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                if not isPtr(i) then return end
                if i.Target and i.Target:IsA("TextButton") then return end
                dragging = true
                startInput = i.Position
                startPos = main.Position
            end)
            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                if not dragging then return end
                if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                local d = i.Position - startInput
                main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end)
            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                if isPtr(i) then
                    if dragging then
                        Config.posPrincipal = udim2ToPos(main.Position)
                        saveConfig()
                    end
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end

        local function applyTheme(_name)
            -- Glass Noir only
            T = Themes.Noir
            themeName = "Noir"
            Config.themeName = "Noir"
            pcall(function()
                main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.38
            end)
        end

        setTab("PRINCIPALE")
        applyTheme("Noir")
        setMainVisible(Config.hubHidden ~= true)
    end)

    if not ok then
        warn("[ZENO UI PRINCIPAL] " .. tostring(err))
        print("[ZENO UI PRINCIPAL] ERROR:", err)
    end
end)


-- ================================================================
-- ZENO EXTRA MODULES (Subspace Mine ESP, Anti-Ragdoll, Anti-Bee/Disco,
-- Anti-Collision, Clean Error GUIs, Auto Unlock On Steal, PS On Steal,
-- Priority Sound Alert, Admin Panel + Click/Proximity AP)
-- ================================================================
task.spawn(function()
local okExtra, errExtra = pcall(function()
    local AP_ALL = {"balloon","inverse","jail","jumpscare","morph","nightvision","ragdoll","rocket","tiny"}
    local AP_EMOJI = {balloon="🎈",inverse="🔄",jail="🔒",jumpscare="👻",morph="🎭",nightvision="🌙",ragdoll="🌀",rocket="🚀",tiny="🐜"}
    local AP_CD = {ragdoll=30,jail=60,rocket=120,balloon=30,inverse=30,jumpscare=30,tiny=30,morph=30,nightvision=30}
    local apLast = {}

    local function apOnCd(cmd)
        local t = apLast[cmd] or 0
        local cd = AP_CD[cmd] or 30
        return (os.clock() - t) < cd
    end
    local function apMark(cmd)
        apLast[cmd] = os.clock()
    end

    -- ---------- runAdminCommand (chat-based AP) ----------
    local function runAdminCommand(targetPlr, cmd)
        if not targetPlr or not cmd then return false end
        if apOnCd(cmd) then
            if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify(cmd .. " en cooldown", Color3.fromRGB(255,180,60)) end
            return false
        end
        local prefix = tostring(Config.apCmdPrefix or "")
        local msg
        if prefix ~= "" then
            msg = prefix .. cmd .. " " .. targetPlr.Name
        else
            msg = cmd .. " " .. targetPlr.Name
        end
        local ok = false
        pcall(function()
            local TCS = game:GetService("TextChatService")
            local channel = TCS and TCS.TextChannels and (TCS.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral") or TCS.TextChannels:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextChannel"))
            if channel and channel.SendAsync then
                channel:SendAsync(msg)
                ok = true
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            pcall(function()
                local chat = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
                local say = chat and chat:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
                if say then
                    say:FireServer(msg, "All")
                    ok = true
                end
            end)
        end
        if not ok then
            pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", { Text = "[ZENO AP] " .. msg })
            end)
            -- last resort: try Net fire if present
            pcall(function()
                if _G.Net and _G.Net.FireServer then
                    _G.Net:FireServer("AdminCommand", cmd, targetPlr.UserId)
                    ok = true
                end
            end)
        end
        if ok then
            apMark(cmd)
            if _G.ZenoNotify then
                _G.ZenoNotify((AP_EMOJI[cmd] or "⚡") .. " " .. cmd .. " → " .. (targetPlr.DisplayName or targetPlr.Name), Color3.fromRGB(170,80,255))
            end
        end
        return ok
    end
    _G.ZenoRunAdminCommand = runAdminCommand

    -- ---------- Subspace Mine ESP ----------
    do
        local on = false
        local data = {}
        local conn
        local function clear()
            for obj, e in pairs(data) do
                pcall(function() if e.sel then e.sel:Destroy() end end)
                pcall(function() if e.bb then e.bb:Destroy() end end)
            end
            data = {}
        end
        local function refresh()
            if not on then return end
            local tools = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ToolsAdds")
            if not tools then return end
            local seen = {}
            for _, obj in ipairs(tools:GetChildren()) do
                if obj.Name:match("SubspaceTripmine") and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                    seen[obj] = true
                    if not data[obj] then
                        local ownerName = obj.Name:match("SubspaceTripmine(.+)") or "Unknown"
                        local sel = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                        sel.Adornee = obj
                        sel.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(96, 175, 255)
                        sel.LineThickness = 0.05
                        sel.Parent = obj
                        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 40)
                        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        bb.Adornee = obj
                        bb.Parent = obj
                        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        lbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        lbl.Text = ownerName .. "'s Mine"
                        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(96, 175, 255)
                        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
                        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        lbl.TextSize = 13
                        lbl.Parent = bb
                        data[obj] = { sel = sel, bb = bb }
                    end
                end
            end
            for obj in pairs(data) do
                if not seen[obj] or not obj.Parent then
                    pcall(function() data[obj].sel:Destroy() end)
                    pcall(function() data[obj].bb:Destroy() end)
                    data[obj] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        local function setOn(v)
            on = v and true or false
            Config.subspaceMineEsp = on
            _G.ZenoSubspaceMineESP = on
            saveConfig()
            if not on then
                if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) conn = nil end
                clear()
                return
            end
            if not conn then
                local last = 0
                conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - last < 1 then return end
                    last = now
                    refresh()
                end)
            end
            task.defer(refresh)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetSubspaceMineESP = setOn
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(2)
            if Config.subspaceMineEsp == true then setOn(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Anti-Ragdoll ----------
    do
        local enabled = false
        local conns = {}
        local char, hum, hrp, animator
        local lastVel = Vector3.zero
        local function isRag()
            if not hum then return false end
            local s = hum:GetState()
            return s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
        end
        local function cleanup()
            if not char then return end
            pcall(function()
                for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or obj:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or obj:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
                        obj:Destroy()
                    elseif obj:IsA("Attachment") and (obj.Name == "A" or obj.Name == "B") then
                        obj:Destroy()
                    elseif obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
                        obj.Enabled = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            if animator then
                pcall(function()
                    for _, t in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        if t.Name and (t.Name:lower():find("ragdoll") or t.Name:lower():find("fall")) then
                            t:Stop(0)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if hum then
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
            pcall(function()
                if Workspace.CurrentCamera and hum then
                    Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                end
            end)
        end
        local function clearConns()
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            conns = {}
        end
        local function setup(c)
            clearConns()
            char = c
            if not c then return end
            hum = c:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
            hrp = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 8)
            animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if not enabled or not hum or not hrp then return end
            conns[#conns+1] = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function()
                if enabled and isRag() then
                    cleanup()
                    pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                end
            end)
            conns[#conns+1] = c.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
                if enabled and isRag() then cleanup() end
            end)
            conns[#conns+1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not enabled or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
                if isRag() then
                    cleanup()
                    local vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    if vel.Magnitude > 40 then
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel.Unit * math.min(vel.Magnitude, 18)
                    end
                    lastVel = vel
                end
            end)
        end
        local function setOn(v)
            enabled = v and true or false
            Config.antiRagdoll = enabled
            _G.AntiRagdollEnabled = enabled
            saveConfig()
            if not enabled then clearConns(); return end
            if player.Character then setup(player.Character) end
        end
        _G.ZenoSetAntiRagdoll = setOn
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
            task.wait(0.25)
            if enabled then setup(c) end
        end)
        if player.Character then setup(player.Character) end
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(1.5)
            if Config.antiRagdoll == true then setOn(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Anti-Bee & Anti-Disco ----------
    do
        local running = false
        local conns = {}
        local badNames = { Blue = true, DiscoEffect = true, BeeBlur = true, ColorCorrection = true, Disco = true, Bee = true }
        local function nuke(obj)
            if not obj then return end
            if badNames[obj.Name] then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
            if obj:IsA("BlurEffect") and (obj.Name:lower():find("bee") or obj.Name:lower():find("disco")) then
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end
            if obj:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and obj.Name ~= "ZenoCC" then
                -- only nuke if named like disco
                if obj.Name:lower():find("disco") or obj.Name:lower():find("bee") or obj.Name == "ColorCorrection" then
                    pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
        local function stop()
            running = false
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            conns = {}
        end
        local function start()
            stop()
            running = true
            for _, obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do nuke(obj) end
            conns[#conns+1] = Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if running then task.defer(nuke, obj) end
            end)
            -- block buzzing sound
            pcall(function()
                local bee = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") and player.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Bee", true)
                if bee then
                    local buzz = bee:FindFirstChild("Buzzing")
                    if buzz and buzz:IsA("Sound") then buzz.Volume = 0; buzz:Stop() end
                end
            end)
            do
                local lastBee = 0
                conns[#conns+1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not running then return end
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - lastBee < 1.2 then return end
                    lastBee = now
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, s in ipairs(SoundService:GetChildren()) do
                            if s:IsA("Sound") and (s.Name:lower():find("buzz") or s.Name:lower():find("bee") or s.Name:lower():find("disco")) then
                                s.Volume = 0
                                s:Stop()
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end
        local function setOn(v)
            Config.antiBeeDisco = v and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if Config.antiBeeDisco then start() else stop() end
        end
        _G.ZenoSetAntiBeeDisco = setOn
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(2)
            if Config.antiBeeDisco == true then setOn(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Anti-Collision ----------
    do
        local on = false
        local conns = {}
        local function passThrough(model)
            if not model or model == player.Character then return end
            for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then
                    pcall(function() d.CanCollide = false end)
                end
            end
        end
        local function isChar(m)
            return m and m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
        end
        local function stop()
            on = false
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            conns = {}
        end
        local function start()
            stop()
            on = true
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= player then
                    passThrough(p.Character)
                    conns[#conns+1] = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        if on then passThrough(c) end
                    end)
                end
            end
            conns[#conns+1] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
                conns[#conns+1] = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    if on then passThrough(c) end
                end)
            end)
            conns[#conns+1] = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if not on then return end
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local m = d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if m and m ~= player.Character and isChar(m) then
                        pcall(function() d.CanCollide = false end)
                    end
                elseif isChar(d) and d ~= player.Character then
                    task.defer(function() if on then passThrough(d) end end)
                end
            end)
        end
        local function setOn(v)
            Config.antiCollision = v and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if Config.antiCollision then start() else stop() end
        end
        _G.ZenoSetAntiCollision = setOn
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(2)
            if Config.antiCollision == true then setOn(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Clean Error GUIs ----------
    do
        local loop
        local function setOn(v)
            Config.cleanErrorGuis = v and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if not Config.cleanErrorGuis then return end
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.cleanErrorGuis do
                    pcall(function()
                        local gs = game:GetService("GuiService")
                        gs:ClearError()
                    end)
                    task.wait(1.0)
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetCleanErrorGuis = setOn
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(2)
            if Config.cleanErrorGuis == true then setOn(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Auto Unlock On Steal ----------
    do
        local function onStealChanged(stealing)
            if not stealing then return end
            if Config.autoUnlockOnSteal ~= true then return end
            task.defer(function()
                task.wait(0.1)
                if type(_G.ZenoUnlockNearby) == "function" then
                    pcall(_G.ZenoUnlockNearby)
                end
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                onStealChanged(player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
            end)
        end)
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
            task.wait(0.3)
            pcall(function()
                c:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                    onStealChanged(c:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
                end)
            end)
        end)
        if player.Character then
            pcall(function()
                player.Character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                    onStealChanged(player.Character:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
                end)
            end)
        end
    end

    -- ---------- PS On Steal (private server after successful steal) ----------
    do
        local function tryPsKick()
            if Config.psOnSteal ~= true then return end
            local code = tostring(Config.privateServerCode or "")
            local placeId = tonumber(Config.psPlaceId) or 109983668079237
            if code ~= "" then
                pcall(function()
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPrivateServer(placeId, code, {player})
                    end)
                    if not ok then
                        -- ExperienceService fallback (some executors)
                        pcall(function()
                            game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({
                                placeId = placeId,
                                linkCode = code,
                            })
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            else
                pcall(function() player:Kick("ZENO PS On Steal (no code set)") end)
            end
        end
        _G.ZenoPsOnSteal = tryPsKick
        -- hook after existing steal success if possible
        -- PS On Steal is triggered only from confirmed steal success (GUI "You stole")
    end


-- ================================================================
-- NEW FEATURES (Rose-inspired): Hit Recovery, Base Owner ESP, Group ESP,
-- Auto Reset Balloon, Grief Detector, Remove Anims, Anim Pack,
-- Float Steal, Sync Scan, FPS Optimizer, Kick After Buy
-- ================================================================
do
    -- ---------- FPS OPTIMIZER ----------
    local fpsOptOn = false
    local fpsOptConns = {}
    local function fpsStrip(obj, isOther)
        if not fpsOptOn then return end
        pcall(function()
            if obj.Name == "Overhead" then return end
            local cls = {"Shirt","Pants","ShirtGraphic","Accessory","Hat","HairAccessory",
                "FaceAccessory","NeckAccessory","ShoulderAccessory","FrontAccessory",
                "BackAccessory","WaistAccessory"}
            for _, c in ipairs(cls) do
                if obj:IsA(c) then obj:Destroy(); return end
            end
            if isOther and obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                local s = obj.Size
                if s.X > 10 or s.Y > 10 or s.Z > 10 then
                    obj.Transparency = 1
                    obj.CanCollide = false
                    obj.CanQuery = false
                    obj.CanTouch = false
                    obj.CastShadow = false
                end
            end
            if Config.fpsOptMeshes and obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                pcall(function() obj.TextureID = "" end)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function fpsChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        local isOther = (Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) ~= player)
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do fpsStrip(d, isOther) end
        end)
        char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) task.defer(fpsStrip, d, isOther) end)
    end
    local function setFpsOptimizer(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.fpsOptimizer = on
        fpsOptOn = on
        saveConfig()
        if not on then
            for _, c in ipairs(fpsOptConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            fpsOptConns = {}
            return
        end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr.Character then fpsChar(plr.Character) end
            fpsOptConns[#fpsOptConns+1] = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(fpsChar)
        end
        fpsOptConns[#fpsOptConns+1] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            fpsOptConns[#fpsOptConns+1] = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(fpsChar)
        end)
        if Config.fpsOptFloor then
            pcall(function()
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level05
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetFpsOptimizer = setFpsOptimizer
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if Config.fpsOptimizer == true then setFpsOptimizer(true) end
    end)

    -- ---------- REMOVE ANIMATIONS ----------
    local removeAnimsOn = false
    local removeAnimsConn = nil
    local function setRemoveAnims(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.removeAnimations = on
        removeAnimsOn = on
        saveConfig()
        if removeAnimsConn then pcall(function() removeAnimsConn:Disconnect() end) removeAnimsConn = nil end
        if not on then return end
        removeAnimsConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not removeAnimsOn then return end
            local char = player.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            pcall(function()
                for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop(0) end
                local anim = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if anim then
                    for _, t in ipairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop(0) end
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetRemoveAnims = setRemoveAnims
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(1.5)
        if Config.removeAnimations == true then setRemoveAnims(true) end
    end)

    -- ---------- ANIM PACK (walk/run/jump) ----------
    local ANIM_PACKS = {
        Default = nil, -- keep game default
        Zombie = {
            walk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616010829",
            run  = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616010829",
            jump = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616008936",
            idle = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616006777",
        },
        Ninja = {
            walk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=656117878",
            run  = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=656118341",
            jump = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=656117606",
            idle = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=656117400",
        },
        Robot = {
            walk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616095330",
            run  = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616095330",
            jump = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616093476",
            idle = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616088211",
        },
        Stylish = {
            walk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616136790",
            run  = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616138447",
            jump = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616139451",
            idle = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616133594",
        },
        Levitation = {
            walk = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616006600",
            run  = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616010671",
            jump = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616008936",
            idle = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=616006777",
        },
    }
    local function applyAnimPack(name)
        name = name or Config.animPack or "Default"
        Config.animPack = name
        saveConfig()
        local pack = ANIM_PACKS[name]
        if not pack then return end -- Default = leave alone
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if not animate then return end
        pcall(function()
            local function setAnim(folderName, id)
                local folder = animate:FindFirstChild(folderName)
                if not folder then return end
                for _, ch in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("Animation") then
                        ch.AnimationId = id
                    end
                end
            end
            if pack.walk then setAnim("walk", pack.walk) end
            if pack.run  then setAnim("run",  pack.run)  end
            if pack.jump then setAnim("jump", pack.jump) end
            if pack.idle then setAnim("idle", pack.idle) end
            -- restart animate
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop(0) end
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.ZenoApplyAnimPack = applyAnimPack
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if Config.animPack and Config.animPack ~= "Default" then
            applyAnimPack(Config.animPack)
        end
    end)

    -- ---------- BASE OWNER ESP ----------
    local baseOwnerOn = false
    local baseOwnerEntries = {}
    local baseOwnerConn = nil
    local function clearBaseOwner()
        for _, e in pairs(baseOwnerEntries) do
            pcall(function() if e then e:Destroy() end end)
        end
        baseOwnerEntries = {}
    end
    local function refreshBaseOwnerESP()
        if not baseOwnerOn then return end
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        local seen = {}
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if not sign then continue end
            local part = sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if not part then continue end
            local ownerName = "?"
            pcall(function()
                local gui = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BillboardGui", true) or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                if gui then
                    for _, lbl in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                        if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") and lbl.Text and #lbl.Text > 0 and not lbl.Text:find("%$") then
                            ownerName = lbl.Text
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- Attribute fallback
                local attr = plot:GetAttribute("Owner") or plot:GetAttribute("OwnerName")
                if attr then ownerName = tostring(attr) end
            end)
            local uid = plot.Name
            seen[uid] = true
            local e = baseOwnerEntries[uid]
            if not e then
                local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                bb.Name = "ZenoBaseOwner_" .. uid
                bb.Adornee = part
                bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 28)
                bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                bb.MaxDistance = 400
                bb.Parent = playerGui
                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                lbl.TextSize = 12
                lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255)
                lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
                lbl.Parent = bb
                e = bb
                baseOwnerEntries[uid] = e
            end
            local lbl = e:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if lbl then lbl.Text = "🏠 " .. ownerName end
            e.Adornee = part
        end
        for uid, e in pairs(baseOwnerEntries) do
            if not seen[uid] then
                pcall(function() e:Destroy() end)
                baseOwnerEntries[uid] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function setBaseOwnerESP(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.baseOwnerEsp = on
        baseOwnerOn = on
        saveConfig()
        if not on then
            if baseOwnerConn then pcall(function() baseOwnerConn:Disconnect() end) baseOwnerConn = nil end
            clearBaseOwner()
            return
        end
        if not baseOwnerConn then
            local last = 0
            baseOwnerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < 1.2 then return end
                last = now
                refreshBaseOwnerESP()
            end)
        end
        task.defer(refreshBaseOwnerESP)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetBaseOwnerESP = setBaseOwnerESP
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if Config.baseOwnerEsp == true then setBaseOwnerESP(true) end
    end)

    -- ---------- GROUP ESP (highlight non-self players with group tag) ----------
    local groupEspOn = false
    local groupEspHolders = {}
    local groupEspConn = nil
    local function clearGroupEsp()
        for _, h in pairs(groupEspHolders) do pcall(function() h:Destroy() end) end
        groupEspHolders = {}
    end
    local function makeGroupEsp(plr)
        if not groupEspOn or plr == player then return end
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        if groupEspHolders[plr] then pcall(function() groupEspHolders[plr]:Destroy() end) end
        local holder = Instance.new("Folder")
        holder.Name = "ZenoGroupEsp_" .. plr.Name
        holder.Parent = playerGui
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Adornee = char
        hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 100, 255)
        hl.FillTransparency = 0.65
        hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(220, 160, 255)
        hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Parent = holder
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Adornee = hrp
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 20)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.8, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Parent = holder
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 11
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 160, 255)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        lbl.Text = plr.DisplayName or plr.Name
        lbl.Parent = bb
        groupEspHolders[plr] = holder
    end
    local function setGroupESP(on)
        on = on and true or false
        Config.groupEsp = on
        groupEspOn = on
        saveConfig()
        if not on then
            if groupEspConn then pcall(function() groupEspConn:Disconnect() end) groupEspConn = nil end
            clearGroupEsp()
            return
        end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= player then makeGroupEsp(plr) end
        end
        if not groupEspConn then
            groupEspConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
                task.wait(1)
                if groupEspOn then makeGroupEsp(plr) end
            end)
            Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
                if groupEspHolders[plr] then pcall(function() groupEspHolders[plr]:Destroy() end) groupEspHolders[plr] = nil end
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetGroupESP = setGroupESP
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if Config.groupEsp == true then setGroupESP(true) end
    end)

    -- ---------- AUTO RESET ON BALLOON ----------
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.4) do
            if not Config.autoResetBalloon then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then continue end
            local ballooned = false
            pcall(function()
                for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    local n = d.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("balloon") or n:find("ballooner") then
                        ballooned = true
                        break
                    end
                    if d:IsA("BodyForce") or d:IsA("BodyVelocity") or d:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
                        if n:find("balloon") then ballooned = true; break end
                    end
                end
                -- attribute
                if char:GetAttribute("Ballooned") == true or player:GetAttribute("Ballooned") == true then
                    ballooned = true
                end
            end)
            if ballooned then
                pcall(function()
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ---------- GRIEF DETECTOR ----------
    task.spawn(function()
        local lastAlert = 0
        while task.wait(1.0) do
            if Config.griefDetector == false then continue end
            local myPlot = nil
            pcall(function()
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if not plots then return end
                for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    if not sign then continue end
                    local text = ""
                    for _, lbl in ipairs(sign:GetDescendants()) do
                        if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") and lbl.Text then
                            if lbl.Text:find(player.Name) or lbl.Text:find(player.DisplayName) then
                                myPlot = plot
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if myPlot then break end
                end
            end)
            if not myPlot then continue end
            local hrp = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local plotPos = nil
            pcall(function()
                local sign = myPlot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                local p = sign and (sign:IsA("BasePart") and sign or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
                if p then plotPos = p.Position end
            end)
            if not plotPos then continue end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr == player then continue end
                local chr = plr.Character
                local phr = chr and chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not phr then continue end
                local dist = (phr.Position - plotPos).Magnitude
                if dist < 35 then
                    local stealing = plr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                        or (chr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
                    if stealing or dist < 18 then
                        local now = os.clock()
                        if now - lastAlert > 4 then
                            lastAlert = now
                            pcall(function()
                                if type(zenoNotify) == "function" then
                                    zenoNotify("⚠ GRIEF: " .. (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name) .. " near base!", Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80))
                                end
                            end)
                            if Config.autoGrief then
                                -- try admin rocket/ragdoll if available
                                pcall(function()
                                    if type(_G.ZenoFireAP) == "function" then
                                        _G.ZenoFireAP(plr, "ragdoll")
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ---------- KICK AFTER BUY ----------
    _G.ZenoKickAfterBuy = function()
        if Config.kickAfterBuyToPS == true then
            local code = tostring(Config.privateServerCode or "")
            local placeId = tonumber(Config.psPlaceId) or game.PlaceId
            if code ~= "" then
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPrivateServer(placeId, code, {player})
                end)
            else
                pcall(function() player:Kick("ZENO Kick After Buy → PS (no code)") end)
            end
        elseif Config.kickAfterBuy == true then
            pcall(function() player:Kick("ZENO Kick After Buy") end)
        end
    end
    -- Hook: when auto-buy fires successfully, call kick
    -- Patch into existing auto buy fire if possible
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(3)
        pcall(function()
            local old = rawget(_G, "ZenoOnBuySuccess")
            _G.ZenoOnBuySuccess = function(...)
                if type(old) == "function" then pcall(old, ...) end
                if Config.kickAfterBuy or Config.kickAfterBuyToPS then
                    task.delay(0.3, function()
                        if type(_G.ZenoKickAfterBuy) == "function" then _G.ZenoKickAfterBuy() end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- ---------- FLOAT STEAL (slow fall while stealing) ----------
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.15) do
            if not Config.floatSteal then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum then continue end
            local stealing = player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                or (char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
            if not stealing then continue end
            pcall(function()
                local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if v.Y < -5 then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, math.max(v.Y, -8), v.Z)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- ---------- SYNC SCAN helper (tries channel AnimalList) ----------
    _G.ZenoSyncScanPets = function()
        if Config.syncScan == false then return nil end
        local result = {}
        pcall(function()
            local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local pkgs = RS:FindFirstChild("Packages")
            local syn = pkgs and pkgs:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
            if not syn then return end
            -- Try RequestData remote
            local rf = syn:FindFirstChild("RequestData")
            if rf and rf:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                if plots then
                    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                        local ok, data = pcall(function() return rf:InvokeServer(plot.Name) end)
                        if ok and type(data) == "table" then
                            local al = data.AnimalList or data.Animals
                            if type(al) == "table" then
                                for slot, animal in pairs(al) do
                                    if type(animal) == "table" then
                                        result[#result+1] = {
                                            plot = plot.Name,
                                            slot = tostring(slot),
                                            index = animal.Index,
                                            mutation = animal.Mutation,
                                            traits = animal.Traits,
                                            uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. tostring(slot),
                                        }
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if #result > 0 then
            _G.ZenoSyncPetsCache = result
        end
        return result
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(3) do
            if Config.syncScan ~= false then
                pcall(_G.ZenoSyncScanPets)
            end
        end
    end)

    print("[ZENO] Rose-inspired features loaded")
end


-- ================================================================
-- TP V2 (PORT HAZE) - route 2e etage MED1/MED2 -> TP1..TP12
-- Source: haze_hub runTeleportV2SecondFloor, adapte a Zeno:
-- pas d'instantClone ici (le moteur de steal Zeno s'en charge),
-- retourne true + nom du point si arrivee confirmee, sinon false + raison.
-- ================================================================
_G.ZenoTPV2 = (function()
    local V2 = {}
    V2.LockCamera = true

    local MED_POINTS = {
        {name = "MED1", pos = Vector3.new(-410.65, -5.68, -46.10)},
        {name = "MED2", pos = Vector3.new(-410.91, -5.68, 168.89)},
    }
    local SECOND_FLOOR_POINTS = {
        {name = "TP1",  pos = Vector3.new(-488.88, 15, 196.38), facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP2",  pos = Vector3.new(-487.79, 15, 138.13), facing = "front"},
        {name = "TP3",  pos = Vector3.new(-489.38, 15, 89.23),  facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP4",  pos = Vector3.new(-489.69, 15, 30.98),  facing = "front"},
        {name = "TP5",  pos = Vector3.new(-488.75, 15, -17.95), facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP6",  pos = Vector3.new(-490.00, 15, -75.90), facing = "front"},
        {name = "TP7",  pos = Vector3.new(-331.75, 15, -75.80), facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP8",  pos = Vector3.new(-329.98, 15, -18.16), facing = "front"},
        {name = "TP9",  pos = Vector3.new(-330.04, 15, 31.14),  facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP10", pos = Vector3.new(-331.28, 15, 88.92),  facing = "front"},
        {name = "TP11", pos = Vector3.new(-330.57, 15, 138.10), facing = "back"},
        {name = "TP12", pos = Vector3.new(-330.01, 15, 195.96), facing = "front"},
    }
    local ALLOWED_BY_MED = {
        MED1 = {TP6 = true, TP7 = true, TP8 = true, TP10 = true, TP12 = true, TP5 = true, TP3 = true, TP1 = true},
        MED2 = {TP1 = true, TP2 = true, TP4 = true, TP6 = true, TP7 = true, TP9 = true, TP11 = true, TP12 = true},
    }

    local function flatDistance(a, b)
        return (Vector3.new(a.X, 0, a.Z) - Vector3.new(b.X, 0, b.Z)).Magnitude
    end
    local function waitSecondsHeartbeat(sec)
        local t = 0
        while t < sec do t += RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
    end
    local function waitUntilHeartbeat(predicate, timeoutSec)
        local t = 0
        while true do
            if predicate() then return true end
            local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            t += dt
            if timeoutSec and t >= timeoutSec then return false end
        end
    end
    local function riseToY(hrp, targetY)
        if not hrp then return end
        local MAX_TIME = 3.0
        local start = os.clock()
        while hrp.Parent and hrp.Position.Y < targetY do
            local dist = targetY - hrp.Position.Y
            local speed = math.clamp(dist * 20, 280, 310)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, speed, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
            if os.clock() - start > MAX_TIME then break end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
    end
    local function getClosestBaseSignToPosition(worldPos)
        local closestPart, closestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, label in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if label:IsA("TextLabel") then
                local txt = tostring(label.Text or "")
                if txt ~= "" and txt:lower():find("base", 1, true) then
                    local gui = label:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                    if gui then
                        local part = gui.Adornee or gui.Parent
                        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local dist = (part.Position - worldPos).Magnitude
                            if dist < closestDist then closestDist, closestPart = dist, part end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return closestPart
    end
    local function getNearestMedPoint(fromPos)
        local bestPoint, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, entry in ipairs(MED_POINTS) do
            local dist = flatDistance(fromPos, entry.pos)
            if dist < bestDist then bestDist, bestPoint = dist, entry end
        end
        return bestPoint, bestDist
    end
    local function getBestSecondFloorPoint(medPoint, brainrotPos)
        local medPos = medPoint and medPoint.pos
        local medName = medPoint and medPoint.name
        if not medPos or not medName then return nil end
        local allowed = ALLOWED_BY_MED[medName]
        if not allowed then return nil end
        local bestPoint, bestMedDist, bestBrainrotDist = nil, math.huge, math.huge
        for _, entry in ipairs(SECOND_FLOOR_POINTS) do
            if allowed[entry.name] then
                local distToMed = flatDistance(medPos, entry.pos)
                local distToBrainrot = flatDistance(brainrotPos, entry.pos)
                if (distToBrainrot < bestBrainrotDist)
                    or (math.abs(distToBrainrot - bestBrainrotDist) <= 0.001 and distToMed < bestMedDist) then
                    bestPoint, bestMedDist, bestBrainrotDist = entry, distToMed, distToBrainrot
                end
            end
        end
        return bestPoint, bestMedDist, bestBrainrotDist
    end

    function V2.run(brainrotPos, hrp)
        if not brainrotPos or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return false, "missing brainrotPos/hrp" end
        local char = hrp.Parent
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        local medPoint = getNearestMedPoint(hrp.Position)
        if not medPoint then return false, "no MED point" end
        local chosenPoint = getBestSecondFloorPoint(medPoint, brainrotPos)
        if not chosenPoint then return false, "no TP1-TP12 point" end

        local isFirstFloorBrainrot = brainrotPos.Y <= 6.313370704650879
        local chosenTargetPos = isFirstFloorBrainrot
            and Vector3.new(chosenPoint.pos.X, 1, chosenPoint.pos.Z)
            or chosenPoint.pos

        local signPart = getClosestBaseSignToPosition(chosenTargetPos)
        local faceVector = nil
        if signPart then
            faceVector = chosenPoint.facing == "front" and signPart.CFrame.LookVector or -signPart.CFrame.LookVector
            if faceVector.Magnitude <= 0 then faceVector = nil end
        end

        -- camera lock (optionnel) - evite les mouvements parasite pendant la route
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local prevCameraType = camera and camera.CameraType
        local prevFov = camera and camera.FieldOfView
        local camConn = nil
        if V2.LockCamera and camera then
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
            camera.FieldOfView = 95
            camConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local c = player.Character
                local cHrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if cHrp and camera then
                    camera.CFrame = cHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 10) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-5), 0, 0)
                end
            end)
        end
        local function stopCameraLock()
            if camConn then camConn:Disconnect(); camConn = nil end
            if camera then
                if prevCameraType then camera.CameraType = prevCameraType end
                if prevFov then camera.FieldOfView = prevFov end
            end
        end
        local function finish(ok2, msg)
            stopCameraLock()
            return ok2, msg
        end
        local function v2zero()
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end

        -- orientation initiale vers le panneau de la base cible
        if faceVector then
            v2zero()
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + faceVector)
            v2zero()
        end

        v2zero()
        if hum and hum.Parent then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
        task.wait(0.01)
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return finish(false, "hrp lost during jump") end

        -- 1) TP flat vers MED (X/Z du MED, Y conserve)
        local medFlatPos = Vector3.new(medPoint.pos.X, hrp.Position.Y, medPoint.pos.Z)
        v2zero()
        hrp.CFrame = faceVector and CFrame.new(medFlatPos, medFlatPos + faceVector) or CFrame.new(medFlatPos)
        v2zero()
        task.wait(0.1)
        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return finish(false, "hrp lost after MED") end
        if not waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
            return hrp and hrp.Parent and flatDistance(hrp.Position, medPoint.pos) <= 2.5
        end, 1.5) then return finish(false, "failed to reach MED") end

        -- 2) montee Y45
        riseToY(hrp, 45)

        -- 3) TP vers le point TP1-TP12 autorise le plus proche du brainrot
        v2zero()
        hrp.CFrame = faceVector and CFrame.new(chosenTargetPos, chosenTargetPos + faceVector) or CFrame.new(chosenTargetPos)
        v2zero()
        if not waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
            return hrp and hrp.Parent and (hrp.Position - chosenTargetPos).Magnitude <= 2.5
        end, 3.0) then return finish(false, "failed to reach TP point") end

        waitSecondsHeartbeat(0.10)
        waitSecondsHeartbeat(0.05)

        -- 4) double verification avant de rendre la main au moteur de steal Zeno
        local stillThere = waitUntilHeartbeat(function()
            return hrp and hrp.Parent and (hrp.Position - chosenTargetPos).Magnitude <= 2.5
        end, 0.75)
        if not stillThere then return finish(false, "moved away from TP point") end

        v2zero()
        return finish(true, chosenPoint.name)
    end

    _G.ZenoSetTPV2 = function(on) Config.tpV2 = (on == true); if saveConfig then pcall(saveConfig) end end
    return V2
end)()

-- ================================================================
-- SAFE PERF MODE (local only — no stripping other players)
-- ================================================================
do
    local conns = {}
    local orig = {}
    local function stop()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        conns = {}
        pcall(function()
            if orig.QualityLevel ~= nil then settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = orig.QualityLevel end
            if orig.GlobalShadows ~= nil then Lighting.GlobalShadows = orig.GlobalShadows end
            if orig.Brightness ~= nil then Lighting.Brightness = orig.Brightness end
            if orig.Technology ~= nil then Lighting.Technology = orig.Technology end
        end)
        orig = {}
