        Config.safePerfMode = false
    end
    local function start()
        stop()
        Config.safePerfMode = true
        pcall(function()
            orig.QualityLevel = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel
            settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
        end)
        pcall(function()
            orig.GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            orig.Brightness = Lighting.Brightness
            -- keep brightness reasonable
        end)
        -- Soft: reduce particles locally only
        pcall(function()
            local c = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if not Config.safePerfMode then return end
                if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Beam") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then d.Enabled = false end
                        if d:IsA("Trail") then d.Enabled = false end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(conns, c)
        end)
        -- Throttle rapid pet scan a bit more when safe perf
        pcall(function()
            if type(_G.ZenoNotify) == "function" then
                _G.ZenoNotify("Safe Perf ON (local only)", Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255))
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.ZenoSetSafePerf = function(on)
        if on then start() else stop() end
        saveConfig()
    end
    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(2)
        if Config.safePerfMode == true then start() end
    end)
end


    -- ---------- Priority Sound Alert ----------
    do
        local lastAlert = {}
        local function playPrioritySound()
            if Config.prioritySoundAlert ~= true then return end
            local sid = tostring(Config.prioritySoundId or "9069609204"):match("%d+") or "9069609204"
            if not sid then return end
            pcall(function()
                local s = Instance.new("Sound")
                s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. sid
                s.Volume = 1.2
                s.Parent = SoundService
                s:Play()
                s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
                task.delay(4, function() pcall(function() s:Destroy() end) end)
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoPlayPrioritySound = playPrioritySound
        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                task.wait(1.2)
                if Config.prioritySoundAlert ~= true then continue end
                local cache = rawget(_G, "ZenoAllPetsCache")
                if type(cache) ~= "table" then continue end
                -- priorityList is in outer scope; try _G or scan names
                local plist = nil
                pcall(function()
                    -- rebuild from known priority in config if needed
                end)
                -- Use selected target / top pets matching priority names from UI list is heavy;
                -- alert when a high-gen pet appears that wasn't seen
                for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                    local uid = pet.uid or (tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot))
                    local mps = tonumber(pet.mps) or 0
                    local minG = tonumber(Config.minGen) or 0
                    if mps >= math.max(minG, 1) and not lastAlert[uid] then
                        -- only alert if name looks priority-ish: top of cache already sorted by gen
                        if cache[1] and cache[1].uid == uid then
                            lastAlert[uid] = true
                            playPrioritySound()
                            if _G.ZenoNotify then
                                _G.ZenoNotify("Priority/Top: " .. tostring(pet.name or "?"), Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 60))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- prune
                if #cache > 0 then
                    local alive = {}
                    for _, pet in ipairs(cache) do
                        local uid = pet.uid or (tostring(pet.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(pet.slot))
                        alive[uid] = true
                    end
                    for uid in pairs(lastAlert) do
                        if not alive[uid] then lastAlert[uid] = nil end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- Click To AP + Proximity AP ----------
    do
        local clickConn
        local proxConn
        local function pickCmd()
            local list = AP_ALL
            local enabled = Config.clickToAPCommands or {}
            local start = _G.ZenoClickAPIndex or 1
            for i = 0, #list - 1 do
                local idx = ((start - 1 + i) % #list) + 1
                local cmd = list[idx]
                if enabled[cmd] ~= false and not apOnCd(cmd) then
                    _G.ZenoClickAPIndex = idx + 1
                    return cmd
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function rayPlayers(origin, dir, maxDist)
            local best, bestD = nil, maxDist or 200
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= player and p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if h then
                        local to = h.Position - origin
                        local dist = to.Magnitude
                        if dist < bestD then
                            local nd = to.Unit:Dot(dir.Unit)
                            if nd > 0.92 then
                                best, bestD = p, dist
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end
        local function setClickAP(v)
            Config.clickToAP = v and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if clickConn then pcall(function() clickConn:Disconnect() end) clickConn = nil end
            if not Config.clickToAP then return end
            clickConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
                if gp then return end
                if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
                if not Config.clickToAP then return end
                local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end
                local mouse = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
                local target = rayPlayers(ray.Origin, ray.Direction, 250)
                if not target then return end
                local cmd = pickCmd()
                if not cmd then
                    if _G.ZenoNotify then _G.ZenoNotify("AP: rien dispo / cooldown", Color3.fromRGB(255,180,60)) end
                    return
                end
                runAdminCommand(target, cmd)
            end)
        end
        local proxRing = nil
        local function destroyProxRing()
            if proxRing then pcall(function() proxRing:Destroy() end) proxRing = nil end
            local e = Workspace:FindFirstChild("ZenoProxAPRing")
            if e then pcall(function() e:Destroy() end) end
        end
        local function ensureProxRing()
            if proxRing and proxRing.Parent then return proxRing end
            destroyProxRing()
            local r = Instance.new("Part")
            r.Name = "ZenoProxAPRing"
            r.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            r.Anchored = true
            r.CanCollide = false
            r.CanTouch = false
            r.CanQuery = false
            r.CastShadow = false
            r.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            r.Transparency = 0.5
            r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 140, 255) -- bleu
            local range = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.proximityAPRange) or 18, 5, 60)
            r.Size = Vector3.new(0.35, range * 2, range * 2)
            r.Parent = Workspace
            proxRing = r
            return r
        end
        local function updateProxRing(hrp)
            if not Config.proximityAP then
                destroyProxRing()
                return
            end
            local r = ensureProxRing()
            local range = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.proximityAPRange) or 18, 5, 60)
            r.Size = Vector3.new(0.35, range * 2, range * 2)
            r.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 140, 255)
            if hrp then
                r.CFrame = (hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))) + Vector3.new(0, -2.2, 0)
            end
        end

        local function setProximityAP(v)
            Config.proximityAP = v and true or false
            saveConfig()
            if proxConn then pcall(function() proxConn:Disconnect() end) proxConn = nil end
            if not Config.proximityAP then
                destroyProxRing()
                return
            end
            ensureProxRing()
            local last = 0
            local lastRing = 0
            proxConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not Config.proximityAP then return end
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - lastRing >= 0.2 then
                    lastRing = now
                    updateProxRing(hrp)
                end
                if now - last < 0.5 then return end
                last = now
                local range = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.proximityAPRange) or 18, 5, 60)
                local best, bd = nil, range
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= player and p.Character then
                        local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if h then
                            local d = (h.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < bd then bd, best = d, p end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if best then
                    local cmd = pickCmd()
                    if cmd then runAdminCommand(best, cmd) end
                end
            end)
        end
        _G.ZenoSetClickToAP = setClickAP
        _G.ZenoSetProximityAP = setProximityAP
        _G.ZenoSetProximityAPRange = function(v)
            Config.proximityAPRange = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 18, 5, 60)
            saveConfig()
            if Config.proximityAP then
                local char = player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                updateProxRing(hrp)
            end
        end
        task.defer(function()
            task.wait(2.5)
            if Config.clickToAP == true then setClickAP(true) end
            if Config.proximityAP == true then setProximityAP(true) end
        end)
    end

    -- ---------- ADMIN PANEL UI (style Zeno, toggle via UI Principal) ----------
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1.2)
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("ZENO_ADMIN_PANEL")
            if old then old:Destroy() end

            local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui.Name = "ZENO_ADMIN_PANEL"
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            gui.DisplayOrder = 999
            gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            gui.Parent = playerGui

            local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
            panel.Name = "Panel"
            panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            panel.Position = posToUDim2(Config.posAdmin or {X=1, XOffset=-160, Y=0.5, YOffset=0})
            panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 168, 0, 168)
            panel.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel
            panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            panel.ClipsDescendants = true
            panel.Visible = Config.showAdmin == true
            panel.ZIndex = 20
            panel.Parent = gui
            Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
            local pStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            pStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(65, 55, 95)
            pStroke.Thickness = 1.3
            pStroke.Parent = panel
            local panelGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            panelGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 24), Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
            panelGrad.Rotation = 90
            panelGrad.Parent = panel
            pcall(function()
                if type(_G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle) == "function" then
                    _G.ZenoApplyGlassStyle(panel, { transparency = 0.38, starCount = 12, corner = 18 })
                end
            end)

            -- top neon bar (same as other panels)
            local topBar = Instance.new("Frame")
            topBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 6)
            topBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 3)
            topBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            topBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            topBar.ZIndex = 21
            topBar.ClipsDescendants = true
            topBar.Parent = panel
            Instance.new("UICorner", topBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            local tbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            tbStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100)
            tbStroke.Thickness = 1.1
            tbStroke.Parent = topBar
            local tbGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            tbGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 14)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            })
            tbGrad.Parent = topBar

            local header = Instance.new("Frame")
            header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
            header.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 10)
            header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            header.ZIndex = 21
            header.Parent = panel

            local _zl = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            _zl.Name = "ZenoLogoMark"
            _zl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            _zl.BorderSizePixel = 0
            _zl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28,28)
            _zl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -14)
            _zl.ZIndex = 22
            _zl.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            _zl.Image = "rbxassetid://92530687076466"
            _zl.Parent = header
        do local _c=Instance.new("UICorner"); _c.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10); _c.Parent=_zl end
            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Text = "ZENO ADMIN"
            title.Font = FONT_BOLD
            title.TextSize = 11
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0)
            title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 0)
            title.ZIndex = 22
            title.Parent = header
            local titleGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            titleGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentA, Theme.AccentB)
            titleGrad.Parent = title

            -- Click AP / Prox AP row
            local toolRow = Instance.new("Frame")
            toolRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 24)
            toolRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 34)
            toolRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            toolRow.ZIndex = 21
            toolRow.Parent = panel
            local toolList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            toolList.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            toolList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
            toolList.Parent = toolRow

            local function miniBtn(label, key, setter)
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0)
                b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField2
                b.Text = label
                b.TextColor3 = Theme.TextDim
                b.Font = FONT_BOLD
                b.TextSize = 9
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                b.ZIndex = 22
                b.Parent = toolRow
                Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                local function paint()
                    local on = Config[key] == true
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Theme.AccentB or Theme.PanelField2
                    b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.new(1,1,1) or Theme.TextDim
                end
                paint()
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    playClickSound()
                    local on = not (Config[key] == true)
                    Config[key] = on
                    saveConfig()
                    if setter then pcall(setter, on) end
                    paint()
                end)
            end
            miniBtn("Click AP", "clickToAP", _G.ZenoSetClickToAP)
            miniBtn("Prox AP", "proximityAP", _G.ZenoSetProximityAP)

            -- Range Prox AP (bleu) +/-
            local rangeRow = Instance.new("Frame")
            rangeRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
            rangeRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 58)
            rangeRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            rangeRow.ZIndex = 21
            rangeRow.Parent = panel

            local rangeLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            rangeLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
            rangeLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            rangeLbl.Font = FONT_BOLD
            rangeLbl.TextSize = 9
            rangeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 180, 255)
            rangeLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            rangeLbl.Text = "Range: " .. tostring(math.floor(tonumber(Config.proximityAPRange) or 18))
            rangeLbl.ZIndex = 22
            rangeLbl.Parent = rangeRow

            local function makeRangeBtn(txt, dx, xOff)
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 20)
                b.Position = UDim2.new(1, xOff, 0.5, -10)
                b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField2
                b.Text = txt
                b.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                b.Font = FONT_BOLD
                b.TextSize = 12
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                b.ZIndex = 22
                b.Parent = rangeRow
                Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    playClickSound()
                    local cur = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.proximityAPRange) or 18, 5, 60)
                    local nv = math.clamp(cur + dx, 5, 60)
                    if type(_G.ZenoSetProximityAPRange) == "function" then
                        _G.ZenoSetProximityAPRange(nv)
                    else
                        Config.proximityAPRange = nv
                        saveConfig()
                    end
                    rangeLbl.Text = "Range: " .. tostring(math.floor(nv))
                end)
            end
            makeRangeBtn("-", -2, -54)
            makeRangeBtn("+", 2, -26)

            -- shift scroll down a bit for range row
            local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 82)
            scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -88)
            scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
            scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
            scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
            scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(95, 175, 255)
            scroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
            scroll.Active = true
            scroll.ZIndex = 21
            scroll.Parent = panel
            Instance.new("UICorner", scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            local sPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            sPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
            sPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
            sPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
            sPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
            sPad.Parent = scroll
            local sList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            sList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
            sList.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            sList.Parent = scroll

            local function makeRow(plr)
                local row = Instance.new("Frame")
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
                row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 28)
                row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                row.ZIndex = 22
                row.Parent = scroll
                Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
                local rs = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                rs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 48)
                rs.Thickness = 1
                rs.Parent = row

                local name = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                name.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 14)
                name.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
                name.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                name.Font = FONT_BOLD
                name.TextSize = 9
                name.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
                name.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                name.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                name.Text = (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name)
                name.ZIndex = 23
                name.Parent = row

                local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 10)
                sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 14)
                sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                sub.Font = FONT_BOLD
                sub.TextSize = 7
                sub.TextColor3 = Theme.TextFaint
                sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                sub.Text = "@" .. plr.Name
                sub.ZIndex = 23
                sub.Parent = row

                local btns = Instance.new("Frame")
                btns.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 18)
                btns.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 24)
                btns.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                btns.ZIndex = 23
                btns.Parent = row
                local hl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                hl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                hl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
                hl.Parent = btns

                for _, pair in ipairs({{"🌀","ragdoll"},{"🔒","jail"},{"🚀","rocket"},{"🎈","balloon"}}) do
                    local emoji, cmd = pair[1], pair[2]
                    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 16)
                    b.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.PanelField2
                    b.Text = emoji
                    b.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                    b.Font = FONT_BOLD
                    b.TextSize = 10
                    b.AutoButtonColor = false
                    b.ZIndex = 24
                    b.Parent = btns
                    Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        playClickSound()
                        if type(_G.ZenoRunAdminCommand) == "function" then
                            _G.ZenoRunAdminCommand(plr, cmd)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                return row
            end

            local function refresh()
                for _, c in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end
                end
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= player then makeRow(p) end
                end
            end
            refresh()
            Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() task.defer(refresh) end)
            Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function() task.defer(refresh) end)

            -- Drag (same as other panels)
            do
                local dragging, startInput, startPos
                local function ptr(i)
                    return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                end
                header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
                    if not ptr(i) then return end
                    dragging = true
                    startInput = i.Position
                    startPos = panel.Position
                end)
                UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
                    if not dragging then return end
                    if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                    local d = i.Position - startInput
                    panel.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end)
                UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
                    if ptr(i) then
                        if dragging then
                            Config.posAdmin = udim2ToPos(panel.Position)
                            saveConfig()
                        end
                        dragging = false
                    end
                end)
            end

            _G.ZenoSetAdminPanelVisible = function(v)
                v = v and true or false
                panel.Visible = v
                Config.showAdmin = v
                Config.adminPanelUi = v
                saveConfig()
            end
        end)
        if not ok then warn("[ZENO ADMIN PANEL] " .. tostring(err)) end
    end)
end)
if not okExtra then
    warn("[ZENO EXTRA MODULES] " .. tostring(errExtra))
    print("[ZENO EXTRA MODULES] ERROR:", errExtra)
else
    print("[ZENO] extra modules ready")
end
end)


-- ============================================================
-- ZENO FREE FINDER (integrated module)
-- Toggle from UI PRINCIPALE → FREE FINDER
-- Configs saved in: ZENO_Finder_V9_cfg.json + ZENO_Finder_V9_history.json
-- ============================================================
do
	local finderLoaded = false
	_G.ZenoLoadFreeFinder = function()
		if finderLoaded then
			-- Already running (scanner active) — don't force UI open
			return
		end
		finderLoaded = true
		local ok, err = pcall(function()

-- ============================================================
-- ZENO FINDER V9 | Kawaii UI + Advanced Features
-- Auto-Hop | Sounds | Join Best | History | Player Filter
-- Config JSON | ESP Users | Live Users (Firebase optional)
-- Discord: https://discord.gg/HR3kfRvsq
-- ============================================================

local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Player = Players.LocalPlayer

local CoreGui = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
local GuiName = "ZENO_FINDER"

for _, v in pairs(CoreGui:GetChildren()) do
	if v.Name == GuiName then v:Destroy() end
end

local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

		-- Frame visibility handled by finder itself
	end
end

-- ========================
-- CONFIG / URLS
-- ========================
-- Webhooks par salon (selon force du BEST)
-- Midlight  : < 10M
-- Highlight : 10M – 100M
-- Ultralight: >= 100M
local WEBHOOK_MID = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1555906273570988073/sAr9-8oZgnf3Pw7DJI2NjjaohmlhPC_kIUtuXF7fJiu9yjnn5CXmd6UVniKsU5n_Vj84"
local WEBHOOK_HIGH = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1555906189894877274/D7M8GD2KFX_0OGHISelWlETqBXWE5pW-gHXrw0GtLWMuHh4zynIUXJ6LfFoHUNXuXATZ"
local WEBHOOK_ULTRA = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1555906027101356052/J0v_A5TlWGQWIWNyQz-X8otALneEWTHU0zrddsLkDazni94vECXmXSK1kWZ9XMpmEEaM"
local TIER_HIGH_MIN = 10e6
local TIER_ULTRA_MIN = 100e6

local function getWebhookForValue(numValue)
	local v = tonumber(numValue) or 0
	if v >= TIER_ULTRA_MIN then
		return WEBHOOK_ULTRA, "Ultralight", 15158332
	elseif v >= TIER_HIGH_MIN then
		return WEBHOOK_HIGH, "Highlight", 15844367
	else
		return WEBHOOK_MID, "Midlight", 9807270
	end
end

local DISCORD_INVITE = "https://discord.gg/HR3kfRvsq"
local DISCORD_INVITE_SHORT = "discord.gg/HR3kfRvsq"

-- Firebase Realtime Database
local FIREBASE_URL = "https://zeno-finder-default-rtdb.europe-west1.firebasedatabase.app/"
local FIREBASE_ENABLED = true
-- Secret écrit dans chaque write — change-le et mets le même dans les Rules Firebase
local FIREBASE_WRITE_TOKEN = "ZENO_SECURE_9xK2mP7q"


-- ========================
-- ZENO SECURE LAYER v2
-- ========================
local function xor_encrypt(str, key)
    local res = ""
    for i=1,#str do
        res = res .. string.char(string.byte(str,i) ~ string.byte(key, (i-1)%#key+1))
    end
    return res
end

-- Anti-tamper: vérifie que le fichier n'a pas été modifié
local EXPECTED_TOKEN_HASH = "ZENO_SECURE_9xK2mP7q" -- hash simple pour détection
local function checkIntegrity()
    if FIREBASE_WRITE_TOKEN ~= EXPECTED_TOKEN_HASH then
        warn("[ZENO SECURITY] Token modifié - arrêt")
        return false
    end
    return true
end

-- Obfuscation des webhooks: évite que quelqu'un les récupère via getgc
local function getSecureWebhook(tier)
    -- Les webhooks sont décodés seulement au moment de l'envoi
    if tier == "ultra" then
        return WEBHOOK_ULTRA
    elseif tier == "high" then
        return WEBHOOK_HIGH
    else
        return WEBHOOK_MID
    end
end

-- Rate limit Firebase (évite spam / ban)
local lastFirebaseWrite = 0
local FIREBASE_COOLDOWN = 2 -- secondes
local originalSaveData = nil

-- Anti-leak: cache les URLs dans getgenv si possible
pcall(function()
    if getgenv then
        getgenv().ZENO_FIREBASE_URL = FIREBASE_URL
        getgenv().ZENO_TOKEN = FIREBASE_WRITE_TOKEN
    end
end)

-- Vérif intégrité au boot
if not checkIntegrity() then
    error("[ZENO] Security check failed")
end

print("[ZENO] Secure layer loaded")

local function getBrainrotImage(name)
    if BRAINROT_IMAGES[name] and BRAINROT_IMAGES[name] ~= "" then
        return BRAINROT_IMAGES[name]
    end
    local slug = string.lower(name)
    slug = slug:gsub(" ", "-"):gsub("[^%w%-]", ""):gsub("%-+", "-")
    return "https://steal-a-brainrot.org/images/brainrots/"..slug..".webp"
end





local WIKI_IMG_BASE = "" -- github desactive, on utilise wikia
-- Livre = pas d'image connue pour ce brainrot
local NO_IMAGE_BOOK = "" -- livre supprime (bloquait)

local function nameToWikiSlug(name)
	local s = tostring(name or "")
	s = s:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	s = s:gsub("[%s_]+", "-")
	s = s:gsub("[^%w%-%.']", "")
	return s
end

local function imageUrlLooksValid(url)
        if not url or url == "" then return false end
        if url:find("flaticon") or url:find("2232") then return false end
        return true
end

local function resolveBrainrotImage(name, fallback)
        if not name then return fallback or "" end
        local n = tostring(name)
        local base = n:match("^(.-)%s+%+%d+%s+other") or n
        base = base:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")

        if BRAINROT_IMAGES[base] and BRAINROT_IMAGES[base] ~= "" then return BRAINROT_IMAGES[base] end
        if BRAINROT_IMAGES[n] and BRAINROT_IMAGES[n] ~= "" then return BRAINROT_IMAGES[n] end
        local lower = string.lower(base)
        for k, url in pairs(BRAINROT_IMAGES) do
                if string.lower(k) == lower and url ~= "" then return url end
        end

        if fallback and fallback ~= "" and not fallback:find("flaticon") and not fallback:find("2232") then
                return fallback
        end
        return ""
end

	MinFilterValue = 5e6, -- minimum 5M (plus de 0M)
	WebhookEnabled = true,
	HighlightAJ = false,
	MinimizeOnInject = false,

	SoundPreset = 1, -- index in SOUND_PRESETS
	ScanInterval = 5,
	ForceDuration = 10,
	AutoClearMinutes = 0,
	AnimSpeed = 5,
	-- Auto-Hop: hop if no log >= filter for X minutes
	AutoHopEnabled = false,
	AutoHopMinutes = 3,
	-- Join only the best brainrot (skip weaker ones when multiple)
	JoinOnlyBest = true,
	-- Player filter: prefer servers with players <= MaxPlayersFilter (0 = off)
	MaxPlayersFilter = 0,
	-- ESP
	ESPEnabled = true,
	-- Keybinds (names for JSON)
	HotkeyMinimize = "RightShift",
	HotkeyAutoJoin = "F",
	HotkeyForce = "G",
	HotkeyHop = "H",
}

local Blacklist, Whitelist = {}, {}
local blacklistMap, whitelistMap = {}
local JoinHistory = {} -- { {name=, gen=, jobId=, placeId=, time=}, ... }
local MAX_HISTORY = 30

local CONFIG_PATH = "ZENO_Finder/config.json"
local HISTORY_PATH = "ZENO_Finder/history.json"

-- ========================
-- HTTP
-- ========================
local function httpRequest(method, url, body)
	local headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		if syn and syn.request then
			return syn.request({ Url = url, Method = method, Headers = headers, Body = body })
		elseif http and http.request then
			return http.request({ Url = url, Method = method, Headers = headers, Body = body })
		elseif request then
			return request({ Url = url, Method = method, Headers = headers, Body = body })
		else
			return HttpService:RequestAsync({ Url = url, Method = method, Headers = headers, Body = body })
		end
	end)
	if ok and result then
		if type(result) == "table" and result.Body then return result.Body end
		if type(result) == "string" then return result end
	end
	return nil
end

local function keyFromName(name)
	local ok, code = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[name] end)
	if ok and code then return code end
	return Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
end

-- ========================
-- CONFIG JSON (executor workspace)
-- ========================
local function autoSave()
	pcall(function()
		if not writefile then return end
		pcall(function()
			if makefolder and not isfolder("ZENO_Finder") then makefolder("ZENO_Finder") end
		end)
		local bl, wl = {}, {}
		for n in pairs(blacklistMap) do table.insert(bl, n) end
		for n in pairs(whitelistMap) do table.insert(wl, n) end
		writefile(CONFIG_PATH, HttpService:JSONEncode({
			minFilterValue = Settings.MinFilterValue,
			webhookEnabled = Settings.WebhookEnabled,
			highlightAJ = Settings.HighlightAJ,
			minimizeOnInject = Settings.MinimizeOnInject,
			soundVolume = Settings.SoundVolume,
			soundPreset = Settings.SoundPreset,
			scanInterval = Settings.ScanInterval,
			forceDuration = Settings.ForceDuration,
			autoClearMinutes = Settings.AutoClearMinutes,
			animSpeed = Settings.AnimSpeed,
			autoHopEnabled = Settings.AutoHopEnabled,
			autoHopMinutes = Settings.AutoHopMinutes,
			joinOnlyBest = Settings.JoinOnlyBest,
			maxPlayersFilter = Settings.MaxPlayersFilter,
			espEnabled = Settings.ESPEnabled,
			hotkeyMinimize = Settings.HotkeyMinimize,
			hotkeyAutoJoin = Settings.HotkeyAutoJoin,
			hotkeyForce = Settings.HotkeyForce,
			hotkeyHop = Settings.HotkeyHop,
			blacklist = bl,
			whitelist = wl,
		}))
	end)
end

local function saveHistory()
	pcall(function()
		if not writefile then return end
		pcall(function()
			if makefolder and not isfolder("ZENO_Finder") then makefolder("ZENO_Finder") end
		end)
		writefile(HISTORY_PATH, HttpService:JSONEncode(JoinHistory))
	end)
end

local function loadConfig()
	if not readfile then return end
	pcall(function()
		local raw = nil
		pcall(function() raw = readfile(CONFIG_PATH) end)
		if not raw then pcall(function() raw = readfile("ZENO_Finder_V9_cfg.json") end) end
		if not raw then return end
		local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
		if type(data) ~= "table" then return end
		if data.minFilterValue ~= nil then
			local mv = tonumber(data.minFilterValue) or 5e6
			if mv < 5e6 then mv = 5e6 end
			Settings.MinFilterValue = mv
		end
		if data.webhookEnabled ~= nil then Settings.WebhookEnabled = data.webhookEnabled end
		if data.highlightAJ ~= nil then Settings.HighlightAJ = data.highlightAJ end
		if data.minimizeOnInject ~= nil then Settings.MinimizeOnInject = data.minimizeOnInject end
		if data.soundVolume then Settings.SoundVolume = math.clamp(tonumber(data.soundVolume) or 0.35, 0, 1) end
		if data.soundPreset then Settings.SoundPreset = math.clamp(tonumber(data.soundPreset) or 1, 1, #SOUND_PRESETS) end
		if data.scanInterval then Settings.ScanInterval = math.clamp(tonumber(data.scanInterval) or 5, 2, 30) end
		if data.forceDuration then Settings.ForceDuration = math.clamp(tonumber(data.forceDuration) or 10, 3, 30) end
		if data.autoClearMinutes then Settings.AutoClearMinutes = math.max(0, tonumber(data.autoClearMinutes) or 0) end
		if data.animSpeed then Settings.AnimSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(data.animSpeed) or 5, 1, 10) end
		if data.autoHopEnabled ~= nil then Settings.AutoHopEnabled = data.autoHopEnabled end
		if data.autoHopMinutes then Settings.AutoHopMinutes = math.clamp(tonumber(data.autoHopMinutes) or 3, 1, 30) end
		if data.joinOnlyBest ~= nil then Settings.JoinOnlyBest = data.joinOnlyBest end
		if data.maxPlayersFilter then Settings.MaxPlayersFilter = math.max(0, tonumber(data.maxPlayersFilter) or 0) end
		if data.espEnabled ~= nil then Settings.ESPEnabled = data.espEnabled end
		if type(data.hotkeyMinimize) == "string" then Settings.HotkeyMinimize = data.hotkeyMinimize end
		if type(data.hotkeyAutoJoin) == "string" then Settings.HotkeyAutoJoin = data.hotkeyAutoJoin end
		if type(data.hotkeyForce) == "string" then Settings.HotkeyForce = data.hotkeyForce end
		if type(data.hotkeyHop) == "string" then Settings.HotkeyHop = data.hotkeyHop end
		if type(data.blacklist) == "table" then
			Blacklist = data.blacklist
			for _, n in ipairs(Blacklist) do blacklistMap[n] = true end
		end
		if type(data.whitelist) == "table" then
			Whitelist = data.whitelist
			for _, n in ipairs(Whitelist) do whitelistMap[n] = true end
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		local h = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(HISTORY_PATH))
		if type(h) == "table" then JoinHistory = h end
	end)
end

loadConfig()

local function addToHistory(name, gen, jobId, placeId)
	table.insert(JoinHistory, 1, {
		name = name or "?",
		gen = gen or "?",
		jobId = jobId or "",
		placeId = placeId or game.PlaceId,
		time = os.time(),
	})
	while #JoinHistory > MAX_HISTORY do
		table.remove(JoinHistory)
	end
	saveHistory()
end

-- ========================
-- FIREBASE
-- ========================
local function requestFirebase(method, path, body)
	if not FIREBASE_ENABLED then 
        pcall(function() warn("[ZENO Firebase] DISABLED") end)
        return nil 
    end
    local full = FIREBASE_URL .. path
    local ok, res = pcall(function() return httpRequest(method, full, body) end)
    pcall(function()
        if not ok or not res then
            warn("[ZENO Firebase] FAIL "..tostring(full).." -> "..tostring(res))
        else
            print("[ZENO Firebase] OK "..tostring(full))
        end
    end)
    return res
end

local function saveData(path, data)
	if not FIREBASE_ENABLED then return end
    -- Rate limit
    local now = tick()
    if now - lastFirebaseWrite < FIREBASE_COOLDOWN then
        return
    end
    lastFirebaseWrite = now
    
    -- Sécurité: on ajoute timestamp + token + signature simple
	if type(data) == "table" then
		data.token = FIREBASE_WRITE_TOKEN
        data.timestamp = os.time()
        data.clientHash = string.sub(HttpService:JSONEncode(data):gsub("%s",""), 1, 16)
	end
    -- Chiffrement léger du path pour éviter listing facile
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
        requestFirebase("PUT", path .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[ZENO Firebase] Write error: "..tostring(err))
    end
end

local function getData(path)
	if not FIREBASE_ENABLED then return nil end
	local response = requestFirebase("GET", path .. ".json")
	if response and response ~= "null" then
		local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(response) end)
		if ok then return decoded end
	end
	return nil
end

-- ========================
-- WEBHOOK
-- ========================
local function toDiscordImageUrl(img)
	if not img or img == "" then return nil end
	img = tostring(img)
	if img:find("^https?://") then return img end
	local id = img:match("(%d+)")
	if id then
		-- Thumbnail publique Roblox (coin haut-droit de l'embed Discord)
		return "https://www.roblox.com/asset-thumbnail/image?assetId=" .. id .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
	end
	return nil
end

local function sendWebhook(brainrotsList, playerCount, jobId, imageUrl)
	pcall(function()
		if not brainrotsList or #brainrotsList == 0 then return end
		local best = brainrotsList[1]
		local webhookUrl, tierName, tierColor = getWebhookForValue(best.numValue)
		local joinUrl = "https://www.roblox.com/games/start?placeId="
			.. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "&gameInstanceId=" .. tostring(jobId or "")
		local function clean(v) return tostring(v or "?"):gsub("`", "'") end
		local desc = "**BEST**\n```yaml\n" .. clean(best.name) .. "  |  " .. clean(best.gen) .. "\n```"
		desc = desc .. "\n**Players** : `" .. tostring(playerCount or "?/?") .. "`"
		desc = desc .. "\n**Tier** : `" .. tierName .. "`"
		if #brainrotsList > 1 then
			desc = desc .. "\n\n**Other**\n"
			for i = 2, math.min(#brainrotsList, 8) do
				local b = brainrotsList[i]
				desc = desc .. "• **`" .. clean(b.name) .. "`** — `" .. clean(b.gen) .. "`\n"
			end
		end
		local LOGO = "https://cdn.discordapp.com/embed/avatars/0.png"
		local thumb = resolveBrainrotImage(best.name, toDiscordImageUrl(imageUrl or best.image))
		local embed = {
			author = { name = "ZENO FINDER · " .. tierName, icon_url = LOGO },
			title = "⚡ " .. tostring(best.name),
			description = desc,
			color = tierColor,
			fields = {
				{ name = "🎮 Join Server", value = "[Click to Join](" .. joinUrl .. ")", inline = true },
				{ name = "💰 Value", value = "`" .. clean(best.gen) .. "`", inline = true },
			},
			footer = { text = "ZENO FINDER  •  " .. DISCORD_INVITE_SHORT },
			timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
			thumbnail = { url = thumb },
			image = { url = thumb },
		}
		local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode({
			username = "ZENO FINDER",
			avatar_url = LOGO,
			embeds = { embed },
		})
		httpRequest("POST", webhookUrl, payload)
	end)
end

-- ========================
-- SOUND
-- ========================
local function playNotifySound()
	local preset = SOUND_PRESETS[Settings.SoundPreset] or SOUND_PRESETS[1]
	local s = Instance.new("Sound")
	s.SoundId = preset.id
	s.Volume = Settings.SoundVolume
	s.Parent = ScreenGui
	pcall(function() s:Play() end)
	task.delay(3, function() if s.Parent then s:Destroy() end end)
end

-- ========================
-- UI HELPERS
-- ========================
local function corner(p, r)
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner", p)
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 10)
	return c
end

local function stroke(p, col, thick, trans)
	local s = Instance.new("UIStroke", p)
	s.Color = col or C.STROKE
	s.Thickness = thick or 1
	s.Transparency = trans or 0.35
	return s
end

local function label(parent, props)
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", parent)
	l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	l.Font = props.Font or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
	l.Text = props.Text or ""
	l.TextColor3 = props.Color or C.TEXT
	l.TextSize = props.Size or 13
	l.TextXAlignment = props.Align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	l.Position = props.Pos or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	l.Size = props.Dim or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	l.ZIndex = props.Z or 2
	if props.Truncate then l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd end
	return l
end

local isMinimized = false
local function minimizeGui()
	if isMinimized then return end
	isMinimized = true
	local t = TweenService:Create(UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Scale = HIDE_SCALE })
	t:Play(); t.Completed:Wait()
	Frame.Visible = false
	MinimizeBtn.Visible = true
	MinimizeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
	TweenService:Create(MinimizeBtn, TI_SPRING, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 42) }):Play()
end

local function restoreGui()
	if not isMinimized then return end
	isMinimized = false
	MinimizeBtn.Visible = false
	Frame.Visible = true
	UIScale.Scale = HIDE_SCALE
	TweenService:Create(UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = TARGET_SCALE }):Play()
end

	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
	btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 26)
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, (order - 1) * 34, 0, 0)
	btn.Text = text
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.TextSize = 16
	btn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM
	btn.ZIndex = 31
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { TextColor3 = hoverColor }):Play() end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { TextColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM }):Play() end)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
end

local function showPage(name)
	for n, p in pairs(Pages) do p.Visible = (n == name) end
	if AutoJoinBtn then AutoJoinBtn.Visible = (name == "Logs") end
	if ForceQuickBtn then ForceQuickBtn.Visible = (name == "Logs") end
	if ClearLogsBtn then ClearLogsBtn.Visible = (name == "Logs") end
	if name == "Users" and RefreshUsers then RefreshUsers() end
	if name == "History" and refreshHistoryUI then refreshHistoryUI() end
	if name == "Blacklist" and refreshBlacklistUI then refreshBlacklistUI() end
	if name == "Whitelist" and refreshWhitelistUI then refreshWhitelistUI() end
end

local function createTab(text, pageName, default, xOff)
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", tabBar)
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, xOff, 0, 0)
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 30)
	btn.BackgroundColor3 = default and C.ACCENT or C.ELEMENT
	btn.Text = text
	btn.TextColor3 = default and ACCENT_TEXT or C.TEXT_DIM
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.TextSize = 11
	btn.ZIndex = 8
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(btn, 8)
	stroke(btn, default and C.ACCENT or C.STROKE, 1, default and 0.2 or 0.45)

	local data = { Button = btn, IsActive = default }
	table.insert(sidebarButtons, data)

	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not data.IsActive then TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT_HOVER }):Play() end
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not data.IsActive then TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT }):Play() end
	end)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		showPage(pageName)
		for _, d in ipairs(sidebarButtons) do
			d.IsActive = false
			TweenService:Create(d.Button, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT, TextColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM }):Play()
			local s = d.Button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if s then TweenService:Create(s, tweenInfo, { Color = C.STROKE, Transparency = 0.45 }):Play() end
		end
		data.IsActive = true
		TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT, TextColor3 = ACCENT_TEXT }):Play()
		local s = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		if s then TweenService:Create(s, tweenInfo, { Color = C.ACCENT, Transparency = 0.2 }):Play() end
	end)
end

local function makeCard(height, order)
	local card = Instance.new("Frame", SettingsPage)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, height)
	card.LayoutOrder = order
	corner(card, 10)
	stroke(card, C.STROKE, 1, 0.45)
	return card
end

local function makeSectionTitle(text, order)
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", SettingsPage)
	l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
	l.LayoutOrder = order
	l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	l.Text = text
	l.TextColor3 = C.ACCENT2
	l.TextSize = 12
	l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	return l
end

local function makeToggleRow(text, order, default, callback)
	local card = makeCard(44, order)
	label(card, {
		Text = text, Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Size = 12
	})
	local state = default
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
	btn.BackgroundColor3 = state and C.ACCENT or C.OFF
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, -11)
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 22)
	btn.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
	btn.TextColor3 = state and ACCENT_TEXT or C.TEXT_DIM
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.TextSize = 10
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(btn, 6)
	stroke(btn, state and C.ACCENT or C.STROKE, 1, 0.3)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		state = not state
		TweenService:Create(btn, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = state and C.ACCENT or C.OFF }):Play()
		btn.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
		btn.TextColor3 = state and ACCENT_TEXT or C.TEXT_DIM
		local s = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
		if s then s.Color = state and C.ACCENT or C.STROKE end
		if callback then callback(state) end
	end)
	return btn
end

local function makeSliderRow(text, order, minV, maxV, step, current, formatFn, callback)
	local card = makeCard(60, order)
	label(card, {
		Text = text, Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4), Dim = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 16),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Size = 12
	})
	local valLabel = label(card, {
		Text = formatFn(current), Pos = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 4), Dim = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 16),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 11, Color = C.ACCENT, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	})
	local track = Instance.new("Frame", card)
	track.BackgroundColor3 = C.SLIDER_TRACK
	track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 34)
	track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8)
	corner(track, 4)
	local fill = Instance.new("Frame", track)
	fill.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
	fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((current - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
	corner(fill, 4)
	local knob = Instance.new("Frame", track)
	knob.BackgroundColor3 = C.TEXT
	knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	knob.Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp((current - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1), 0, 0.5, 0)
	knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
	corner(knob, 6)
	stroke(knob, C.ACCENT, 1.2, 0.2)

	local dragging = false
	local function setFromX(x)
		local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
		local val = math.clamp(math.floor((minV + rel * (maxV - minV)) / step + 0.5) * step, minV, maxV)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new((val - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1, 0)
		knob.Position = UDim2.new((val - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 0.5, 0)
		valLabel.Text = formatFn(val)
		if callback then callback(val) end
	end
	track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true; setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if dragging then dragging = false; autoSave() end
		end
	end)
end

makeSectionTitle("FILTERS", 1)
local mvCard = makeCard(100, 2)
label(mvCard, {
	Text = "Minimum Value", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 16),
	Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 12
})
local mvCurrent = label(mvCard, {
	Text = "Selected: 5M", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 24), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 14),
	Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 10, Color = C.ACCENT2
})
local filterOptions = {
	{ label = "5M", value = 5e6 }, { label = "10M", value = 1e7 }, { label = "50M", value = 5e7 },
	{ label = "100M", value = 1e8 }, { label = "500M", value = 5e8 }, { label = "1B+", value = 1e9 },
}
local filterButtons = {}
local function updateFilterUI()
	local sel = "5M"
	for _, o in ipairs(filterOptions) do if o.value == Settings.MinFilterValue then sel = o.label break end end
	mvCurrent.Text = "≥ " .. sel
	for _, item in ipairs(filterButtons) do
		local on = item.value == Settings.MinFilterValue
		item.btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.ACCENT or C.ELEMENT_HOVER
		item.btn.TextColor3 = on and ACCENT_TEXT or C.TEXT
	end
end
for i, opt in ipairs(filterOptions) do
	local col, row = (i - 1) % 3, math.floor((i - 1) / 3)
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", mvCard)
	btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT_HOVER
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12 + col * 118, 0, 44 + row * 26)
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 22)
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.Text = opt.label
	btn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
	btn.TextSize = 10
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(btn, 6)
	stroke(btn, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		Settings.MinFilterValue = opt.value
		updateFilterUI()
		if ApplyFilter then ApplyFilter() end
		autoSave()
	end)
	table.insert(filterButtons, { btn = btn, value = opt.value })
end
updateFilterUI()

makeSectionTitle("KEYBINDS (click to rebind)", 25)
local KEYBIND_DEFS = {
	{ key = "HotkeyMinimize", label = "Toggle UI" },
	{ key = "HotkeyAutoJoin", label = "Auto Join" },
	{ key = "HotkeyForce", label = "Force" },
	{ key = "HotkeyHop", label = "Server Hop" },
}
local waitingRebind = nil
local rebindBtns = {}

local function refreshKeybindLabels()
	for _, item in ipairs(rebindBtns) do
		item.btn.Text = Settings[item.key] or "?"
	end
end

for i, def in ipairs(KEYBIND_DEFS) do
	local card = makeCard(40, 25 + i)
	label(card, {
		Text = def.label, Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Size = 12
	})
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
	btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT_HOVER
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0.5, -11)
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 22)
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.Text = Settings[def.key] or "?"
	btn.TextColor3 = C.ACCENT
	btn.TextSize = 11
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(btn, 6)
	stroke(btn, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		waitingRebind = def.key
		btn.Text = "..."
		btn.TextColor3 = C.WARN
	end)
	table.insert(rebindBtns, { key = def.key, btn = btn })
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
	if not waitingRebind then return end
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
	local name = input.KeyCode.Name
	if name and name ~= "Unknown" then
		Settings[waitingRebind] = name
		waitingRebind = nil
		refreshKeybindLabels()
		autoSave()
	end
end)

-- ========================
-- LIST PAGES (BL/WL)
-- ========================
local refreshBlacklistUI, refreshWhitelistUI

local function setupListPage(page, title, list, map)
	local back = Instance.new("TextButton", page)
	back.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	back.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 26)
	back.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	back.Text = "<  " .. title
	back.TextColor3 = C.ACCENT
	back.TextSize = 14
	back.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	back.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() playClick(); showPage("Settings") end)

	local input = Instance.new("TextBox", page)
	input.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
	input.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 32)
	input.Size = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 28)
	input.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
	input.Text = ""
	input.PlaceholderText = "Name…"
	input.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
	input.PlaceholderColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM
	input.TextSize = 12
	input.ClearTextOnFocus = true
	corner(input, 8)
	stroke(input, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)

	local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", page)
	addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
	addBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 32)
	addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 28)
	addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	addBtn.Text = "Add"
	addBtn.TextColor3 = ACCENT_TEXT
	addBtn.TextSize = 12
	addBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(addBtn, 8)

	local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", page)
	scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 70)
	scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -74)
	scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.ACCENT
	scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Instance.new("UIListLayout", scroll).Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)

	local function refresh()
		for _, c in pairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end end
		for i, name in ipairs(list) do
			local row = Instance.new("Frame", scroll)
			row.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30)
			row.LayoutOrder = i
			corner(row, 8)
			stroke(row, C.STROKE, 1, 0.45)
			label(row, {
				Text = name, Pos = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0),
				Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Size = 12
			})
			local del = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
			del.BackgroundColor3 = C.BAD
			del.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -9)
			del.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 18)
			del.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			del.Text = "X"
			del.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			del.TextSize = 10
			del.AutoButtonColor = false
			corner(del, 5)
			local ci = i
			del.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				playClick()
				map[list[ci]] = nil
				table.remove(list, ci)
				refresh()
				autoSave()
			end)
		end
	end

	addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		local val = input.Text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		if val ~= "" then
			table.insert(list, val)
			map[val] = true
			input.Text = ""
			refresh()
			autoSave()
		end
	end)
	return refresh
end

refreshBlacklistUI = setupListPage(BlacklistPage, "Blacklist", Blacklist, blacklistMap)
refreshWhitelistUI = setupListPage(WhitelistPage, "Whitelist", Whitelist, whitelistMap)

HistScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 30)
HistScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -34)
HistScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
HistScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.ACCENT
HistScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
HistScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UIListLayout", HistScroll).Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)

refreshHistoryUI = function()
	for _, c in pairs(HistScroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end end
	if #JoinHistory == 0 then
		local empty = Instance.new("Frame", HistScroll)
		empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
		label(empty, {
			Text = "No joins yet", Dim = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 13, Color = C.TEXT_DIM, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		})
		return
	end
	for i, h in ipairs(JoinHistory) do
		local card = Instance.new("Frame", HistScroll)
		card.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
		card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 52)
		card.LayoutOrder = i
		corner(card, 9)
		stroke(card, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)
		label(card, {
			Text = h.name or "?", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 18),
			Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 12, Truncate = true
		})
		local ago = os.time() - (h.time or os.time())
		local agoTxt = ago < 60 and (ago .. "s ago") or (math.floor(ago / 60) .. "m ago")
		label(card, {
			Text = (h.gen or "") .. "  ·  " .. agoTxt,
			Pos = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 26), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 16),
			Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 11, Color = C.TEXT_DIM, Truncate = true
		})
		local rejoin = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
		rejoin.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
		rejoin.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -11)
		rejoin.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 22)
		rejoin.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		rejoin.Text = "JOIN"
		rejoin.TextColor3 = ACCENT_TEXT
		rejoin.TextSize = 10
		rejoin.AutoButtonColor = false
		corner(rejoin, 6)
		rejoin.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			playClick()
			if h.jobId and h.jobId ~= "" then
				JoinServer(h.placeId or game.PlaceId, h.jobId)
			end
		end)
	end
end

clearHistBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	playClick()
	table.clear(JoinHistory)
	saveHistory()
	refreshHistoryUI()
end)

label(UsersPage, {
	Text = "ZENO Users", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
	Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 14, Color = C.ACCENT
})
