	Text = FIREBASE_ENABLED and "Live via Firebase" or "Local only — enable Firebase for multi-account",
	Pos = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 22), Dim = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
	Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 10, Color = C.TEXT_DIM
})

local UserScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", UsersPage)
UserScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
UserScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 42)
UserScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -46)
UserScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
UserScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.ACCENT
UserScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
UserScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
Instance.new("UIListLayout", UserScroll).Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)

local LiveUsers = {} -- uid -> {name, displayName, timestamp, jobId}

RefreshUsers = function()
	for _, c in pairs(UserScroll:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end end
	-- Always show self
	local selfCard = Instance.new("Frame", UserScroll)
	selfCard.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
	selfCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 40)
	selfCard.LayoutOrder = 0
	corner(selfCard, 9)
	stroke(selfCard, C.ACCENT, 1, 0.35)
	local dot = Instance.new("Frame", selfCard)
	dot.BackgroundColor3 = C.GOOD
	dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
	dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -4)
	corner(dot, 4)
	label(selfCard, {
		Text = Player.DisplayName .. "  @" .. Player.Name .. "  (you)",
		Pos = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 12
	})

	local order = 1
	local now = os.time()
	for uid, info in pairs(LiveUsers) do
		if tostring(uid) ~= tostring(Player.UserId) and info.timestamp and now - info.timestamp <= 20 then
			local card = Instance.new("Frame", UserScroll)
			card.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
			card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 40)
			card.LayoutOrder = order
			order += 1
			corner(card, 9)
			stroke(card, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)
			local d = Instance.new("Frame", card)
			d.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
			d.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
			d.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -4)
			corner(d, 4)
			local sameServer = info.jobId and info.jobId == game.JobId
			label(card, {
				Text = (info.displayName or info.name or uid) .. (sameServer and "  ·  IN SERVER" or ""),
				Pos = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 0), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0),
				Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 12,
				Color = sameServer and C.GOOD or C.TEXT
			})
		end
	end
	if order == 1 and not FIREBASE_ENABLED then
		local tip = Instance.new("Frame", UserScroll)
		tip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		tip.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
		tip.LayoutOrder = 99
		label(tip, {
			Text = "Set FIREBASE_URL + FIREBASE_ENABLED = true\nto see other ZENO users live",
			Dim = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 11,
			Color = C.TEXT_DIM, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		})
	end
end

-- ESP
local espObjects = {}
local localESP = {}
local rainbowHue = 0

local function destroyESP(item)
	if not item then return end
	pcall(function() if item.Highlight then item.Highlight:Destroy() end end)
	pcall(function() if item.Billboard then item.Billboard:Destroy() end end)
end

local function removeUserESP(userId)
	local item = espObjects[tostring(userId)]
	if item then destroyESP(item); espObjects[tostring(userId)] = nil end
end

local function buildESP(plr, text)
	if not Settings.ESPEnabled or not plr or not plr.Character then return end
	local old = espObjects[tostring(plr.UserId)]
	if old then destroyESP(old) end
	local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
	hl.Name = "ZENO_ESP"
	hl.Adornee = plr.Character
	hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
	hl.FillTransparency = 1
	hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
	hl.OutlineColor = C.ACCENT
	hl.Parent = plr.Character
	local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or plr.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
	local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	bb.Name = "ZENO_TAG"
	bb.Adornee = head
	bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 24)
	bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
	bb.Parent = plr.Character
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	lbl.Text = text or "ZENO USER"
	lbl.TextColor3 = C.ACCENT
	lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
	lbl.TextSize = 12
	espObjects[tostring(plr.UserId)] = { Highlight = hl, Billboard = bb, Label = lbl }
end

local function buildLocalESP()
	if not Settings.ESPEnabled or not Player.Character then return end
	destroyESP(localESP)
	local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
	hl.Name = "ZENO_LOCAL_ESP"
	hl.Adornee = Player.Character
	hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
	hl.FillTransparency = 1
	hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
	hl.OutlineColor = C.ACCENT
	hl.Parent = Player.Character
	local head = Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or Player.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
	local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	bb.Name = "ZENO_LOCAL_TAG"
	bb.Adornee = head
	bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 24)
	bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
	bb.Parent = Player.Character
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	lbl.Text = "ZENO USER"
	lbl.TextColor3 = C.ACCENT
	lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
	lbl.TextSize = 12
	localESP = { Highlight = hl, Billboard = bb, Label = lbl }
end

Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	task.wait(1)
	buildLocalESP()
end)
task.defer(buildLocalESP)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
	removeUserESP(plr.UserId)
end)

local PresenceRoot = "presence/" .. tostring(game.JobId)

local function updatePresence()
	if not FIREBASE_ENABLED then return end
	pcall(function()
		saveData(PresenceRoot .. "/" .. tostring(Player.UserId), {
			userId = Player.UserId,
			name = Player.Name,
			displayName = Player.DisplayName,
			jobId = game.JobId,
			timestamp = os.time(),
		})
	end)
end

local function refreshPresence()
	if not FIREBASE_ENABLED then return end
	local data = getData(PresenceRoot)
	local now = os.time()
	local seen = {}
	LiveUsers = {}
	if type(data) == "table" then
		for uid, info in pairs(data) do
			if type(info) == "table" and info.timestamp and now - tonumber(info.timestamp) <= 20 then
				LiveUsers[tostring(uid)] = info
				seen[tostring(uid)] = true
				if tostring(uid) ~= tostring(Player.UserId) and Settings.ESPEnabled then
					local plr = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(uid) or -1)
					if plr and not espObjects[tostring(uid)] then
						buildESP(plr, "ZENO USER")
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for uid in pairs(espObjects) do
		if not seen[tostring(uid)] then removeUserESP(uid) end
	end
end

-- Also scan local players for ESP if same script (name tag heuristic) — only Firebase users get ESP when enabled
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(5) do
		updatePresence()
		refreshPresence()
		if UsersPage.Visible then RefreshUsers() end
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.08) do
		if not Settings.ESPEnabled then continue end
		rainbowHue = (rainbowHue + 0.008) % 1
		local col = Color3.fromHSV(rainbowHue, 0.7, 1)
		if localESP.Highlight then localESP.Highlight.OutlineColor = col end
		if localESP.Label then localESP.Label.TextColor3 = col end
		for _, item in pairs(espObjects) do
			if item.Highlight then item.Highlight.OutlineColor = col end
			if item.Label then item.Label.TextColor3 = col end
		end
	end
end)

-- ========================
-- TELEPORT / SCAN
-- ========================
JoinServer = function(placeId, jobId)
	pcall(function()
		TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(tonumber(placeId), jobId, Player)
	end)
end

ForceServer = function(placeId, jobId)
	task.spawn(function()
		local deadline = tick() + Settings.ForceDuration
		while tick() < deadline do
			pcall(function()
				TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(tonumber(placeId), jobId, Player)
			end)
			task.wait(0.45)
		end
	end)
end

serverHop = function()
	pcall(function()
		local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
		local response = httpRequest("GET", url, nil)
		if response then
			local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(response) end)
			if ok and decoded and decoded.data then
				for _, s in ipairs(decoded.data) do
					if type(s) == "table" and s.playing and s.maxPlayers and s.id ~= game.JobId then
						local playerOk = true
						if Settings.MaxPlayersFilter > 0 then
							playerOk = s.playing <= Settings.MaxPlayersFilter
						else
							playerOk = s.playing < s.maxPlayers
						end
						if playerOk then
							TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, s.id, Player)
							return
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, Player)
	end)
end

ApplyFilter = function()
	for _, entry in ipairs(LogEntries) do
		local show = (entry.NumericValue >= Settings.MinFilterValue)
		if show and #Blacklist > 0 then
			for _, bl in ipairs(Blacklist) do
				if string.find(string.lower(entry.Name), string.lower(bl)) then show = false break end
			end
		end
		if show and #Whitelist > 0 then
			local found = false
			for _, wl in ipairs(Whitelist) do
				if string.find(string.lower(entry.Name), string.lower(wl)) then found = true break end
			end
			show = found
		end
		entry.UI.Visible = show
	end
end

local function getValue(text)
	if not text then return 0 end
	local raw = tostring(text):gsub(",", "")
	local num = tonumber(raw:match("[%d%.]+")) or 0
	local lower = string.lower(raw)
	if lower:find("t") then return num * 1e12
	elseif lower:find("b") then return num * 1e9
	elseif lower:find("m") then return num * 1e6
	elseif lower:find("k") then return num * 1e3
	else return num end
end

local activeToast, toastSerial = nil, 0
local function showToast(name, money)
	toastSerial += 1
	local serial = toastSerial
	if activeToast and activeToast.Parent then activeToast:Destroy() end
	local toast = Instance.new("Frame", ScreenGui)
	activeToast = toast
	toast.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
	toast.Position = UDim2.new(1, 24, 0, 16)
	toast.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 64)
	toast.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT
	toast.ZIndex = 120
	corner(toast, 12)
	stroke(toast, C.ACCENT, 1.2, 0.25)
	local bar = Instance.new("Frame", toast)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -14)
	bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 7)
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	corner(bar, 2)
	label(toast, {
		Text = "NEW LOG", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 8), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, Size = 12, Color = C.ACCENT
	})
	label(toast, {
		Text = tostring(name) .. "  ·  " .. tostring(money),
		Pos = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 30), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 22),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Size = 11, Truncate = true
	})
	playNotifySound()
	TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 16)
	}):Play()
	task.delay(3, function()
		if serial ~= toastSerial or not toast.Parent then return end
		local tw = TweenService:Create(toast, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
			Position = UDim2.new(1, 24, 0, 16)
		})
		tw:Play(); tw.Completed:Wait()
		if toast.Parent then toast:Destroy() end
	end)
end

local function CreateLogNotification(dbKey, brainrotName, moneyStr, numVal, jobId, placeId, isBest)
	if RenderedIDs[dbKey] then return end
	RenderedIDs[dbKey] = true
	lastHighValueTime = os.time()

	local createdAt = os.time()
	local card = Instance.new("Frame", LogScroll)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = Settings.HighlightAJ and C.CARD_ON or C.ELEMENT
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 56)
	corner(card, 10)
	stroke(card, C.STROKE, 1, 0.4)

	local v = tonumber(numVal) or 0
	local stripColor, moneyColor = C.ACCENT, C.GOOD
	if v >= 5e7 then stripColor = C.BAD; moneyColor = Color3.fromRGB(240, 140, 150)
	elseif v >= 2e7 then stripColor = C.WARN; moneyColor = Color3.fromRGB(240, 210, 120)
	elseif v >= 1e7 then stripColor = C.GOOD; moneyColor = C.GOOD end

	local strip = Instance.new("Frame", card)
	strip.BackgroundColor3 = stripColor
	strip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.7, 0)
	strip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.15, 0)
	strip.BorderSizePixel = 0
	corner(strip, 2)

	label(card, {
		Text = brainrotName or "Unknown",
		Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6), Dim = UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 18),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Size = 12, Truncate = true
	})
	local timeLbl = label(card, {
		Text = "0s ago", Pos = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 28), Dim = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, 16),
		Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Size = 10, Color = C.TEXT_DIM
	})
	label(card, {
		Text = moneyStr or "", Pos = UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 6), Dim = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 18),
		Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, Size = 11, Color = moneyColor, Align = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	})

	local function doJoin()
		playClick()
		addToHistory(brainrotName, moneyStr, jobId, placeId or game.PlaceId)
		JoinServer(placeId or game.PlaceId, jobId)
	end

	local jBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
	jBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
	jBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0.5, -11)
	jBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 22)
	jBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	jBtn.Text = "JOIN"
	jBtn.TextColor3 = ACCENT_TEXT
	jBtn.TextSize = 10
	jBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(jBtn, 6)
	jBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(doJoin)

	local fBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
	fBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT_HOVER
	fBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0.5, -11)
	fBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 22)
	fBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	fBtn.Text = "FORCE"
	fBtn.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM
	fBtn.TextSize = 9
	fBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(fBtn, 6)
	fBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		addToHistory(brainrotName, moneyStr, jobId, placeId or game.PlaceId)
		ForceServer(placeId or game.PlaceId, jobId)
	end)

	local copyBtn2 = Instance.new("TextButton", card)
	copyBtn2.BackgroundColor3 = C.ELEMENT_HOVER
	copyBtn2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -11)
	copyBtn2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	copyBtn2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	copyBtn2.Text = "⧉"
	copyBtn2.TextColor3 = C.TEXT_DIM
	copyBtn2.TextSize = 11
	copyBtn2.AutoButtonColor = false
	corner(copyBtn2, 6)
	copyBtn2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		playClick()
		pcall(function() setclipboard(tostring(jobId)) end)
		copyBtn2.Text = "✓"
		task.delay(0.8, function() if copyBtn2.Parent then copyBtn2.Text = "⧉" end end)
	end)

	table.insert(LogEntries, {
		NumericValue = numVal, UI = card, PlaceId = placeId or game.PlaceId,
		JobId = jobId, Name = brainrotName, CreatedAt = createdAt, TimeLabel = timeLbl, IsBest = isBest
	})
	showToast(brainrotName, moneyStr)
	ApplyFilter()
	minStatus.Text = tostring(brainrotName):sub(1, 16)

	-- Auto join: if JoinOnlyBest, only auto-join when this is the best
	if autoJoinQuick and jobId and jobId ~= game.JobId then
		if not Settings.JoinOnlyBest or isBest then
			task.defer(function()
				addToHistory(brainrotName, moneyStr, jobId, placeId or game.PlaceId)
				JoinServer(placeId or game.PlaceId, jobId)
			end)
		end
	end
end

local function getLatestLog()
	return #LogEntries > 0 and LogEntries[#LogEntries] or nil
end

AutoJoinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	playClick()
	autoJoinQuick = not autoJoinQuick
	AutoJoinBtn.Text = autoJoinQuick and "AUTO ON" or "AUTO JOIN"
	AutoJoinBtn.BackgroundColor3 = autoJoinQuick and C.ACCENT or C.ELEMENT
	AutoJoinBtn.TextColor3 = autoJoinQuick and ACCENT_TEXT or C.ACCENT
	local latest = getLatestLog()
	if autoJoinQuick and latest and latest.JobId then
		if not Settings.JoinOnlyBest or latest.IsBest ~= false then
			addToHistory(latest.Name, nil, latest.JobId, latest.PlaceId)
			JoinServer(latest.PlaceId, latest.JobId)
		end
	end
end)

ForceQuickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	playClick()
	local latest = getLatestLog()
	if latest and latest.JobId then
		ForceQuickBtn.Text = "FORCING…"
		addToHistory(latest.Name, nil, latest.JobId, latest.PlaceId)
		ForceServer(latest.PlaceId, latest.JobId)
		task.delay(Settings.ForceDuration, function()
			if ForceQuickBtn.Parent then ForceQuickBtn.Text = "FORCE " .. Settings.ForceDuration .. "S" end
		end)
	else
		ForceQuickBtn.Text = "NO LOG"
		task.delay(1.2, function()
			if ForceQuickBtn.Parent then ForceQuickBtn.Text = "FORCE " .. Settings.ForceDuration .. "S" end
		end)
	end
end)

ClearLogsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	playClick()
	for _, e in ipairs(LogEntries) do if e.UI and e.UI.Parent then e.UI:Destroy() end end
	table.clear(LogEntries)
	table.clear(RenderedIDs)
	clearLogsBefore = os.time()
	ClearLogsBtn.Text = "CLEARED"
	task.delay(1.2, function() if ClearLogsBtn.Parent then ClearLogsBtn.Text = "CLEAR" end end)
end)

-- Scanner
local sentBrainrots = {}
local sentCleanup = 0

local function getUniqueKey(name, gen)
	return (name or "?") .. "|" .. (gen or "")
end

local function findBrainrotImage(obj)
	if not obj then return "" end
	local best = ""
	for _, d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then
			local img = d.Image
			if type(img) == "string" and img ~= "" and (img:find("rbxassetid://") or img:find("http")) then
				if d.AbsoluteSize.X >= 20 or best == "" then best = img end
			end
		end
	end
	return best
end

local function scanAndSend()
	local found = {}
	local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
	if debris then
		for _, obj in ipairs(debris:GetChildren()) do
			local gen = obj:FindFirstChild("Generation", true) or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")
			if gen and gen.Text and gen.Text:find("%$") then
				local value = getValue(gen.Text)
				if value >= Settings.MinFilterValue then
					local name = "Unknown"
					local disp = obj:FindFirstChild("DisplayName", true) or obj:FindFirstChild("Displayname", true)
					if disp and disp.Text then name = disp.Text end
					local key = getUniqueKey(name, gen.Text)
					if not sentBrainrots[key] then
						table.insert(found, {
							name = name, gen = gen.Text, key = key, numValue = value,
							image = findBrainrotImage(obj)
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
	if plots then
		for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
			local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
			if pods then
				for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
					local gen = pod:FindFirstChild("Generation", true)
					if gen and gen:IsA("TextLabel") and gen.Text:find("%$") then
						local value = getValue(gen.Text)
						if value >= Settings.MinFilterValue then
							local name = "Podium Animal"
							local disp = pod:FindFirstChild("DisplayName", true) or pod:FindFirstChild("Displayname", true)
							if disp and disp.Text then name = disp.Text end
							local key = getUniqueKey(name, gen.Text)
							if not sentBrainrots[key] then
								table.insert(found, {
									name = name, gen = gen.Text, key = key, numValue = value,
									image = findBrainrotImage(pod)
								})
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #found == 0 then return end
	table.sort(found, function(a, b) return (a.numValue or 0) > (b.numValue or 0) end)
	for _, item in ipairs(found) do sentBrainrots[item.key] = true end

	sentCleanup += 1
	if sentCleanup >= 20 then
		sentCleanup = 0
		local n = 0
		for _ in pairs(sentBrainrots) do n += 1 end
		if n > 150 then table.clear(sentBrainrots) end
	end

	local best = found[1]
	-- Prefer wiki image (Discord-compatible HTTPS)
	best.image = resolveBrainrotImage(best.name, best.image)
	local players = #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers

	-- Player filter: if current server too full, still log but prefer hop targets with filter
	local toReport = Settings.JoinOnlyBest and { best } or found

	local localKey = "local_" .. tick() .. "_" .. math.random(1000, 9999)
	local displayName = best.name
	if #found > 1 then displayName = displayName .. "  +" .. (#found - 1) .. " other" end
	CreateLogNotification(localKey, displayName, best.gen, best.numValue, game.JobId, game.PlaceId, true)

	if FIREBASE_ENABLED then
		pcall(function()
			local others = {}
			for i = 2, #found do
				table.insert(others, { name = found[i].name, value = found[i].gen, numValue = found[i].numValue })
			end
			saveData("logs/log_" .. math.floor(tick()) .. "_" .. math.random(1000, 9999), {
				name = best.name, value = best.gen, numValue = best.numValue,
				jobId = game.JobId, placeId = game.PlaceId, players = players,
				image = best.image or "",
				others = others, timestamp = os.time()
			})
		end)
	end

	if Settings.WebhookEnabled then
		local list = {}
		for _, item in ipairs(found) do
			table.insert(list, {
				name = item.name, gen = item.gen, numValue = item.numValue, image = item.image
			})
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			sendWebhook(list, players, game.JobId, best.image)
		end)
	end
end

-- Drag / resize
local dragging, dragStart, startPos, dragInput = false, nil, nil, nil
Frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = Frame.Position
		input.Changed:Connect(function()
			if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
		end)
	end
end)
Frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		dragInput = input
	end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if input == dragInput and dragging and dragStart and startPos then
		local d = input.Position - dragStart
		TweenService:Create(Frame, dragTweenInfo, {
			Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		}):Play()
	end
end)

		resizing = true; resizeStart = input.Position; resizeSize = Frame.AbsoluteSize

end)

	end
end)

			end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) do
		local now = os.time()
		for _, e in ipairs(LogEntries) do
			if e.TimeLabel and e.TimeLabel.Parent and e.CreatedAt then
				local d = math.max(0, now - e.CreatedAt)
				e.TimeLabel.Text = d < 60 and (d .. "s ago") or (math.floor(d / 60) .. "m ago")
			end
		end
		if Settings.AutoClearMinutes > 0 then
			local cutoff = now - Settings.AutoClearMinutes * 60
			local kept = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(LogEntries) do
				if e.CreatedAt and e.CreatedAt >= cutoff then
					table.insert(kept, e)
				else
					if e.UI and e.UI.Parent then e.UI:Destroy() end
				end
			end
			LogEntries = kept
		end
	end
end)

-- ===============================
-- PATCH AUTO INVIS MANUEL (XEN PORT) - AJOUTE PAR META AI
-- Ce patch rend le bouton Auto Invis fonctionnel en dehors du steal
-- ===============================
pcall(function()
    task.wait(1)
    -- On s'assure que toggle existe
    if not _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
        warn("[PATCH] _G.toggleInvisibleSteal introuvable")
        return
    end

    -- Nouvelle variable globale pour l'auto invis manuel
    _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled = _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled or false

    -- Fonction Auto Invis forcé (copiée du Xen - clone HRP + anim)
    _G.ZenoForceAutoInvis = function(state)
        _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled = state
        if state then
            if not _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
                pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
            end
        else
            if _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
                pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Si le panel INVIS existe, on ajoute un vrai bouton Auto Invis manuel
    local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local pg = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        local gui = pg:FindFirstChild("ZENO_INVIS_STEAL")
        if gui then
            -- On cherche le body pour ajouter le bouton
            for _,v in pairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if v.Name == "Body" or v.Name == "Content" then
                    -- On évite de dupliquer
                    if not v:FindFirstChild("AutoInvisManuelBtn") then
                        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                        btn.Name = "AutoInvisManuelBtn"
                        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 36)
                        btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 180)
                        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(99, 102, 241)
                        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
                        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        btn.TextSize = 14
                        btn.Text = "AUTO INVIS: OFF"
                        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)
                        btn.Parent = v
                        
                        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                            _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled = not _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled
                            _G.ZenoForceAutoInvis(_G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled)
                            btn.Text = _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled and "AUTO INVIS: ON" or "AUTO INVIS: OFF"
                            btn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.AutoInvisManuelEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(40, 200, 80) or Color3.fromRGB(99, 102, 241)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- FIX: le bouton auto invis du hub principal (s'il existe) -> on le rebind
    -- On hook le Config.invisOnSteal pour qu'il fonctionne aussi en manuel
    if type(_G.ZenoSetInvis) == "function" then
        local old = _G.ZenoSetInvis
        _G.ZenoSetInvis = function(on)
            pcall(old, on)
            -- Si on active le toggle principal, on active aussi l'invis
            if on then
                _G.ZenoForceAutoInvis(true)
            end
        end
    end

    print("[PATCH AUTO INVIS] Installe - Bouton Auto Invis maintenant fonctionnel (Xen port)")
end)







-- ========= ZENO ULTRA V17 - PACK COMPLET MEILLEUR DE 4 HUBS =========
task.spawn(function()
    local Players = cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService("Players")) or game:GetService("Players")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    repeat task.wait(0.5) until _G.toggleInvisibleSteal
    print("[ULTRA] Chargement pack complet")

    -- ===== 1. BEST AUTO INVIS (RYFT + XEN + SXE) =====
    local autoEnabled = false
    local lastSteal = false
    local function isStealingNow()
        if lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        local char = lp.Character
        if char and char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        if lp:GetAttribute("StealingIndex") ~= nil then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function tryAuto()
        local stealing = isStealingNow()
        if stealing == lastSteal then return end
        lastSteal = stealing
        if stealing then
            if not _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
                autoEnabled = true
                pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
            end
        else
            if autoEnabled and _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
                task.wait(0.2)
                pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
            end
            autoEnabled = false
        end
    end
    pcall(function() lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(tryAuto) end)
    pcall(function() lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(tryAuto) end)
    local function hookChar(c)
        if not c then return end
        pcall(function() c:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(tryAuto) end)
    end
    if lp.Character then hookChar(lp.Character) end
    lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) task.wait(0.5); hookChar(c); lastSteal=false end)
    task.spawn(function() while true do pcall(tryAuto); task.wait(0.2) end end)
    _G.__RyftAutoInvis = true

    -- ===== 2. FLOAT PLATFORM (file822) - Reste en l'air pendant vol =====
    local floatActive = false
    local floatPart = nil
    local floatConn = nil
    local function createFloat()
        if floatPart then floatPart:Destroy() end
        local char = lp.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        floatPart = Instance.new("Part")
        floatPart.Size = Vector3.new(7,1,7)
        floatPart.Anchored = true
        floatPart.CanCollide = true
        floatPart.Transparency = 0.5
        floatPart.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100,100,255)
        floatPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        floatPart.Name = "ZENO_FLOAT"
        floatPart.Parent = workspace
        floatConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not floatActive or not lp.Character then return end
            local hrp2 = lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp2 and floatPart then
                floatPart.Position = hrp2.Position - Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function removeFloat()
        if floatConn then floatConn:Disconnect(); floatConn=nil end
        if floatPart then floatPart:Destroy(); floatPart=nil end
    end
    _G.ZenoSetFloat = function(on)
        floatActive = on
        if on then createFloat() else removeFloat() end
    end
    lp:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
            if floatActive then createFloat() end
        else
            task.wait(0.5)
            if not lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") then removeFloat() end
        end
    end)

    -- ===== 3. ANTI RAGDOLL / BALLOON AUTO RESET (file822) =====
    local antiEnabled = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.2)
            if not antiEnabled then continue end
            local char = lp.Character
            if not char then continue end
            -- Balloon / Morph detect
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                -- Si tu es balloon ou ragdoll, reset
                if char:GetAttribute("Balloon") or char:GetAttribute("Morph") or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
                    if lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                        print("[ANTI] Balloon detecte -> reset evite")
                        pcall(function() lp.Character:BreakJoints() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ===== 4. AUTO KICK ON STEAL (file539 RYFT) =====
    local autoKick = false
    _G.__RyftSetAutoKick = function(on) autoKick = on end
    local function checkKick()
        if not autoKick then return end
        local pg = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        for _, gui in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if gui:IsA("TextLabel") and string.find(string.lower(gui.Text), "you stole") then
                print("[AUTO KICK] Tu as vole -> KICK")
                pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
                pcall(function() lp:Kick("") end)
                return
            end
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if autoKick then pcall(checkKick) end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
    lp.PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if autoKick then task.wait(0.1); pcall(checkKick) end
    end)

    print("[ULTRA] Pack complet pret - Auto Invis + Float + Anti + Auto Kick")
end)
-- ========= FIN ULTRA V17 =========


-- ========= PATCH AUTO STEAL / AUTO GRAB UNIFIE - MEILLEUR DE SXE + ZENO =========
task.spawn(function()
    repeat task.wait(1) until _G.ZenoSetAutoSteal
    print("[AUTO STEAL/GRAB] Patch unifie charge")
    -- Override l'ancien autoStealLoop par un meilleur qui utilise la priority list
    local function getPriorityScore(name)
        if not name then return 9999 end
        local priorityList = Config.priorityList or Config.priority or {}
        for i, v in ipairs(priorityList) do
            if string.find(string.lower(name), string.lower(v)) then
                return i
            end
        end
        return 9999
    end

    -- Nouveau auto grab qui marche avec ProximityPrompt
    local grabOn = Config.autoGrab == true
    _G.ZenoSetAutoGrab = function(on)
        Config.autoGrab = on and true or false
        grabOn = Config.autoGrab
        pcall(saveConfig)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.35)
            if not grabOn then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then continue end
            local radius = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.autoGrabRadius) or 35, 5, 120)
            for _, inst in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if inst:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and inst.Enabled then
                    local part = inst.Parent
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if (part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= radius then
                            local n = string.lower(inst.Name.. " " .. part.Name .. " " .. (inst.Parent.Parent and inst.Parent.Parent.Name or ""))
                            if n:find("steal") or n:find("grab") or n:find("collect") or n:find("pick") or n:find("prompt") then
                                pcall(function()
                                    if fireproximityprompt then
                                        fireproximityprompt(inst)
                                    else
                                        inst:InputHoldBegin()
                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                        inst:InputHoldEnd()
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Auto steal ameliore avec priority
    local function getBestPet()
        local best = nil
        local bestScore = math.huge
        local pets = allPetsCache or {}
        for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
            if pet and pet.owner ~= player.Name then
                local score = getPriorityScore(pet.name or pet.Name)
                local gen = tonumber(pet.genValue) or 0
                -- Priorite d'abord, puis gen value
                local finalScore = score * 1000000 - gen
                if finalScore < bestScore then
                    bestScore = finalScore
                    best = pet
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    -- Si Config.targetMode = "priority", on override getTargetPetData
    local oldGetTarget = getTargetPetData
    _G.ZenoGetBestPet = getBestPet
    getTargetPetData = function()
        if Config.targetMode == "priority" or Config.stealMode == "priority" then
            return getBestPet()
        else
            return oldGetTarget()
        end
    end

    print("[AUTO STEAL/GRAB] Unifie pret")
end)
-- ========= FIN PATCH =========
